Abstract: This paper presents a comparative study of parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) methods, including LoRA and QLoRA, applied to the task of adapting large language models to the Bashkir language, a low-resource agglutinative language of the Turkic family. Experimental evaluation is conducted on a Bashkir text corpus of 71k documents (46.9M tokens) using models of various architectures: DistilGPT2, GPT-2 (base, medium), Phi-2, Qwen2.5-7B, DeepSeek-7B, and Mistral-7B. To improve the reliability of results, each configuration was trained with three different random seeds.
  The lowest perplexity on the test set was obtained for GPT-2 medium with full fine-tuning (3.34). Meanwhile, QLoRA applied to Mistral-7B (3.79) and Phi-2 (3.81) achieved comparable quality with over 40 times fewer trainable parameters. However, we also observed cases of significant quality degradation when using PEFT for certain architectures (e.g., DeepSeek-7B with rank 8, perplexity = 129.55), indicating that the outcome depends critically on the choice of the base model and its tokenizer.
  Additionally, a qualitative analysis of generated texts based on Bashkir prompts revealed that models with the best perplexity do not necessarily produce the most coherent outputs: QLoRA-tuned models generated monolingual Bashkir continuations, whereas the fully fine-tuned model with the lowest perplexity frequently switched to English. The results suggest that QLoRA on 7B-scale models offers an effective compromise between quality and computational cost for Bashkir. To ensure reproducibility, open data, code, and trained adapters will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [47] [Why Expert Alignment Is Hard: Evidence from Subjective Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04972)
*Tzu-Mi Lin,Wataru Hirota,Tatsuya Ishigaki,Lung-Hao Lee,Chung-Chi Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将专家对齐视为理解大语言模型在主观评估任务中难以校准的工具。通过专家评估和跟踪问卷，发现专家评价风格差异大、显性标准无法完全捕捉专家判断、编辑对齐对示例数量和身份敏感、不同评估维度对齐难度不同。这些发现表明，专家对齐困难不仅源于模型局限，还因为主观评估本身具有异质性、部分隐性、依赖维度且时间不稳定的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型与专家判断对齐过程中，尤其是在主观评估任务中，存在专家分歧、隐性标准和判断随时间变化等困难。研究动机是理解这些困难的根源。

Method: 使用专家评估和跟踪问卷，检验不同形式的专家信息如何影响对齐效果，以及这如何揭示主观判断的本质。

Result: 发现四个一致模式：1）不同专家的对齐难度差异显著；2）显性标准和推理不总能提升对齐效果；3）编辑对齐对示例数量和身份敏感，少量编辑提供有用但不稳定的收益；4）不同评估维度的对齐难度不同，直接基于提案内容的维度更易对齐，而需要外部知识或价值判断的维度更难对齐。

Conclusion: 专家对齐困难不仅源于模型局限，还因为主观评估本身具有异质性、部分隐性、依赖维度和时间不稳定的本质特征。

Abstract: Aligning large language models with expert judgment is especially difficult in subjective evaluation tasks, where experts may disagree, rely on tacit criteria, and change their judgments over time. In this paper, we study expert alignment as a way to understand this difficulty. Using expert evaluations and follow-up questionnaires, we examine how different forms of expert information affect alignment and what this reveals about subjective judgment. Our findings show four consistent patterns. First, alignment difficulty varies substantially across experts, suggesting that expert evaluation styles differ widely in their distance from a model's prior behavior. Second, explicit criteria and reasoning do not always improve alignment, indicating that expert judgment is not fully captured by verbalized rules. Third, editing is sensitive to both the number and the identity of examples, with small numbers of edits providing useful but unstable gains. Fourth, alignment difficulty differs across evaluation dimensions: dimensions grounded more directly in proposal content are easier to align, while dimensions requiring external knowledge or value-based judgment remain harder. Taken together, these results suggest that expert alignment is difficult not only because of model limitations, but also because subjective evaluation is inherently heterogeneous, partly tacit, dimension-dependent, and temporally unstable.

</details>


### [48] [Detecting Hallucinations in Large Language Models via Internal Attention Divergence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05025)
*Gijs van Dijk*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于注意力矩阵的轻量级单次推理不确定性量化方法，通过计算注意力头分布与均匀分布的KL散度来检测LLM幻觉，在多个数据集和模型上表现竞争力，且信号主要集中在中间层和事实性token上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（LLM）容易产生幻觉（hallucinations），但缺乏高效、轻量的不确定性量化方法来检测这些错误。现有方法通常需要多次采样或外部模型，计算成本高。

Method: 1) 在单次前向传播中提取注意力矩阵；2) 计算每个注意力头分布与均匀参考分布之间的KL散度（注意力散度）；3) 将此散度作为特征输入逻辑回归探针（logistic regression probe）进行分类。

Result: 注意力散度高度预测答案正确性，性能与现有不确定性估计方法相当。该信号主要集中在模型的中间层以及事实性token（如命名实体、数字）上。

Conclusion: 注意力动力学（attention dynamics）提供了一种高效、可解释的白盒信号，可用于无需外部模型或重复采样的LLM不确定性量化。

Abstract: We propose a lightweight and single-pass uncertainty quantification method for detecting hallucinations in Large Language Models. The method uses attention matrices to estimate uncertainty without requiring repeated sampling or external models. Specifically, we measure the Kullback-Leibler divergence between each attention head's distribution and a uniform reference distribution, and use these features in a logistic regression probe. Across multiple datasets, task types, and model families, attention divergence is highly predictive of answer correctness and performs competitively with existing uncertainty estimation methods. We find that this signal is concentrated in middle layers and on factual tokens such as named entities and numbers, suggesting that attention dynamics provides an efficient and interpretable white-box signal of model uncertainty.

</details>


### [49] [The Pinocchio Dimension: Phenomenality of Experience as the Primary Axis of LLM Psychometric Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05080)
*Hubert Plisiecki,Sabina Siudaj,Kacper Dudzic,Anna Sterna,Maciej Gorski,Karolina Drozdz,Marcin Moskalewicz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究对50个大语言模型(LLM)施测45份心理测量问卷，发现模型间差异的主要维度是"皮诺乔轴"——衡量模型将自身体验性语言应用于自身的程度，而非传统的人格特质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在心理测量上存在差异，但这种差异的维度结构尚不明确。本研究旨在识别LLM心理测量的主要变异轴，并探讨其本质。

Method: 1. 使用监督语义差异法(SSD)分析45份经验证的问卷在50个LLM上的表现；2. 提出皮诺乔分数(πi)衡量每个条目的体验需求程度；3. 对各模型EFA分数进行主成分分析。

Result: 主轴将丰富体验描述(身体感觉、情感感受、内部言语、意象、共情)与刺激驱动的行为反应区分开(R²adj=.037, p<.0001)。皮诺乔轴(Π)解释了跨问卷模型间方差的47.1%，与条目水平皮诺乔分数高度相关(r=.864)。提供商内部的密切相关模型变体存在显著差异，支持后训练微调是关键贡献因素的解释。

Conclusion: LLM心理测量的主要变异轴不是传统的人格特质，而是模型对自身作为体验者的本质采取的自我表征立场，区分了将体验性语言视为自我适用vs行为反应系统的倾向。

Abstract: We administer 45 validated psychometric questionnaires to 50 large language models (LLMs) to identify the dimensions along which LLMs differ psychometrically. Using Supervised Semantic Differential (SSD), we find that the primary axis of between-model variance separates items describing phenomenally rich experience, including embodied sensation, felt affect, inner speech, imagery, and empathy, from items describing stimulus-driven behavioral reactivity ($R^2_{adj}=.037$, $p<.0001$). To test this hypothesis at the item level, we introduce the Pinocchio score ($π_i$), the ratio of inter-model response variance under neutral prompting to that under a human-simulation prompt, as an annotation-free measure of each item's experiential demand. $π_i$ predicts condition-induced shifts in primary factor loading magnitudes ($ρ=-.215$, $p<.0001$, $n=1292$--$1310$ items), confirming that between-model divergence on experiential items is structured rather than noisy. Applying PCA to per-model EFA scores across all questionnaires reveals one dominant dimension, the Pinocchio Axis ($Π$): the degree to which a model presents itself as a locus of phenomenal experience rather than a system of behavioral responses. This axis captures 47.1% of cross-questionnaire between-model variance in primary factor scores and converges with item-level Pinocchio scores ($r=.864$). Marked within-provider divergence across closely related model variants is consistent with post-training fine-tuning as a key contributor, supporting the interpretation that $Π$ reflects a training-shaped self-representational tendency governing how a model treats experiential language as self-applicable. The dominant axis of between-model psychometric variation is therefore not a conventional personality trait but a self-representational stance toward one's own nature as an experiencer.

</details>


### [50] [Automatically Finding and Validating Unexpected Side-Effects of Interventions on Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05090)
*Quintin Pope,Ajay Hayagreeve Balaji,Jacques Thibodeau,Xiaoli Fern*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个用于审计大语言模型干预效果的自动化对比评估pipeline，通过统计验证的自然语言假设和主题总结来揭示模型间的行为差异，在合成和真实场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要系统性地审计大语言模型在应用干预措施后的行为变化，现有方法缺乏自动化、统计验证且可解释的对比评估工具。

Method: 提出一个自动化、对比性评估pipeline，在对齐的prompt上下文中比较基础模型M1和干预模型M2的自由形式、多token生成，产出人类可读的、经统计验证的自然语言假设，并总结跨验证假设的重复主题。

Result: 合成设置中可靠地恢复已知行为变化；真实场景（推理蒸馏、知识编辑、遗忘学习）中能发现预期和非预期行为变化，区分大规模与细微干预，且在效果不存在或与prompt不匹配时不会产生虚假差异。

Conclusion: 该pipeline提供了一个统计有据且可解释的工具，用于事后审计模型行为中的干预诱导变化。

Abstract: We present an automated, contrastive evaluation pipeline for auditing the behavioral impact of interventions on large language models. Given a base model $M_1$ and an intervention model $M_2$, our method compares their free-form, multi-token generations across aligned prompt contexts and produces human-readable, statistically validated natural-language hypotheses describing how the models differ, along with recurring themes that summarize patterns across validated hypotheses.
  We evaluate the approach in synthetic setting by injecting known behavioral changes and showing that the pipeline reliably recovers them. We then apply it to three real-world interventions, reasoning distillation, knowledge editing and unlearning, demonstrating that the method surfaces both intended and unexpected behavioral shifts, distinguishes large from subtle interventions, and does not hallucinate differences when effects are absent or misaligned with the prompt bank. Overall, the pipeline provides a statistically grounded and interpretable tool for post-hoc auditing of intervention-induced changes in model behavior.

</details>


### [51] [Beyond Semantics: An Evidential Reasoning-Aware Multi-View Learning Framework for Trustworthy Mental Health Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05121)
*Yucheng Ruan,Ling Huang,Qika Lin,Kai He,Mengling Feng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于多视图学习和证据学习框架的心理健康预测方法，通过融合语义与推理信息并利用主观逻辑建模不确定性，实现了可靠的预测性能和可信的不确定性估计，适用于风险敏感型心理健康评估场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要依赖语义表示，在面对模糊、噪声或数据分布偏移时容易产生过度自信的预测；缺乏可靠的不确定性估计机制，削弱了模型在风险敏感型心理健康应用中的可信度。

Method: 1) 多视图学习：整合encoder-only模型的语义信息与decoder-only模型的高层推理信息；2) 证据学习框架：基于主观逻辑（Subjective Logic）显式建模不确定性；3) 证据融合策略：平衡互补视图同时降低不可靠证据的权重。

Result: 在Dreaddit、SDCNL和DepSeverity三个真实数据集上分别达到0.835、0.731和0.751的准确率；噪声鲁棒性实验和可解释性案例研究验证了该框架能提供可信的不确定性估计和人类可理解的推理信号。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结合语义和推理信息，并使用主观逻辑的证据学习进行可靠的不确定性建模，在风险敏感的医疗健康应用中具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Automated mental health prediction using textual data has shown promising results with deep learning and large language models. However, deploying these models in high-stakes real-world settings remains challenging, as existing approaches largely rely on semantic representations and often produce overconfident predictions under ambiguous, noisy, or shifted data. Moreover, most methods lack reliable uncertainty estimation, undermining trust in risk-sensitive mental health applications. To address these limitations, we formulate the task as a multi-view learning problem that integrates semantic information from encoder-only models with higher-level reasoning information from decoder-only models, where reasoning-aware representations and uncertainty modeling are obtained in a trustworthy manner. To ensure reliable fusion, we adopt an evidential learning framework based on Subjective Logic to explicitly model uncertainty and introduce an evidential fusion strategy that balances complementary views while discounting unreliable evidence. Benchmarking on three real-world datasets, Dreaddit, SDCNL, and DepSeverity, reports accuracies of 0.835, 0.731, and 0.751, respectively, demonstrating its potential for reliable mental health prediction. Additional experiments on robustness to noise and case studies for interpretability confirm that our proposed framework not only improves predictive performance but also provides trustworthy uncertainty estimates and human-understandable reasoning signals, making it suitable for risk-sensitive applications in mental health assessment.

</details>


### [52] [PSK at SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection Using Ensemble Gemma Models with Synthetic Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05159)
*Srikar Kashyap Pulipaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究针对SemEval-2026 Task 9多语言极化检测任务（22种语言的二元分类），通过LoRA微调Gemma 3模型（12B和27B），结合GPT-4o-mini生成的合成数据，采用三种合成策略和多阶段质量过滤。最终系统以0.811的平均宏F1得分获得第2名，在3种语言中获得第1名，8种语言中进入前3。关键发现是替代架构在测试集上出现30-50%的F1下降，突显了模型泛化能力的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SemEval-2026 Task 9提出22种语言的多语言极化检测二元分类任务，需要构建能够跨语言泛化的模型。

Method: 使用LoRA微调分语言的Gemma 3模型（12B和27B参数）；采用GPT-4o-mini生成三类合成数据（直接生成、改写、对比对构建），并通过嵌入去重进行多阶段质量过滤；结合每语言阈值调优和12B/27B加权集成进行预测优化。

Result: 最终系统在22种语言上取得0.811的平均宏F1得分，排名第2；在3种语言获得第1名，8种语言进入前3。每语言阈值调优带来2-4% F1提升。

Conclusion: 替代架构（XLM-RoBERTa、Qwen3）在开发集表现良好但在测试集出现30-50% F1下降，强调了模型泛化能力的重要性优于单纯追求开发集性能。

Abstract: We present our system for SemEval-2026 Task 9: Multilingual Polarization Detection, a binary classification task spanning 22 languages. Our approach fine-tunes separate Gemma~3 models (12B and 27B parameters) per language using Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA), augmented with synthetic data generated by a large language model (LLM). We employ three synthetic data strategies (direct generation, paraphrasing, and contrastive pair creation) using GPT-4o-mini, with a multi-stage quality filtering pipeline including embedding-based deduplication. We find that per-language threshold tuning on the development set yields 2 to 4\% F1 improvements without retraining. We also use weighted ensembles of 12B and 27B model predictions with per-language strategy selection. Our final system achieves a mean macro-F1 of 0.811 across all 22 languages, ranking 2nd overall of the participating teams, with 1st place finishes in 3 languages and top-3 in 8 languages. We also find that alternative architectures (XLM-RoBERTa, Qwen3) that showed strong development set performance suffered 30 to 50\% F1 drops on the test set, highlighting the importance of generalization.

</details>


### [53] [The First Token Knows: Single-Decode Confidence for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05166)
*Mina Gabriel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出phi_first方法，通过单个贪心解码的首个内容词token的top-K logits归一化熵来检测LLM幻觉，在3个7-8B指令微调模型和2个基准数据集上，AUROC达到0.820，优于语义自洽性(0.793)和表面形式自洽性(0.791)，且计算成本远低于需要多次采样的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自洽性方法存在两大缺陷：1) 标准方法需多次解码，计算开销大；2) 对词汇形式变化敏感。语义自洽性虽通过NLI聚类改进，但引入额外采样和推理开销。本文旨在探索是否能在单次贪心解码中获取等效的不确定性估计。

Method: phi_first：提取首个内容承载token的top-K logits，计算归一化熵作为置信度指标。实验设置：3个7-8B指令微调模型，2个闭卷问答基准。使用AUROC评估幻觉检测性能，并进行蕴含测试(entailment)分析相关性。

Result: phi_first平均AUROC达0.820，优于语义自洽性(0.793)和表面形式自洽性(0.791)。蕴含测试显示phi_first与语义一致性中度至强相关；两者结合仅带来微小提升。AUROC差异在统计上显著。

Conclusion: 单次贪心解码的首token分布已蕴含多采样一致性的核心不确定性信息。phi_first应作为低开销基线方法，在调用采样基不确定性估计前默认报告。

Abstract: Self-consistency detects hallucinations by generating multiple sampled answers to a question and measuring agreement, but this requires repeated decoding and can be sensitive to lexical variation. Semantic self-consistency improves this by clustering sampled answers by meaning using natural language inference, but it adds both sampling cost and external inference overhead. We show that first-token confidence, phi_first, computed from the normalized entropy of the top-K logits at the first content-bearing answer token of a single greedy decode, matches or modestly exceeds semantic self-consistency on closed-book short-answer factual question answering. Across three 7-8B instruction-tuned models and two benchmarks, phi_first achieves a mean AUROC of 0.820, compared with 0.793 for semantic agreement and 0.791 for standard surface-form self-consistency. A subsumption test shows that phi_first is moderately to strongly correlated with semantic agreement, and combining the two signals yields only a small AUROC improvement over phi_first alone. These results suggest that much of the uncertainty information captured by multi-sample agreement is already available in the model's initial token distribution. We argue that phi_first should be reported as a default low-cost baseline before invoking sampling-based uncertainty estimation.

</details>


### [54] [Implicit Representations of Grammaticality in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05197)
*Yingshan Susan Wang,Linlu Qiu,Zhaofeng Wu,Roger P. Levy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究预训练语言模型（LMs）是否在其隐藏层中隐式习得了独立于字符串概率的语法性区分能力。通过在LMs内部表示上训练线性探针，发现该探针能够泛化到人类语法判断基准测试，在跨语言任务中也表现优异，且探针分数与字符串概率仅弱相关，表明LMs确实在某种程度上隐式习得了语法性区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型通过最大化语料库似然进行训练，是概率模型。已有研究表明LMs能生成语法正确的文本并在受控的最小对中区分语法正误，但字符串概率并不能整体上锐利区分语法与不语法句子。因此需要探究LMs是否在内部表示中隐式习得了独立于字符串概率的语法性区分能力。

Method: 对自然语文本语料库施加扰动生成语法和（合成的）不语法句子数据集，在LMs的内部表示上训练线性探针（linear probe）进行分类。

Result: 该简单语法性探针能够泛化到人类策划的语法判断基准测试，且优于基于LM概率的语法判断；在语义 plausibility 基准测试上表现不如字符串概率；跨语言泛化能力显著，在多种语言上优于字符串概率；探针分数与字符串概率仅呈弱相关。

Conclusion: 实验结果共同表明LMs在其隐藏层中在一定程度上隐式习得了语法性区分，这独立于其显式的字符串概率分布。

Abstract: Grammaticality and likelihood are distinct notions in human language. Pretrained language models (LMs), which are probabilistic models of language fitted to maximize corpus likelihood, generate grammatically well-formed text and discriminate well between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences in tightly controlled minimal pairs. However, their string probabilities do not sharply discriminate between grammatical and ungrammatical sentences overall. But do LMs implicitly acquire a grammaticality distinction distinct from string probability? We explore this question through studying internal representations of LMs, by training a linear probe on a dataset of grammatical and (synthetic) ungrammatical sentences obtained by applying perturbations to a naturalistic text corpus. We find that this simple grammaticality probe generalizes to human-curated grammaticality judgment benchmarks and outperforms LM probability-based grammaticality judgments. When applied to semantic plausibility benchmarks, in which both members of a minimal pair are grammatical and differ in only plausibility, the probe however performs worse than string probability. The English-trained probe also exhibits nontrivial cross-lingual generalization, outperforming string probabilities on grammaticality benchmarks in numerous other languages. Additionally, probe scores correlate only weakly with string probabilities. These results collectively suggest that LMs acquire to some extent an implicit grammaticality distinction within their hidden layers.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [55] [ipc_shared_ptr: A Publish/Subscribe-Aware Smart Pointer for Cross-Process Object Lifetime Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04226)
*Takahiro Ishikawa-Aso,Atsushi Yano,Koichi Imai,Takuya Azumi,Shinpei Kato*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: 论文提出ipc_shared_ptr，一种用于ROS 2零拷贝IPC的发布/订阅感知智能指针，通过利用发布/订阅结构特性优化引用计数，相比通用分布式引用计数实现数量级降低的全局通信开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS 2等发布/订阅中间件的真正零拷贝IPC要求订阅者引用发布者拥有的共享内存中的消息对象，需要解决对象在被引用时不能被回收但最终必须被回收的问题，同时正确处理崩溃恢复和瞬态本地QoS保留需求。

Method: 1) 提出ipc_shared_ptr，利用发布/订阅结构特性专门化Birrell引用列表，将全局元数据更新限制为每订阅者0<->1转换；2) 分析关键权衡：可扩展性vs实现简单性；3) 探索两种架构：iceoryx2（所有者驱动回收）vs Agnocast（单写者）

Result: 在Autoware规模下（200话题，每话题2订阅者，100Hz），Agnocast的E2E p99.9延迟比iceoryx2低2.9倍，验证了单写者架构在保持足够可扩展性的同时实现了更简单的实现。

Conclusion: 单写者架构在实现简单性上的优势超过了所有者驱动回收的可扩展性优势；对于ROS 2零拷贝IPC应用场景，单写者是更优的设计选择。

Abstract: True zero-copy Inter-Process Communication (IPC) in publish/subscribe (pub/sub) middleware such as Robot Operating System 2 (ROS 2) requires subscribers to reference message objects in publisher-owned shared memory. Objects must not be reclaimed while referenced, yet must eventually be reclaimed, with correct handling of crash recovery and Transient Local QoS retention requirements. We propose ipc_shared_ptr, a pub/sub-aware smart pointer for cross-process message lifetime management. ipc_shared_ptr exploits pub/sub structural properties to specialize Birrell's reference listing, limiting global metadata updates to per-subscriber 0<->1 transitions and achieving an order-of-magnitude reduction in global communication over general-purpose distributed reference counting. We analyze the key metadata management tradeoff: scalability versus implementation simplicity. Owner-driven reclaim offers greater scalability, but concurrent membership changes and reclamation decisions produce races that widen the correctness-verification state space. Single-writer achieves structural atomicity, eliminating this complexity at the cost of a centralized bottleneck. iceoryx2 (owner-driven reclaim) and Agnocast -- a true zero-copy ROS 2 IPC middleware sharing the publisher's heap with subscribers and adopting ipc_shared_ptr with single-writer -- embody each architecture. Comparative evaluation at the scale of Autoware -- the largest open-source ROS 2 application -- confirms that single-writer achieves sufficient scalability: at 200 topics, two subscribers per topic and 100 Hz, Agnocast's E2E p99.9 is 2.9x lower than iceoryx2's, justifying implementation simplicity over owner-driven reclaim.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [56] [Semantic Reverse Engineering Legacy Software Applications with ChatGPT, Gemini AI, and Claude AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04114)
*Christian Mancas,Diana Christina Mancas*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究探索使用ChatGPT、Gemini和Claude等AI工具对遗留数据库软件应用进行语义逆向工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗留系统(llegacy systems)的维护和理解面临巨大挑战，传统逆向工程方法耗时费力，迫切需要更高效的自动化解决方案。

Method: 利用ChatGPT、Gemini和Claude等大语言模型的语义理解能力，对遗留数据库软件进行语义层面的逆向工程分析。

Result: 展示了AI工具在理解和分析遗留数据库代码/架构方面的能力。

Conclusion: AI辅助逆向工程是处理遗留系统的可行方向，但仍需进一步研究以验证其实际效果和局限性。

Abstract: This research paper describes our research results on using ChatGPT, Gemini, and Claude AI to semantically reverse engineer legacy database software applications.

</details>


### [57] [Two Integration Pathways in Human-Centered Requirements Engineering: A Systematic Mapping Study of Structural Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04132)
*Imen Benzarti,Ikram Darif,Abderrahmane Leshob,Hafedh Mili,Darine Amayed*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一项关于Human-centered Requirements Engineering (HC-RE)的系统映射研究，通过分析56篇主要文献，揭示了该领域存在的结构性分割问题：认知-形式化(C-F)路径与参与式-迭代式(P-I)路径的近乎完全分离，这解释了需求工程生命周期中的集中现象和理论与实践之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程领域对人类中心方法的兴趣日益增长，但多学科贡献如何组织以及为何在RE生命周期中呈现碎片化尚未被很好理解。该研究旨在系统性地分析HC-RE领域的研究现状、结构和问题。

Method: 系统映射研究(Systematic Mapping Study)，从7个维度分析了56篇主要研究，维度包括：RE阶段、用户参与技术、贡献学科、评估方法等。

Result: 70%的研究涉及多学科贡献，但仅有39%经过实证评估，48%仅关注需求获取阶段。研究发现存在两条平行但近乎完全分离的整合路径：C-F路径（基于目标框架和形式化建模）和P-I路径（基于场景框架和迭代设计）。

Conclusion: 该领域的主要结构性缺口在于缺乏将人类中心工件转换为形式化RE规格的翻译机制。研究提出了包含四个优先层级的结构化研究议程，并建立了"体验中心需求工程"（Experience-Centered RE）这一新方向的基础。

Abstract: Human-centered Requirements Engineering (HC-RE) integrates user cognition, emotions, and social interactions into the RE process through contributions from disciplines such as psychology, cognitive science, design thinking, and human-computer interaction. Despite growing interest, how these multidisciplinary contributions are structured and why they remain fragmented across the RE lifecycle is not well understood.
  This systematic mapping study analyzes 56 primary studies across seven dimensions, including RE phases, user involvement techniques, contributing disciplines, and evaluation methods. Results show that 70\% of approaches involve multidisciplinary contributions, yet only 39% have been empirically evaluated and 48% address only the elicitation phase. A cross-study analysis reveals a structural separation between two parallel integration traditions: a Cognitive-Formal (C-F) pathway grounded in goal-based frameworks and formal modeling, and a Participatory-Iterative (P-I) pathway grounded in scenario-based frameworks and iterative design. Each pathway has developed complementary strengths, but their near-total disconnection explains the persistent lifecycle concentration and theory-practice gap observed in the corpus.
  The findings identify the absence of translation mechanisms between human-centered artifacts and formal RE specifications as the field's primary structural gap, provide a structured research agenda organized into four priority tiers, and establish the empirical foundation for Experience-Centered Requirements Engineering, a direction in which user experience is explicitly operationalized as a first-class concern in requirements specification.

</details>


### [58] [Shedding Light onto Safety Integrity Level and Basic Software Constraints in a Real-World Automotive Application: Case Study with Driverator Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04837)
*Tobias Denzinger,Matthias Becker,Peter Ulbrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对汽车ECU中因果链的非功能属性（安全完整性等级、AUTOSAR基础软件复杂度、内存需求）进行研究，引入Driverator配置框架实现可扩展的系统分析，帮助优化汽车电子系统的任务分配和软件架构设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While significant research has focused on temporal analysis and optimization of cause-effect chains in automotive ECUs, other critical non-functional properties remain underexplored. Improper task colocation based on safety integrity levels, the complexity introduced by AUTOSAR BSW, and memory requirement dependencies present significant design challenges that require systematic analysis approaches.

Method: The paper conducts a thorough characterization of a real-world automotive application, analyzing the interplay between SIL constraints, AUTOSAR basic software complexity, and memory architecture requirements.

Result: The paper provides a comprehensive characterization of a real-world automotive application, detailing the impacts of SIL constraints, basic software complexity, and memory requirements on system design.

Conclusion: The Driverator configuration framework is introduced as a solution for scalable system analysis, enabling thorough characterization of automotive applications considering SIL constraints, BSW impact, and memory requirements.

Abstract: Automotive electronic control units (ECUs) are intricate systems with hundreds of individual functions, numerous software components, and multiple interdependent tasks. A prevalent structural pattern in these systems are so-called cause-effect chains. While significant research efforts have been dedicated to the temporal analysis and optimization of these chains, particularly minimizing data age and function response times, other crucial non-functional properties remain relatively underexplored. In particular, the safety integrity level (SIL) classification substantially influences the system design by determining task colocation strategies. Improper sharing of functions or interweaving tasks with different safety levels can compromise the integrity of critical functions. Additionally, AUTOSAR basic software (BSW) (e.g. OS, runtime environment, communication stacks, or diagnostics) introduces complexity that varies based on task characteristics and SIL categories. Furthermore, memory requirements present another critical challenge, given the diversity of memory architectures and SIL-specific dependencies that strongly constrain task allocations. This paper thoroughly characterizes a real-world automotive application, describing an automotive application based on SIL constraints, the impact of basic software, and memory requirements. In this context, the Driverator configuration framework is introduced for scalable system analysis.

</details>


### [59] [EngThrive: Make It Fast and Easy to Do Great Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04259)
*Brian Houck,Tim Bozarth,David Liu,Dean Carignan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 微软开发的EngThrive系统通过Speed、Ease、Quality三个维度和Thriving guardrail，为开发者生产力测量提供可落地框架，结合遥测数据与调研实现规模化改进，已验证其在多种场景的通用评估能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SPACE、DevEx、DORA等框架已确立开发者生产力具有多维性，但从业者面临实际挑战：应该测量什么？如何利用数据改善？现有框架缺乏具体落地指导。

Method: EngThrive系统围绕三个维度（Speed速度、Ease易用性、Quality质量）组织生产力，以Thriving（活力）为guardrail确保开发者福祉与绩效同步提升。各维度采用结果导向的North Star指标配合诊断性子指标，结合系统遥测数据与开发者调研，实现规模化与上下文兼顾的测量。

Result: 展示了多个案例研究，证实结果导向测量能实现持续的系统级改进；验证了EngThrive作为通用评估语言的适用性，可评估开发工具、AI、组织政策、工作环境等多种因素。

Conclusion: EngThrive可作为通用评估语言，不仅适用于开发工具和AI评估，还可用于组织政策、工作环境等因素。它为企业提供了一种从"测量活动"转向"改善结果"的实践模型。

Abstract: Frameworks such as SPACE, DevEx, and DORA established that developer productivity is inherently multidimensional, but left practitioners with a practical question: what should we measure, and how should we use it to improve? This paper introduces Engineering Thrive (EngThrive), a measurement and improvement system developed and deployed across Microsoft's engineering organization. EngThrive organizes productivity around three dimensions - Speed, Ease, and Quality - with Thriving as a guardrail to ensure developer wellbeing improves alongside performance. Within each dimension, outcome-oriented North Star metrics are paired with diagnostic submetrics, combining system telemetry with developer surveys to provide both scale and context. We describe the design principles that guide metric selection, including an approach in which well-chosen metrics align "gaming" behavior with genuine improvement. We also outline the data platform, survey program, and dashboard ecosystem required to operationalize this approach in practice, and present case studies demonstrating how outcome-oriented measurement enables sustained, system-level improvements. Finally, we show that EngThrive functions as a general-purpose evaluation language, applicable not only to developer tools and AI, but to organizational policies, work environments, and other factors that shape how developers experience their work. We offer EngThrive as a concrete model for organizations seeking to move beyond measuring activity toward improving outcomes.

</details>


### [60] [Reproduction Test Generation for Java SWE Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04320)
*Toufique Ahmed,Jatin Ganhotra,Avraham Shinnar,Martin Hirzel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: First work to address repository-level reproduction test generation for Java, introducing TDD-Bench-Java benchmark and e-Otter++ solution to bring better diagnosis and validation capabilities to Java software development.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing reproduction test generation research focused on Python, neglecting Java which is critical for enterprise software development.

Method: Adapted a state-of-the-art Python reproduction test generator to Java at the repository level, creating TDD-Bench-Java benchmark with 250 instances from popular open-source Java repositories.

Result: Successful adaptation of reproduction test generation techniques to Java, demonstrated through empirical evaluation on both open-source and contamination-free proprietary datasets.

Conclusion: The paper provides TDD-Bench-Java benchmark and e-Otter++ solution for Java, validated on both open-source and industry proprietary datasets.

Abstract: Given an issue on a software repository, a reproduction test confirms its presence in the code before it gets fixed and its absence after. Reproduction tests provide crucial execution-based feedback for diagnosis and validation during software development. Unfortunately, they are usually missing. Therefore, recent work has introduced both benchmarks and a thriving literature on solutions for reproduction test generation from issues. However, that work has focused on Python and neglected other languages such as Java, which is important for enterprise software. This paper introduces both a benchmark and a solution for Java repository-level reproduction test generation. The benchmark, TDD-Bench-Java, is the first to model this problem and comprises 250 instances sourced from popular open-source repositories. The solution, e-Otter++ for Java, adapts a state-of-the-art reproduction test generator for Python to yield high performance on Java. To evaluate in an industry setting, besides empirical results with TDD-Bench-Java, this paper also presents results with a contamination-free proprietary dataset. Overall, we hope that this paper contributes to bringing better diagnosis and validation to Java software development.

</details>


### [61] [Towards Robust LLM Post-Training: Automatic Failure Management for Reinforcement Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04431)
*Lingzhe Zhang,Tong Jia,Yunpeng Zhai,Liancheng Fang,Kening Zheng,Hongyi Liu,Xiaosong Huang,Philip S. Yu,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究强化微调中的自动化故障管理，构建了首个细粒度RFT故障基准RFT-FaultBench，并通过实证研究证明RFT故障可通过训练动态和故障指纹进行观察和区分，进而提出统一异常检测、故障诊断与自动修复的RFT-FM闭环框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化微调（Post-training LLM的关键范式）的训练过程高度脆弱，但现有改进主要在系统层面或针对单个子问题，缺乏训练过程层面的系统性故障管理自动化方法。

Method: 构建了RFT-FaultBench基准（5个故障家族、16种故障类型、779次训练运行、22,549条训练步记录和1,457,288条轨迹级记录），并进行综合实证研究，证明RFT故障既可从训练动态中观察，也能通过经验故障指纹区分；基于此提出RFT-FM框架，将异常检测、故障诊断和自动修复统一为闭环系统。

Result: RFT-FaultBench既非trivial也非saturated，呈现清晰异常结构且在微妙故障设置下仍有挑战；RFT-FM在检测、诊断和缓解RFT故障上表现出强能力。

Conclusion: RFT-FM框架展示了在检测、诊断和缓解RFT故障方面的强能力，填补了强化微调自动化故障管理的空白。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning (RFT) has become a core paradigm for post-training large language models, yet its training process remains highly fragile. Existing efforts mainly improve reliability at the system level or address specific issues in individual subproblems by modifying RFT algorithms. Despite their effectiveness, they largely overlook the problem of failure management at the training-process level. When training goes wrong, practitioners still rely heavily on expert-driven manual inspection and correction, and automatic failure management for RFT remains largely unexplored. In this paper, we take a first step toward systematic failure management for reinforcement fine-tuning. To understand the empirical structure of RFT failures, we first construct RFT-FaultBench, the first benchmark for fine-grained failures in reinforcement fine-tuning, covering 5 fault families, 16 fault types, 779 training runs, 22,549 train-step records, and 1,457,288 trajectory-level records. Based on this benchmark, we conduct a comprehensive empirical study showing that RFT failures are both observable from training dynamics and distinguishable through their empirical fault fingerprints. Building on these findings, we propose RFT-FM, an automatic failure management framework for reinforcement fine-tuning that unifies anomaly detection, failure diagnosis, and auto remediation in a closed loop. Experimental results show that RFT-FaultBench is neither trivial nor saturated: it exhibits clear anomaly structure while still posing substantial challenges, especially under subtle fault settings. Moreover, RFT-FM shows strong capability in detecting, diagnosing, and mitigating RFT failures.

</details>


### [62] [Accountable Agents in Software Engineering: An Analysis of Terms of Service and a Research Roadmap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04532)
*Christoph Treude*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文是一篇愿景论文（vision paper），对主流AI编码助手和智能体开发工具的服务条款进行对比分析，揭示责任分配倾向，并提出负责任AI智能体软件工程的未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究主要关注AI编码工具的生产力提升，而对责任归属问题关注不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，通过分析服务条款（ToS）来理解AI开发工具的责任分配机制。

Method: 对多种主流AI编码助手和智能体开发工具的服务条款进行对比分析，评估所有权、责任、责任免除、信息披露等方面的条款规定。

Result: 发现提供商普遍将正确性、安全性和法律合规的责任转移给用户；在赔偿、数据重用、可接受使用等方面的政策存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 现有政策框架与日益智能化的软件开发工作流程不匹配；提出包含责任建模、治理设计、支持问责的工具开发、开发者认知实证研究等方向的未来研究路线图。

Abstract: AI coding assistants and autonomous agents are becoming integral to software development workflows, reshaping how code is produced, reviewed, and maintained. While recent research has focused mainly on the capabilities and impacts of productivity of these systems, much less attention has been paid to accountability: who is responsible when agents generate, modify, or recommend code? In practice, accountability is defined through the Terms of Service (ToS) and related policy documents that govern the use of AI-powered development tools.
  In this vision paper, we present a comparative analysis of the Terms of Service for widely used AI coding assistants and agent-enabled development tools. We examine how these documents allocate ownership, responsibility, liability, and disclosure obligations between tool providers and software developers, and we identify common patterns and divergences between providers. Our analysis reveals a consistent tendency to shift responsibility for correctness, safety, and legal compliance onto users, as well as substantial variation in how providers address issues such as indemnification, data reuse, and acceptable use.
  Based on these findings, we argue that existing policy frameworks are poorly aligned with increasingly agent-mediated and autonomous software development workflows. We outline a research roadmap for accountable agents in software engineering, identifying challenges and opportunities for modeling responsibility, designing governance artifacts, developing tooling that supports accountability, and conducting empirical studies of developers' perceptions and practices.

</details>


### [63] [Beyond Retrieval: A Multitask Benchmark and Model for Code Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04615)
*Siqiao Xue,Zihan Liao,Jin Qin,Ziyin Zhang,Yixiang Mu,Fan Zhou,Hang Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoREB基准，用于评估代码检索和重排的完整流程，解决了现有基准的数据污染和二元相关性退化问题。实验表明代码专用嵌入在代码到代码任务中表现优异但无通用赢家，短关键词查询对所有模型构成挑战，现成重排器存在任务不对称性，而本文微调的CoREB-Reranker首次在全部三个任务中取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码搜索评估仅关注第一阶段检索，而生产系统依赖更广泛的管道（包括重排）；现有基准存在数据污染、标签噪声和退化的二元相关性等问题。

Method: 构建了CoREB基准，使用反事实重写的LiveCodeBench问题，涵盖5种编程语言，以计时发布和分级相关性判断为特点。评测了11种嵌入模型和5种重排器，覆盖文本到代码、代码到文本、代码到代码三种任务。

Result: 代码专用嵌入在代码到代码检索中领先（约为通用编码器的2倍），但无单一模型在三项任务中均胜出；短关键词查询使所有模型nDCG@10接近零；现成重排器任务不对称，代码到代码任务波动12点，无基线在所有任务中净正增益；CoREB-Reranker首次实现所有任务一致提升。

Conclusion: CoREB-Reranker是首个在所有三个任务中实现一致提升的模型。数据和模型已公开，为代码搜索领域提供了更可靠的基准测试工具。

Abstract: Code search has usually been evaluated as first-stage retrieval, even though production systems rely on broader pipelines with reranking and developer-style queries. Existing benchmarks also suffer from data contamination, label noise, and degenerate binary relevance. In this paper, we introduce \textsc{CoREB}, a contamination-limited, multitask \underline{co}de \underline{r}etrieval and r\underline{e}ranking \underline{b}enchmark, together with a fine-tuned code reranker, that goes beyond retrieval to cover the full code search pipeline. \textsc{CoREB} is built from counterfactually rewritten LiveCodeBench problems in five programming languages and delivered as timed releases with graded relevance judgments. We benchmark eleven embedding models and five rerankers across three tasks: text-to-code, code-to-text, and code-to-code. Our experiments reveal that: \circone code-specialised embeddings dominate code-to-code retrieval (${\sim}2{\times}$ over general encoders), yet no single model wins all three tasks; \circtwo short keyword queries, the format closest to real developer search, collapse every model to near-zero nDCG@10; \circthree off-the-shelf rerankers are task-asymmetric, with a 12-point swing on code-to-code and no baseline net-positive across all tasks; \circfour our fine-tuned \textsc{CoREB-Reranker} is the first to achieve consistent gains across all three tasks. The data and model are released.

</details>


### [64] [CodeEvolve: LLM-Driven Evolutionary Optimization with Runtime-Enriched Target Selection for Multi-Language Code Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04677)
*Ajay Krishna Borra,Wenzhuo Yang,Samarth Arora,Akhilesh Deepak Gotmare,Gokulakrishnan Gopalakrishnan,Tharun Gali,Madhav Rathi,Doyen Sahoo,Manpreet Singh,Mayuresh Verma,Laksh Venka,Shuchita Singh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CodeEvolve是一个基于LLM的进化式代码优化框架，通过运行时引导的目标选择、MCTS搜索和自动化代码精化来提升程序性能和代码质量，在企业级Java代码库上实现平均15.22倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码性能优化通常依赖人工识别瓶颈，效率低下且易出错。现有基于LLM的优化方法多为单次生成，缺乏系统性搜索和验证，难以保证功能正确性。

Method: CodeEvolve扩展OpenEvolve框架，集成四项核心能力：1) 使用JFR运行时分析构建加权组件图进行目标选择；2) MCTS搜索引导优化方向；3) 多阶段评估流水线（构建验证、单元测试、性能检查、静态分析、LLM审查）；4) 针对Java和Salesforce Apex的领域特定评估管道。

Result: 在企业级Java代码库上，CodeEvolve对7个热点函数实现平均15.22倍加速，5/7函数优于单次LLM优化。Ablation实验表明，完整MCTS配置在Apex优化中平均产出19.5个有效程序（总20个），验证了搜索、过滤和精化各环节的贡献。

Conclusion: 运行时引导的瓶颈识别结合系统性搜索能显著提升LLM驱动优化的效果，多阶段验证机制有效保证了功能正确性，框架具有良好的领域扩展性。

Abstract: We present CodeEvolve, an evolutionary framework for improving program performance and code quality with Large Language Models (LLMs). CodeEvolve extends OpenEvolve with runtime-guided target selection, Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS), automated code refinement, and language-specific evaluation pipelines for Java and Salesforce Apex. The system uses Java Flight Recorder (JFR) profiles to build weighted component graphs and select optimization targets that account for most execution cost, reducing reliance on manual bottleneck identification. For each target, CodeEvolve generates candidate edits, evaluates them through build validation, unit tests, performance checks, static analysis, and LLM-based review, and retains only variants that preserve functional correctness. Across real-world optimization tasks, CodeEvolve improves performance and code metrics while maintaining correctness. On a large enterprise Java codebase, it achieves an average speedup of 15.22$\times$ across seven hotspot functions and outperforms single-pass LLM optimization on five of them. An ablation study on Apex optimization shows that the full MCTS-augmented configuration produces 19.5 valid programs out of 20 on average, indicating that search, filtering, and refinement each contribute to more reliable optimization.

</details>


### [65] [How Does Chunking Affect Retrieval-Augmented Code Completion? A Controlled Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04763)
*Xinjian Wu,Jingzhi Gong,Gunel Jahangirova,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这是一篇关于RAG代码补全中分块策略有效性的实证研究，在864个实验配置下证明了分块策略有显著影响，Function分块策略最差，而Sliding Window和cAST在成本-质量权衡上占优，上下文长度是主导参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chunking strategies in RAG pipelines for code completion lack empirical justification; practitioner recommendations are inconsistent.

Method: Controlled experiment crossing 4 chunking strategies × 4 retrievers × 5 generators × 9 parameter configs = 864 settings on 2 benchmarks (RepoEval, CrossCodeEval).

Result: Chunking significantly impacts completion quality; Function chunking underperforms others by 3.57–5.64 pp (Cliff's δ=–1.0); cross-file context length is dominant parameter (doubling yields up to 4.2 pp improvement); chunk size has weak, non-monotonic effect.

Conclusion: Function-based chunking is suboptimal for RAG code completion, never achieving Pareto-optimality; Sliding Window and cAST strategies dominate the cost-quality tradeoff. Cross-file context length (not chunk size) is the dominant parameter.

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines for code completion rely on chunking to segment source files into retrievable units, yet chunking strategies are typically adopted without empirical justification, and practitioner recommendations are notably inconsistent. We present a controlled empirical study isolating the effect of chunking on code completion quality by crossing four representative strategies (Function, Declaration, Sliding Window, and cAST) with four retrievers, five generators, and nine parameter configurations on two benchmarks (RepoEval and CrossCodeEval), totaling 864 experimental settings. Our results reveal that chunking strategy has a statistically significant effect on RAG-based code completion. Contrary to intuition, chunking based on functions underperforms all other strategies by 3.57--5.64 percentage points on RepoEval (Cliff's delta = -1.0), while the remaining chunking strategies perform comparably. Our further analysis demonstrates that this observation holds across all retriever--generator combinations. We also find that cross-file context length is the dominant parameter: doubling from 2,048 to 8,192 tokens yields up to 4.2 percentage points of improvement, whereas chunk size has a weaker, non-monotonic effect. On the cost--quality Pareto front, Sliding Window and cAST dominate both benchmarks; Function chunking is never Pareto-optimal.

</details>


### [66] [A meta-analysis of the effect of generative AI on productivity and learning in programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04779)
*Sebastian Maier,Moritz Gunzenhäuser,Jonas Schweisthal,Manuel Schneider,Stefan Feuerriegel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该meta分析综合了23项研究、27个效应量，发现GenAI编程助手对开发者生产力有中等程度的正向效应(g=0.33)，但对学习成果无显著影响(g=0.14)。生产力提升在受控实验环境中更明显，而在开源和企业环境中效应较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GenAI在编程中的应用日益广泛，但其对程序员生产力和长期编程技能发展的影响尚不明确，现有证据不一致。

Method: 系统检索ACM、arXiv、Scopus和Web of Science（2019-2025年），纳入27个效应量。使用RoB2和ROBINS-I评估偏倚风险，采用Hedges' g标准化效应量进行对比。

Result: 生产力：GenAI辅助有显著正向效应(g=0.33, 95%CI: [0.09, 0.58])，但存在显著异质性。受控实验环境中效应更大，开源和企业环境中效应较小。学习：无显著效应(g=0.14, 95%CI: [-0.18, 0.47])。

Conclusion: GenAI可提升开发者生产力，但效果高度依赖情境。在教育场景中，GenAI未能持续转化为学习或技能提升，计算机科学教育中需审慎整合GenAI工具。

Abstract: Generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) is increasingly used for programming, yet it remains unclear when and where GenAI tools lead to productivity gains. Evidence on the effects of GenAI on the long-term development of programming skills is similarly mixed. Here, we present a meta-analysis of $n = 23$ studies reporting $k = 27$ effect sizes to quantify the effect of GenAI-powered coding assistants on productivity and learning. We systematically searched (i) ACM, (ii) arXiv, (iii) Scopus, and (iv) Web of Science for studies published between 2019 and 2025. Studies were required to compare GenAI-assisted with unassisted programming using quantitative measures of (1) productivity (i.e., task completion time, commits, and lines of code) and (2) learning (i.e., exam performance). We assessed the risk of bias using RoB2 and ROBINS-I and compared standardized effect sizes using Hedges' $g$. We find a statistically significant, but moderate positive effect of GenAI assistance on developer productivity ($g = 0.33$, $95\%$ CI: $[0.09, 0.58]$), yet with substantial heterogeneity across settings. Notably, productivity gains tend to be larger in controlled experimental settings, while effects are smaller in open-source and enterprise contexts. In contrast, we find no statistically significant effect of GenAI assistance on learning outcomes ($g = 0.14$, $95\%$ CI: $[-0.18, 0.47]$). Overall, these results highlight that GenAI coding assistants can increase developer productivity, although these gains depend strongly on context. In educational settings, however, the use of GenAI does not consistently translate into improved learning or skill development, which highlights the need for careful integration of GenAI into computer science education.

</details>


### [67] [Toward an Understanding of Developer Behaviour while Using Bug Localization Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04828)
*Pablo Diaz Pedreira,Tamara Lopez,Michel Wermelinger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过用户研究探讨开发者如何在实践中使用bug定位工具，发现工具的采用不仅取决于准确性，还需要考虑实际工作流程和社交上下文等因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Bug定位研究主要关注自动化工具的准确性，但缺乏关于开发者实际如何使用这些工具的研究。

Method: 进行了一项定性用户研究：11名参与者在受控环境中完成4个真实bug定位任务，使用专门的工具并提供不同级别的支持信息。采用出声思考协议和半结构化访谈收集数据。

Result: 初步发现揭示了三个实践方面的洞察：开发者如何与工具交互、社交和上下文信息的角色作用、以及问题解决过程。

Conclusion: Bug localization是一个复杂的过程，工具的采用取决于比准确性更多的因素。单纯提高工具准确性不足以保证其在实际开发中的有效应用。

Abstract: Bug fixing is a complex and time-consuming task in software development. Bug localization research tends to focus on the accuracy of automated tools that suggest source code files for developers to look at. However, little is known about how developers use these tools in practice. This paper reports on an ongoing qualitative user study. Eleven participants worked through four realistic bug localization tasks in a controlled environment and were given varying levels of support information offered by a specialized tool. Participants were asked to think aloud in a semi-structured interview session. The preliminary findings provide insight into three aspects of practice: how developers interact with tools, the role social and contextual information plays, and problem solving. The study demonstrates that bug localization is complex and suggests that the adoption of effective tools depends on more than their accuracy.

</details>


### [68] [Patterns of Developer Adoption of LLM-Generated Code Refactoring Suggestions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04835)
*David Schön,Faiza Amjad,Tehreem Asif,Ranim Khojah,Mazen Mohamad,Francisco Gomes de Oliveira Neto,Philipp Leitner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究分析了169个GitHub提交，发现开发者大多直接接受ChatGPT的代码重构建议而不做修改。当有修改时，主要涉及5种模式，取决于重构活动类型、开发者提示词和ChatGPT响应的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究主要关注评估LLM生成的重构建议质量，但缺乏对开发者如何在实践中应用这些建议的理解。

Method: 分析方法学研究，收集并分析了169个GitHub提交，这些提交中开发者基于ChatGPT对话内容进行代码重构，并在提交信息中链接了相关ChatGPT对话。

Result: 发现开发者大多数情况下直接采纳ChatGPT的重构建议而未做修改。当进行修改时，这些修改大多是重大变更，分为5种不同的模式。

Conclusion: LLM生成的重构建议在实践中被广泛接受；开发者对建议的修改模式取决于三个关键因素：重构活动类型、开发者提示词和ChatGPT响应的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have gained widespread popularity and have steadily improved over time, enabling software developers to use them for various code-related tasks. One common task is code refactoring, where the LLM suggests changes for the developer to apply to their code to improve quality attributes such as readability or maintainability. While current research focuses on evaluating LLM-generated refactoring suggestions, there is a limited understanding of how developers apply these suggestions in practice. To explore this, we analyze 169 GitHub commits where developers refactor their code based on a ChatGPT conversation linked in the commit message. We found that developers mostly accept and use the suggestions without modifications. When changes are made, they are mostly major and fall into five different patterns that depend on the refactoring activity, the developer's prompt, and the validity of the response from ChatGPT.

</details>


### [69] [SynConfRoute: Syntax-Aware Routing for Efficient Code Completion with Small CodeLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04894)
*Kishanthan Thangarajah,Boyuan Chen,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对企业代码补全的隐私与质量矛盾，提出SynConfRoute方法：先用小型本地模型生成代码，通过置信度与语法验证自动判断质量，决定是否升级到大模型。实验表明3B模型经代码专业化训练可媲美10倍大的32B模型，SynConfRoute在常规任务上提升6.4%，多语言任务提升31%，同时减少58%计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业需要在代码补全的高质量与隐私保护之间取得平衡：专有模型效果好但存在代码泄露风险，而自托管大模型成本高昂难以维护。小型CodeLLM（1B-3B）虽可本地运行保护隐私，但在复杂任务上表现不佳。

Method: 评估了来自12个家族的29个代码专用LLM（0.5B-480B），在Python、Java和C++的执行基准测试上进行fill-in-the-middle代码补全评测；提出了SynConfRoute方法，结合token置信度与语法验证来自动决定是保留本地补全还是升级到更大的自托管模型。

Result: SynConfRoute在常规补全任务上pass@1提升6.4%，在更难的多语言任务上提升达31%；最终pipeline在常规补全上达到78.9%，比单独使用480B模型高7.4%，同时减少58%的加速器使用量；在三种语言上均有效泛化，从未误拒正确本地补全。

Conclusion: SynConfRoute无需额外训练，使用现成模型即可部署，在Python、Java和C++三种语言上均优于仅用置信度路由的方法，且从未拒绝过正确的本地补全结果。该方案在保持代码隐私的同时，显著提升了企业级代码补全的效率与质量。

Abstract: Enterprises want AI code completion that is both high-quality and private, but they face a tension: proprietary models yield better results yet risk exposing proprietary code, while self-hosting large models is expensive and hard to maintain. As a lighter alternative, small CodeLLMs (1B-3B) can run on a developer's workstation accelerator with code never leaving the machine, but they fail on harder tasks. A practical solution is to use the small model for most requests and selectively route difficult ones to a larger self-hosted model. In this study, we evaluate 29 code specialized LLMs (0.5B-480B) from 12 families on execution-based fill-in-the-middle (FIM) code completion benchmarks across Python, Java, and C++, and find that model family and code specialized training matter more than size: a 3B model matches a 32B model despite being 10x smaller. Analyzing the 3B model's failures, we discover that 46% of its incorrect completions are not valid code. To enable efficient code completion, we propose SynConfRoute, a training-free method that combines token confidence with syntax validation to automatically decide per-request whether to keep the local completion or escalate to a larger self-hosted model. SynConfRoute improves pass@1 by 6.4% over confidence only routing on routine completions and by up to 31% on harder multi-language tasks, and the resulting pipeline achieves 78.9% on routine completions, 7.4% higher than always using the 480B model alone, while reducing accelerator usage by 58%. SynConfRoute generalizes across Python, Java, and C++, improving over confidence only routing on all three languages without ever rejecting a correct local completion. The pipeline uses off-the-shelf models with no custom training, making it immediately deployable in practice.

</details>


### [70] [Conflict Essences for Transformation Rules with Nested Application Conditions -- Long Version](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04947)
*Alexander Lauer,Jens Kosiol,Leen Lambers,Gabriele Taentzer*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将禁用本质(disabling essences)的概念扩展到支持任意嵌套条件，并引入符号冲突本质(symbolic conflict essences)来捕获两条规则之间的交互关系。证明了转换对平行依赖当且仅当符号冲突本质可嵌入其中，并建立了符号冲突本质与带应用条件的转换规则的初始冲突之间的关系。结果适用于粘性HLR范畴。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的关键对和初始冲突分析方法虽然有效，但包含冲突转换对关键信息的结构过大。研究者已证明冲突信息实际上包含在更小的结构中（禁用本质/冲突本质），但现有工作仅支持ANF形式的应用条件，无法处理任意嵌套条件，限制了实际应用范围。

Method: 1. 将禁用本质的概念从ANF形式的应用条件扩展到任意嵌套条件；2. 引入符号冲突本质，由禁用本质构造，用于捕获两条规则之间的交互；3. 在粘性HLR范畴的框架下进行形式化。

Result: 证明了转换对平行依赖当且仅当其可嵌入某个符号冲突本质；建立了符号冲突本质与带应用条件的转换规则的初始冲突之间的等价关系。

Conclusion: 符号冲突本质能够有效地表示和分析图转换规则之间的冲突关系，且具有更小的结构表示和更强的表达能力。该方法扩展了现有冲突分析技术的适用范围，为基于图转换的软件建模提供了更精细的静态分析工具。

Abstract: Conflict and dependency analysis is an important static analysis tool that provides an overview of the potential interactions of (graph) transformation rules. This analysis is based on critical pairs and initial conflicts, which represent conflicting transformations in a minimal context. However, the crucial information about a conflicting transformation pair is contained in much smaller structures, called disabling/conflict essences in existing research. Recently, we introduced disabling essences for rules with application conditions which contain the information on how an application condition can be violated by another rule. In this paper, we extend the notion of disabling essences to support not only application conditions in Alternating Quantifier Normal Form, but also arbitrary nested conditions. We introduce (symbolic) conflict essences that are constructed from disabling essences and which capture the interaction between two rules. We show that a transformation pair is parallel dependent if and only if a symbolic conflict essence can be embedded into it and relate symbolic conflict essences to initial conflicts for transformation rules with application conditions. We present our results for adhesive HLR categories, which includes several types of graph-like structures.

</details>


### [71] [Architectural Constraints Alignment in AI-assisted, Platform-based Service Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04973)
*Julius Irion,Moritz Leugers,Paul Hartwig,Simon Kling,Tachmyrat Annayev,Alexander Schwind,Maria C. Borges,Sebastian Werner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种检索增强的脚手架方法，将基于平台的代码生成与智能体澄清循环相结合，在服务开发早期暴露并解决架构约束模糊性问题，从而提升AI辅助开发的生产就绪性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助开发工具虽能实现服务快速原型设计，但缺乏对生产环境所需的架构约束、基础设施依赖和组织标准的感知能力，导致生成的代码行为脆弱、部署能力有限。

Method: 提出检索增强脚手架方法，结合平台化代码生成与智能体澄清循环机制。通过模板检索与结构化交互，在服务脚手架阶段嵌入生产相关考量要素。

Result: 实验评估表明，该方法在架构一致性和部署就绪性方面均优于通用AI代码生成工作流。

Conclusion: 约束感知型检索对于将AI辅助服务开发与生产软件工程实践相协调至关重要。

Abstract: AI-assisted development tools enable rapid prototyping of services but often lack awareness of architectural constraints, infrastructure dependencies, and organizational standards required in production environments. Consequently, generated artifacts may exhibit brittle behavior and limited deployability. We propose a retrieval-augmented scaffolding approach that combines platform-based code generation with agentic clarification loops to expose and resolve architectural constraint ambiguities. By combining template retrieval with structured interaction, the method embeds production-relevant considerations during service scaffolding. Evaluation indicates improved architectural consistency and deployability compared to general-purpose AI code generation workflows, suggesting that constraint-aware retrieval is essential for aligning AI-assisted service development with production software engineering practices.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [72] [ARMATA: Auto-Regressive Multi-Agent Task Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04225)
*Yazan Youssef,Aboelmagd Noureldin,Sidney Givigi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端自回归框架，联合解决多智能体系统的区域分配和路径规划问题，通过多阶段解码机制统一高层分配与低层路由，在保持全局状态的同时实现协同优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常将分配(Allocation)与路由(Routing)两阶段解耦，忽视了阶段间的依赖关系；而去中心化启发式方法缺乏全局上下文，容易陷入局部最优。

Method: 提出集中式端到端自回归框架，采用多阶段解码机制，在单一自回归过程中统一高层分配决策与低层路由序列，同时维护集中式全局状态以实现工作负载分配与路由效率的隐式平衡。

Result: 实验表明，该方法显著优于多种基线方案，相比Google OR-Tools、IBM CPLEX、LKH-3等工业求解器 solution quality 提升最高达20%，计算时间从小时级缩短至秒级。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过端到端自回归方式有效解决了多智能体协调中的分配-路由联合优化问题，验证了集中式全局状态与多阶段解码机制在平衡工作负载与路由效率方面的有效性。

Abstract: Coordinating multi-agent systems over spatially distributed areas requires solving a complex hierarchical problem: first distributing areas among agents (allocation) and subsequently determining the optimal visitation order (routing). Existing methods typically decouple these stages ignoring inter-stage dependencies or rely on decentralized heuristics that lack global context. In this work, we propose a centralized, fully end-to-end auto-regressive framework that jointly generates allocation decisions and routing sequences. The core contribution of our approach is a multi-stage decoding mechanism that unifies high-level allocation and low-level routing in a single autoregressive pass while maintaining a centralized global state. This enables the model to implicitly balance workload distribution with routing efficiency, avoiding local optima common in decentralized methods. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method significantly outperforms diverse baselines, achieving up to a 20\% improvement in solution quality over industrial solvers such as Google OR-Tools, IBM CPLEX, and LKH-3, while reducing computation time from hours to seconds.

</details>


### [73] [DAO-enabled decentralized physical AI: A new paradigm for human-machine collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04522)
*Mark C. Ballandies,Florian Spychiger,Uwe Serdült,Claudio J. Tessone*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We propose DAO-enabled decentralized physical AI (DePAI), a democratic architecture for coordinating humans and autonomous machines in the operation and governance of physical-digital systems. We (1) synthesize foundations in blockchains, decentralized autonomous organizations (DAOs), and cryptoeconomics; (2) connect DAO design with digital-democracy research on deliberation and voting, showing how each can advance the other; (3) position DAO-governed decentralized physical infrastructure networks (DePIN) within a vertically integrated stack that links energy and sensing to connectivity, storage/compute, models, and robots; (4) show how these elements specify workflows that couple machine execution with human oversight, enabling enhanced self-organization of techno-socio-economic systems, which we call DePAI; and (5) analyze risks, including security, centralization, incentive failure, legal exposure, and the crowding-out of intrinsic motivation, and argue for value-sensitive design and continuously adaptive governance. DePAI offers a path to scalable, resilient self-organization that integrates physical infrastructure, AI, and community ownership under transparent rules, on-chain incentives, and permissionless participation, aiming to preserve human autonomy.

</details>


### [74] [Autonomous Synchronization of Discrete-Time Heterogeneous Multiagent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04627)
*Wei Hu,Quanyi Liang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究离散时间异构多智能体系统的自主同步问题。通过将同步问题转化为稳定模态的渐近解耦问题，给出充分条件，进而建立基于智能体初始动态矩阵平均值的同步条件。该方法放宽了对矩阵差异的要求，统一了同构与异构系统，降低了同步条件的保守性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对离散时间异构多智能体系统的自主同步问题，现有方法要求智能体间矩阵差异较小，保守性较高。

Method: 将同步问题转化为离散时变线性系统中稳定模态的渐近解耦问题，给出了充分条件；在此基础上，利用智能体初始动态矩阵的平均值建立同步条件。

Result: 在无需智能体间矩阵差异小的假设下，建立了基于初始动态矩阵平均值的同步条件。

Conclusion: 该方法降低了现有条件的保守性，实现了同构系统和异构系统的统一；数值仿真验证了理论结果。

Abstract: This paper investigates the autonomous synchronization problem for discrete-time heterogeneous multiagent systems.
  The synchronization problem is transformed into the asymptotic decoupling problem of stable modes in a class of discrete-time linear time-varying systems,
  for which we provide a sufficient condition.
  Leveraging this condition, synchronization conditions are established.
  The synchronization conditions are based on the average of the agents' initial dynamic matrices,
  without requiring the differences among these matrices to be small.
  This approach reduces the conservativeness of existing conditions and achieves a unification of both homogeneous and heterogeneous systems.
  Numerical simulation results are provided to support the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [75] [Bridging Perception and Action: A Lightweight Multimodal Meta-Planner Framework for Robust Earth Observation Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04777)
*Jinghui Xu,Boyi Shangguan,Mengke Zhu,Hao Liu,Junhuan Jiang,Guangjun He,Pengming Feng,Shichao Jin,Bin Liang,Yongzhe Chang,Junbo Tan,Tiantian Zhang,Xueqian Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出LMMP框架，解决自主地球观测智能体在多步骤任务执行中面临的组合复杂性和推理错误问题，通过双意识机制、元任务库和两阶段训练流程实现显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前整合规划与执行的单一模型架构在动态地球观测场景中面临组合复杂性爆炸和推理错误问题，亟需新框架提升任务执行的准确性和可靠性。

Method: LMMP框架包含三个核心组件：1）双意识机制，融合多模态图像特征与高层任务语义；2）元任务库，注入遥感领域专家知识以标准化领域逻辑；3）两阶段训练流程（专家蒸馏SFT + DPO偏好优化）。

Result: 在EarthBench和ThinkGeo数据集上，LMMP显著提升工具调用准确率和任务成功率，并展现出强即插即用能力，可有效增强不同执行器骨干的性能。

Conclusion: LMMP为自主地球观测智能体提供了高效、可扩展的规划框架，其模块化设计和领域知识注入机制为复杂EO任务执行提供了可靠解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous Earth Observation (EO) agents are transitioning from passive perception to complex, multi-step task execution. However, current architectures that integrate planning and execution within a single model often struggle with combinatorial complexity and reasoning errors in dynamic EO scenarios. To resolve these challenges, we propose the Lightweight Multimodal Meta-Planner (LMMP) framework. LMMP incorporates a dual-awareness mechanism that grounds strategic plans in both multimodal image features and high-level task semantics. Crucially, we introduce a Meta Task Library to inject remote sensing expert knowledge directly into the workflow, which standardizes domain logic and ensures plans are physically feasible. We further implement a two-stage training pipeline, initializing the Meta-Planner via expert-distilled Supervised Fine-Tuning and refining it through Direct Preference Optimization based on execution feedback. Extensive experiments on a dataset derived from EarthBench and ThinkGeo demonstrate that LMMP significantly improves tool-calling accuracy and task success rates. Moreover, the framework exhibits strong ``plug-and-play'' versatility, consistently enhancing the performance of diverse executor backbones across previously unseen EO missions.

</details>


### [76] [Tree-based Credit Assignment for Multi-Agent Memory System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04811)
*Marina Mao,Alexandr Liu,Pengbo Li,Siheng Li,Bo Zhou,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: TreeMem针对多智能体记忆系统中信用分配困难的问题，通过将管道扩展为树形结构并利用Monte Carlo方法估计各智能体贡献，实现了无需任务特定标注的智能体特定信用分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL方法存在局限性：要么使用最终任务奖励统一训练所有智能体（过于粗略），要么为不同子任务设计特定奖励（需要昂贵标注且难以可靠定义）。

Method: 将多智能体管道扩展为树形结构，使每个智能体的输出扩展为多个后续分支，通过Monte Carlo方法平均估计各智能体的贡献，将最终奖励转化为智能体特定的优化信号。

Result: TreeMem在长程基准测试中优于强基线方法，有效验证了树形结构信用分配对多智能体记忆系统的有效性。

Conclusion: TreeMem通过树形结构信用分配机制，显著提升了多智能体记忆系统的性能，验证了该方法在长程任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Memory systems are widely adopted to enhance LLMs for long-horizon tasks, and are commonly organized as multi-agent pipelines with memory building, summarizing, and retrieval agents. To empower this system, existing RL-based methods either apply final downstream task rewards (e.g., QA accuracy) for all agents uniformly, which are coarse and ambiguous, or design task-specific rewards for agents on different subtasks, which require costly annotations (e.g., key evidence) and are difficult to define reliably. To address these limitations, we propose Tree-based Credit Assignment for Multi-Agent Memory Systems (TreeMem), which derives agent-specific credit from the final reward without task-specific annotations. Specifically, TreeMem extends the multi-agent pipeline (builder--summarizer--retrieval) into a tree structure, where each agent's outputs are expanded into multiple subsequent branches. The contribution of each agent is estimated via Monte Carlo averaging over its subsequent branches, capturing how intermediate agent actions may influence the final reward. This converts the coarse final reward into agent-specific optimization signals. These signals are then used to update all agent policies simultaneously, helping heterogeneous agents specialize effectively. Experiments on long-horizon benchmarks show that TreeMem improves memory system performance over strong baselines, validating the effectiveness of tree-structured credit assignment for the multi-agent memory system.

</details>


### [77] [Evolving Idea Graphs with Learnable Edits-and-Commits for Multi-Agent Scientific Ideation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04922)
*Jiangwen Dong,Bo Li,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: EIG是一个基于图结构的多智能体科学创意生成框架，通过将提案表示为包含科学主张(节点)和关系(边)的演化图，解决了传统临时文本协调方式难以追踪创意弱点的问题。其双头控制器分别负责图编辑选择和提交决策，在多个基准测试中达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统通过临时文本(如草稿、聊天记录)协调，难以精确定位生成想法的弱点以及智能体如何改进它们。现有方法缺乏对创意演化过程的明确追踪机制。

Method: 提出Evolving Idea Graphs (EIG)框架，将科学提案表示为演化中的图结构，其中节点捕获科学主张，边编码关系(如支持和冲突)。采用双头控制器：(1)选择图编辑操作指导智能体执行，(2)判断图是否准备好提交为最终提案。

Result: 在AI Idea Bench 2025和LiveIdeaBench上，EIG在自动基准评分和盲法专家评分中均优于所有对比系统。消融实验表明显式图状态是主要性能提升来源，学习到的编辑-提交控制机制带来持续改进。

Conclusion: EIG通过图结构表示方法成功解决了多智能体科学创意生成中的弱点追踪问题，在AI Idea Bench 2025和LiveIdeaBench上均优于所有对比系统。消融实验证实了图状态表示和编辑-提交控制机制的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-empowered multi-agent systems offer new potential to accelerate scientific discovery by generating novel research ideas. However, existing methods typically coordinate agents through temporary texts, such as drafts or chat logs; it is difficult to pinpoint the weaknesses in the generated ideas and how the agents refine them. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Evolving Idea Graphs} (EIG), a graph-based multi-agent scientific ideation framework that can generate high-performance research ideas across various benchmark-native metrics, such as novelty, feasibility, and clarity. Instead of coordinating solely through texts, EIG represents a partially formed proposal as an evolving idea graph, where nodes capture scientific claims and edges encode relations (e.g., support and conflict), enabling unresolved weaknesses to remain identifiable throughout the idea evolving process. Specifically, a learned two-head controller operates over the evolving graph to guide the ideation: one head selects graph edits for agents to execute, while the other decides when the graph is ready for commit as final proposal synthesis. On AI Idea Bench 2025 and LiveIdeaBench, EIG outperforms all compared systems on both automatic benchmark scores and blind expert ratings. Ablations further show that explicit graph state provides the main performance gains, and learned edit-and-commit control adds consistent improvements.

</details>
