### [78] [Evaluating Patient Safety Risks in Generative AI: Development and Validation of a FMECA Framework for Generated Clinical Content](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04085)
*Lydie Bednarczyk,Jamil Zaghir,Julien Ehrsam,Maria Tcherepanova,Christian Skalafouris,Karim Gariani,Catherine Geslin,Claire-Bénédicte Rivara,Pascal Bonnabry,Laetitia Gosetto,Richard Dubos,Mina Bjelogrlic,Christophe Gaudet-Blavignac,Christian Lovis*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该研究开发并验证了首个基于FMECA的框架，用于前瞻性评估LLM生成临床摘要的患者安全风险。框架包含14个失败模式，在36份出院小结的应用中表现出良好的评分者间信度和可用性，为LLM临床应用提供结构化风险评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床文本摘要中的应用日益广泛，但缺乏结构化方法来评估相关的患者安全风险。FMECA虽为通用的主动风险识别框架，但尚未被改编用于LLM生成的临床内容。

Method: 由跨学科专家小组（n=8）通过文献综述和头脑风暴开发失败模式分类法，将标准FMECA维度（发生率、严重性、可检测性）改编为5点序数量表，并应用于36份由GPT-OSS 120B生成的出院小结，两轮独立标注后评估评分者间信度、可用性和内容效度。

Result: 最终框架包含14个失败模式，组织为多个类别。两轮标注间评分者间一致性逐步提升，最终失败模式识别达到中度至高度一致，严重性和可检测性评分达到良好一致，可用性评分良好（SUS均值79.2/100），评估者信心较高。

Conclusion: 该研究首次提出基于FMECA的框架，用于系统评估LLM生成临床摘要的患者安全风险，提供了一种结构化、可重复的方法来识别这些摘要导致的临床相关风险。

Abstract: Objectives: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for clinical text summarization, yet structured methods to assess associated patient safety risks remain limited. Failure Mode, Effects, and Criticality Analysis (FMECA) provides a proactive framework for systematic risk identification but has not been adapted to LLM-generated clinical content. This study aimed to develop and validate a novel FMECA framework for the prospective assessment of patient safety risks in LLM-generated clinical summaries.
  Materials and Methods: An interdisciplinary expert panel (n = 8) developed a taxonomy of failure modes through literature review and brainstorming. Standard FMECA dimensions (occurrence, severity, detectability) were adapted into 5-point ordinal scales. The framework was applied to 36 discharge summaries from four patients, generated by an open LLM (GPT-OSS 120B) using real-world clinical data from the Geneva University Hospitals. Reviewers independently annotated the summaries across two rounds. Inter-rater reliability was assessed at failure mode, severity and detectability score levels. Usability and content validity were evaluated using an adapted System Usability Scale and structured feedback.
  Results: The final framework comprised 14 failure modes organized into categories. Inter-rater agreement improved between rounds, reaching moderate-to-substantial agreement for failure mode identification and good agreement for severity and detectability scoring. Usability was rated as good (mean SUS: 79.2/100), with high evaluator confidence.
  Discussion and Conclusion: This study presents the first FMECA-based framework for systematic patient safety risk assessment of LLM-generated clinical summaries. The framework provides a structured and reproducible method for identifying clinically relevant risks caused by these summaries.

</details>


### [79] [Frontier Lag: A Bibliometric Audit of Capability Misrepresentation in Academic AI Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04135)
*David Gringras,Misha Salahshoor*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究揭示了LLM能力评估文献中系统性存在的"发表诱发差距"问题：对112,303条记录的预注册审计表明，中位论文评估的模型落后前沿约10.85个ECI单位，且该差距正以每年5.53个单位的速度扩大。同时，配置信息披露严重不足（仅3.2%摘要披露推理模式），结论层级普遍被不当泛化至"AI"层面。研究团队提出了VERSIO-AI检查清单作为改进方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用领域LLM能力评估文献存在根本性问题：读者想知道当前AI系统能做什么，但实际报告的是"数月或数年前更老旧、更便宜、激发程度更低的模型能做什么"。这种"发表诱发差距"导致研究结论系统性失准，且常以稀疏配置细节和泛化到"AI"的表述传播开来。

Method: 预注册审计（pre-registered audit）方式，采集112,303条LLM关键词匹配记录（2022-01至2026-04），经过筛选后得到18,574条合格记录和4,766篇可检索全文。以Epoch AI Capabilities Index (ECI)为基准测量 Frontiers，并结合Arena Elo和Artificial Analysis进行复现验证。设置8个假设检验：H1为中位数差距检验，H2为差距趋势检验，H4为推理模式披露率检验，H8为理性滞后分解。

Result: H1：中位论文评估的模型落后前沿约10.85个ECI单位（约等于Claude Sonnet 3.7与Claude Opus 4.5差距的1.4倍）。H2：该差距以每年+5.53 ECI单位的速度扩大（95% CI [5.03, 5.83]）。H4：仅3.2%的摘要披露推理模式（在支持推理的模型中），全文披露率也仅21.2%。52.5%的论文（95% CI [48.2, 56.9]）将结论层级提升至"AI"层面，并以OR=1.23/年递增。H8：理性滞后基线分解显示约25%源于同行评审延迟，75%源于"过度滞后"（excess lag）。

Conclusion: 作者提出了两项改进措施：(1) API访问补贴以降低研究门槛；(2) 编辑强制要求报告框架（如VERSIO-AI检查清单，Core 3条目可作为快速拒稿标准），强制披露模型快照、推理模式、工具访问、支架工程、提示词配置等信息，并在frontierlag.org提供按DOI的分析工具。

Abstract: Readers of applied-domain LLM capability evaluations want to know what AI systems can currently do. That literature answers a related, but consequentially different, question: what older, cheaper, less-elicited models could do months or years earlier (a 2026 paper evaluating GPT-4o-mini zero-shot, say, against a frontier of reasoning-capable, tool-using systems like GPT-5.5 Pro and Claude Opus 4.7), often reported with sparse configuration details and abstracted upward into claims about "AI" that propagate through citations, media, and policy. We measure the 'publication elicitation gap' (the gap between these answers) in a pre-registered audit of 112,303 LLM-keyword-matched candidate records (2022-01 to 2026-04; 18,574 admissible, 4,766 full-paper texts retrievable), comparing tested models to the contemporaneous frontier on the Epoch AI Capabilities Index (ECI), reproduced under Arena Elo and Artificial Analysis.
  The median paper evaluates a model +10.85 ECI (~1.4x the distance between Claude Sonnet 3.7 and Claude Opus 4.5) behind the contemporaneous frontier at evaluation time (H1); an exploratory rational-lag baseline (H8) decomposes this into ~25% peer-review latency, ~75% excess lag. The gap is widening at +5.53 ECI/year (H2; 95% CI [+5.03, +5.83]). Meanwhile, only 3.2% of abstracts (21.2% of full-texts) disclose reasoning-mode status on reasoning-capable models (H4) and 52.5% (95% CI [48.2, 56.9]) state conclusions at the level of "AI" rather than the evaluated model(s), rising at OR = 1.23/year.
  Proposed remedies include API-access subsidies and editorial enforcement of reporting frameworks mandating configuration-surface disclosure (model snapshot, reasoning mode/effort, tool access, scaffolding, prompting, etc.); VERSIO-AI is a 13-item checklist (Core 3 desk-reject) extending existing frameworks at the elicitation surface, with per-DOI analysis at frontierlag.org.

</details>


### [80] [Coupled-NeuralHP: Directional Temporal Coupling Between AI Innovation Exposure and Public Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04194)
*Amir Rafe,Subasish Das*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Artificial intelligence innovation exposure and public response co-evolve, but innovation arrives as irregular event streams while response is observed monthly. We introduce Coupled-NeuralHP, a hybrid event-plus-state model linking eight-domain USPTO AI patent publication streams to a train-only Google Trends response index. Under the cleaned response protocol, the validation-selected one-way real-data variant gives the best held-out innovation count forecasts in the registered comparison set (pseudo-log-likelihood -30.4 vs. -34.7; root mean squared error (RMSE) 471 vs. 532) while matching the stronger multi-lag factor-family baseline on response RMSE (0.295). Ablations show that the real-data response signal is carried mainly by the structured forecast head, whereas the reverse response-to-innovation block is not supported on held-out count prediction. Across 60 semi-synthetic replications with known structure, the broader coupled family recovers innovation-to-response links much better than vector autoregression with exogenous inputs (VARX) (F1 = 0.734 vs. 0.386). A placebo-controlled 2022 split-date analysis finds no robust milestone-specific regime break.

</details>


### [81] [AI and Suicide Prevention: A Cross-Sector Primer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04321)
*Emily Saltz,Claire R. Leibowicz*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文是关于AI与自杀预防的跨行业综述性文献，由 Partnership on AI 2026年多利益相关方研讨会推动，综合临床最佳实践与当前前沿AI系统的响应机制，识别模型、产品、政策层面的核心挑战，呼吁建立统一标准与协调监管框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI聊天机器人已成为数百万人事实上的心理健康支持工具，包括危机状态的人群，但当前缺乏临床验证、统一标准和协调监管，难以匹配其社会角色所需的责任与安全性。

Method: 通过临床文献综述、公开AI实验室政策分析、评估框架研究，以及与AI和心理健康领域领导者的访谈，结合2026年 Partnership on AI 主办的多利益相关方研讨会，形成跨部门参考框架。

Result: 从模型层、产品层和政策层三个维度识别了通用AI聊天机器人在心理健康领域面临的核心挑战，并明确了跨行业对齐的优先领域。

Conclusion: 提出跨行业协调的优先领域，呼吁AI实验室、心理健康从业者、政策制定者之间建立统一标准与监管框架，以应对AI聊天机器人在心理健康领域面临的系统性挑战。

Abstract: AI chatbots already function as de facto mental health support tools for millions of people, including people in crisis. Yet, they lack the clinical validation, shared standards, and coordinated oversight that their societal role demands. This primer was developed in conjunction with a multistakeholder workshop hosted by Partnership on AI in 2026, convening AI labs, mental health practitioners, people with lived experience, and policymakers, to provide a common cross-sector reference point for the current state of the field of AI and suicide prevention. It begins with an overview of clinical best practices, then turns to how frontier AI systems (as of winter 2026) detect and respond to suicide and non-suicidal self-injury (NSSI) queries. Together, these provide insight into what it would take to design and implement AI tools that not only better prevent suicide and NSSI, but also promote overall well-being. Drawing on clinical literature, publicly available AI lab policies, an emerging landscape of evaluation frameworks, and conversations with leaders across the AI and mental health fields, we map challenges posed by general-purpose AI chatbots for mental health across model, product, and policy layers, ultimately highlighting priority areas where cross-industry alignment is both urgently needed and achievable.

</details>


### [82] [An Evaluation of Chat Safety Moderations in Roblox](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04491)
*Priya Kaushik,Sonja Brown,Rakibul Hasan,Sazzadur Rahaman*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究对Roblox平台的自动聊天审核系统效能进行了首次独立评估。通过分析200万条聊天消息并结合LLM辅助分类，发现审核系统存在明显漏洞，大量有害内容（尤其是针对未成年人的 grooming和性化内容）成功绕过审核，违规用户还会主动采用规避技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Roblox是全球最受欢迎的在线游戏平台之一，拥有数亿日活用户，其中相当比例是未成年人。这些未成年用户面临被不良用户通过实时聊天功能接触的风险。Roblox虽然部署了自动化聊天审核机制，但这些机制的有效性从未被独立研究过。本研究旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 研究团队从四个游戏中收集约200万条聊天消息，遵循伦理规范和Roblox服务条款。首先手动标注了9.98万条消息作为ground truth，评估了四个本地部署的大语言模型（LLM）。表现最佳的LLM被用于分析整个语料库以识别潜在不安全消息，随后通过迭代式开放编码和轴向编码方法对这些消息进行人工分类，直至达到主题饱和。

Result: 研究发现了令人担忧的现实：大量涉及 grooming、性化未成年人、霸凌与骚扰、暴力、自残以及敏感信息分享等类型的不安全聊天内容成功绕过了现有审核系统。被标记过的违规用户继续发送有害信息，并采用多种技术手段规避审核机制。

Conclusion: Roblox的自动化聊天审核系统存在显著漏洞，大量涉及未成年人保护的高风险内容（ grooming、性化未成年人、霸凌、暴力、自残等）成功绕过审核。违规用户会主动采用多种技术手段规避审核机制。这表明需要改进现有的审核策略以更好地保护平台上的未成年用户。

Abstract: Roblox is among the most popular online gaming platforms, used by hundreds of millions of users every day. A substantial portion of these users are underage, who are at a greater risk, where abusive users may utilize Roblox's real-time chat interface to make the initial contact with potential victims. Roblox employs automated chat moderation mechanisms to detect potentially abusive messages; however, to date, their effectiveness has not been independently investigated. Toward this goal, we collected approximately 2 million chat messages from four games across multiple age groups and analyzed them to evaluate the moderation system. These messages were collected from public game servers following ethical and legal norms as well as Roblox's terms of service.
  We use this corpus to qualitatively study which types of unsafe chats escape the moderation system and how policy-violating users evade the moderation system. Given the dataset's scale, it is prohibitively expensive to conduct qualitative content analysis manually. Therefore, we adopt a two-step approach. First, we manually labeled safe and unsafe messages (n=99.8K) and used them as a ground truth to evaluate four locally hosted state-of-the-art large language models (LLMs). Next, the best-performing LLM was applied to the entire corpus to identify potentially unsafe messages, which we manually categorized using iterative open and axial coding methods until thematic saturation was reached. Overall, our findings reveal a troublesome reality: numerous instances of unsafe chat messages related to grooming, sexualizing minors, bullying, & harassment, violence, self-harm, and sharing sensitive information, etc., escaped the current moderation. Our analysis of users whose messages were previously flagged revealed that they continue to send harmful messages by employing a wide range of techniques to evade the moderation system.

</details>


### [83] [Guidelines for Designing AI Technologies to Support Adult Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04616)
*Jennifer M. Reddig,Glen R. Smith,Sanaz Ahmadzadeh Siyahrood,Wesley G. Morris,Yoojin Bae,Kaitlyn Crutcher,John Kos,Rahul K. Dass,Jinho Kim,Momin Naushad Siddiqui,Daniel Weitekamp,Ploy Thajchayapong,Sandeep Kakar,Alex Endert,Scott Crossley,Min Kyu Kim,Chris Dede,Ashok Goel,Christopher J. MacLellan*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: AI-powered educational technologies have demonstrated measurable benefits for learners, but their design and evaluation have largely centered on K-12 contexts. As a result, many AI-supported learning systems remain poorly aligned with the needs, constraints, and goals of adult learners. To better understand how AI systems function in adult education, this paper examines the deployment of several AI learning technologies developed within a multidisciplinary, national research institute in the United States focused on adult learning and online education. Drawing on longitudinal deployment data, we conducted a reflexive thematic analysis to identify recurring challenges and design considerations across systems. These insights were synthesized into a set of 19 design guidelines intended to inform future AI-supported adult learning technologies. We demonstrate the utility of these guidelines through a heuristic evaluation of the deployed systems. Lastly, we present a guideline exploration tool that aids in the ideation of technologies by connecting the guidelines to stakeholder statements surfaced in the analysis process.

</details>

### [84] [Beyond Static Best-of-N: Bayesian List-wise Alignment for LLM-based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04559)
*Ruijun Chen,Chongming Gao,Jiawei Chen,Weiqin Yang,Xiangnan He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: BLADE提出了一个贝叶斯框架，通过将历史先验与模型当前rollout的动态证据融合来持续更新目标分布，构建自适应于模型能力增长的自我演化目标，从而解决现有BoN对齐方法中的静态监督和梯度衰减问题，在多个真实数据集上实现持续性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM4Rec methods rely on token-level objectives, making it difficult to optimize list-level and non-differentiable metrics (e.g., NDCG, fairness). While Best-of-N (BoN) directly optimizes these metrics during inference, it has high computational cost. BoN Alignment aims to distill this capability into the model but suffers from: (1) Indiscriminate Supervision - static reference fails to distinguish relative quality of candidates exceeding empirical range; (2) Gradient Decay - effective supervision signal rapidly diminishes as policy improves.

Method: Introduces a Bayesian framework that continuously updates the target distribution by fusing historical priors with dynamic evidence from the model's current rollouts, constructing a self-evolving target that adapts to the model's growing capabilities.

Result: Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate BLADE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving sustained gains in both ranking accuracy and complex list-wise metrics.

Conclusion: BLADE achieves sustained gains in both ranking accuracy (Recall, NDCG) and complex list-wise metrics (Fairness, Diversity), breaking the static performance upper bound.

Abstract: Large Language Models have revolutionized recommender systems (LLM4Rec) by leveraging their generative capabilities to model complex user preferences. However, existing LLM4Rec methods primarily rely on token-level objectives, making it difficult to optimize list-level and non-differentiable metrics (e.g., NDCG, fairness) that define actual recommendation quality. While Best-of-N (BoN) directly optimizes these metrics during inference, its high computational cost hinders real-world deployment. To address this, BoN Alignment aims to distill the search capability into the model itself, yet current approaches suffer from two critical limitations: (1) Indiscriminate Supervision, where the static reference fails to distinguish the relative quality of candidates exceeding its empirical range, leading to a loss of ranking guidance; and (2) Gradient Decay, where the effective supervision signal rapidly diminishes as the evolving policy improves, resulting in inefficient optimization.
  To overcome these challenges, we propose BLADE (Bayesian List-wise Alignment via Dynamic Estimation). Unlike static approaches, BLADE introduces a Bayesian framework that continuously updates the target distribution by fusing historical priors with dynamic evidence from the model's current rollouts. This mechanism constructs a self-evolving target that adapts to the model's growing capabilities, ensuring the training signal remains informative throughout the learning process. Extensive experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that BLADE significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. Crucially, it breaks the static performance upper bound, achieving sustained gains in both ranking accuracy (Recall, NDCG) and complex list-wise metrics (Fairness, Diversity). The code is available via https://github.com/RegionCh/BLADE.

</details>


### [85] [RecGPT-Mobile: On-Device Large Language Models for User Intent Understanding in Taobao Feed Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04726)
*Bin Zhang,Weipeng Huang,Dimin Wang,Jialin Zhu,Yuning Jiang,Zhaode Wang,Chengfei Lv,Jian Wang,Qichao Ma,Li Chen,Junqing Wu,Yipeng Yu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RecGPT-Mobile proposes a lightweight LLM-based agent for mobile e-commerce recommendation systems, enabling real-time next-query prediction by deploying LLMs directly on-device, addressing the cost and latency limitations of cloud-based approaches.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional cloud-deployed LLM solutions for next-query prediction face high inference costs and latency issues, particularly problematic for rapidly evolving user intent in mobile e-commerce scenarios with resource-constrained devices.

Method: Lightweight LLM-based intent understanding agent deployed directly on mobile devices to capture evolving user interests and adjust recommendations in real time.

Result: Extensive offline analyses and online experiments demonstrate significant improvements in recommendation accuracy compared to existing approaches.

Conclusion: RecGPT-Mobile enables real-time recommendation adjustments on mobile devices with significantly improved accuracy, providing a scalable solution for LLM deployment in production-scale mobile recommendation systems.

Abstract: Predicting a user's next search query from recent interaction behaviors is a critical problem in modern e-commerce systems, particularly in scenarios where user intent evolves rapidly. Large Language Models (LLMs) offer strong semantic reasoning capabilities and have recently been adopted to enhance training data construction for next-query prediction. However, due to resource constraints on mobile devices, existing applications are deployed on cloud servers, resulting in high inference costs. In this paper, we propose RecGPT-Mobile, a framework that designs a lightweight LLM-based intent understanding agent to improve recommendation quality in mobile e-commerce scenarios. By deploying LLMs directly on mobile devices, our approach can capture evolving interests of users more quickly and adjust the recommendation results in real time. Extensive offline analyses and online experiments demonstrate that our method significantly improves the accuracy of recommendation results, laying a practical path for LLM deployment in production-scale recommendation systems on mobile devices, as well as a scalable solution for integrating LLMs into real-world next-query prediction systems.

</details>


### [86] [Beyond Seeing Is Believing: On Crowdsourced Detection of Audiovisual Deepfakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04797)
*Michael Soprano,Andrea Cioci,Stefano Mizzaro*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究通过众包实验评估了人类在音视频深度伪造检测中的表现，发现虽然众包可提供可扩展的真实性筛查信号，但识别具体篡改模态仍具挑战性，特别是音视频联合篡改检测准确率较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度伪造技术日益逼真且易于制作，引发了关于人类判断在假信息环境中可靠性的担忧。需要了解众包方式能否有效检测音视频深度伪造内容。

Method: 使用AV-Deepfake1M和Trusted Media Challenge数据集，每个数据集采样48个视频（共96个），通过Prolific平台招募众包工作者，每个视频收集10份判断（共960份判断）。研究者测量了工作人员在区分真实与篡改视频时的一致性、准确识别篡改类型（仅音频、仅视频、音频-视频联合）的能力，以及报告篡改时间戳的一致性。

Result: 众包工作人员很少将真实视频误判为篡改（低假阳性），但会遗漏大量篡改内容，各视频间的判断一致性较低。聚合多个判断可以稳定真实性信号，但无法恢复大多数工作者一致遗漏的篡改。篡改类型识别比真实性检测噪声更大，音视频联合篡改尤其难以识别。

Conclusion: 众包可以提供可扩展的音视频真实性筛查信号，但可靠地识别具体篡改类型（模态归因）仍然是一个开放性挑战。人工标注员在区分真实视频方面表现良好，但在检测篡改和识别篡改类型方面存在显著困难，尤其是音视频联合篡改。

Abstract: Deepfakes are increasingly realistic and easy to produce, raising concerns about the reliability of human judgments in misinformation settings. We study audiovisual deepfake detection by measuring how consistently crowd workers distinguish authentic from manipulated videos and, when they flag a video as manipulated, how accurately they identify the manipulation type (audio-only, video-only, or audio-video) and how consistently they report manipulation timestamps. We run two matched crowdsourcing studies on Prolific using AV-Deepfake1M and the Trusted Media Challenge (TMC) dataset. We sample 48 videos per dataset (96 total) and collect 960 judgments (10 per video). Results show that crowd workers rarely misclassify authentic videos as manipulated, but they miss many manipulations, and agreement remains limited across videos. Aggregating multiple judgments per video stabilizes the authenticity signal, but it cannot recover manipulations that most workers consistently miss. Manipulation type identification is substantially noisier than authenticity detection even when workers detect a manipulation, with joint audio-video cases being particularly hard to recognize. Overall, these findings suggest that crowdsourcing can provide a scalable screening signal for audiovisual authenticity, while reliable modality attribution remains an open challenge.

</details>


### [87] [CapsID: Soft-Routed Variable-Length Semantic IDs for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05096)
*Wenzhuo Cheng,Menghang Gong,Qixin Guo,Hang Zheng,Zhaobin Yang,Jianguo Lou,Zhengwei Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CAPSID通过胶囊路由机制替代硬残差量化来解决生成式推荐中SID分词器的语义崩溃问题，并结合SEMANTICBPE子词组合方法，在多个数据集上实现平均9.6%的Recall@10提升，同时将推理延迟降低至COBRA系统的51%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐系统中的分词器存在瓶颈：残差向量量化在每层采用硬最近邻分配，导致聚类边界处的多面语义崩溃，且早期错误会传播到后续SID位置。现有解决方案采用双表示设计（稀疏SID + 密集向量前缀），但增加了推理成本并放弃了生成式接口的简洁性。

Method: 1. CAPSID（胶囊路由机制）：每层将item概率路由到多个语义胶囊，用路由重构替代单一胜出码更新残差，当活跃胶囊置信度足够高时终止SID生成。2. SEMANTICBPE（语义BPE）：通过结合相邻SID token的共现频率与嵌入兼容性，将它们组合为可复用的子词单元。

Result: 在Amazon Beauty、Sports、Toys及3500万item的工业目录数据集上，CAPSID+SEMANTICBPE相比最强单表示基线ReSID平均提升Recall@10达9.6%，且在所有公开基准上匹配或超越COBRA稀疏-密集系统，同时推理延迟仅为COBRA的51%。消融实验表明软路由、迭代一致性和置信度驱动长度各自独立贡献，在尾部item（边界语义主导）上提升最大。

Conclusion: 胶囊路由机制有效解决了硬量化导致的语义崩溃问题，置信度驱动的动态SID长度和语义子词组合进一步提升了表示效率。该方法在保持单表示生成式接口简洁性的同时，兼顾了性能与效率，特别适用于长尾分布的推荐场景。

Abstract: Generative recommendation maps each item to a sequence of Semantic IDs (SIDs) and recasts retrieval as autoregressive token generation. In this paradigm the main bottleneck is the tokenizer rather than the Transformer: residual vector quantization with a hard nearest-neighbor assignment at every layer collapses multi-faceted item semantics at cluster boundaries and propagates early errors to later SID positions. A common workaround is to append a dense vector or attribute prefix to the SID, but this dual-representation design inflates inference cost and gives up the simplicity of a generative interface. We address the bottleneck at the tokenizer itself. CAPSID replaces hard residual quantization with capsule routing: at each layer an item probabilistically routes to several semantic capsules, the residual is updated by the routed reconstruction rather than by a single winning code, and the SID terminates once the active capsule's confidence is high enough. On top of CAPSID, SEMANTICBPE composes adjacent SID tokens into reusable subwords by combining their co-occurrence with their embedding compatibility. On Amazon Beauty, Sports, Toys, and a 35M-item proprietary industrial catalog, CAPSID+SEMANTICBPE improves Recall at 10 by 9.6% on average over ReSID, the strongest single-representation baseline, and matches or exceeds a COBRA-style sparse-dense system on every public benchmark while running at 51% of its inference latency. Ablations show that soft routing, iterative agreement, and confidence-driven length each contribute independently, and the gains are largest on tail items where boundary semantics dominate.

</details>


### [88] [Interests Burn-down Diffusion Process for Personalized Collaborative Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05165)
*Yifang Qin,Zhaobin Li,Arisa Watanabe,Wei Ju,Zhiping Xiao,Ming Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对推荐系统定制的扩散方案——兴趣燃烧过程（interests burn-down process），取代传统高斯噪声扩散过程。该方法模拟用户兴趣向候选物品衰减的过程，通过反向燃烧过程生成个性化推荐。实验验证了StageCF方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散模型在协同过滤任务中存在不足：Gaussian噪声与用户个性化交互行为的微妙特性之间存在不匹配（incongruity），导致次优结果。需要一种更贴合用户兴趣扩散本质的扩散方案。

Method: 1. 提出"兴趣燃烧过程"（burn-down process）：模拟用户兴趣向候选物品的衰减
2. 设计"燃烧上升过程"（burn-up process）：反向过程，生成个性化推荐
3. 实现StageCF方法验证该扩散方案的有效性

Result: StageCF在实验中优于现有的生成式方法和基于扩散的基线方法

Conclusion: 兴趣燃烧过程能够有效建模扩散式用户兴趣，与协同过滤任务需求高度契合，具备生成个性化交互的能力

Abstract: Generative methods have gained widespread attention in Collaborative Filtering (CF) tasks for their ability to produce high-quality personalized samples aligned with users' interests. Among them, diffusion generative models have raised increasing attention in recommendation field. Despite that the pioneering efforts have applied the conventional diffusion process to model diffusive user interests, the incongruity between the Gaussian noise and the subtle nature of user's personalized interaction behavior has led to sub-optimal results. To this end, we introduce a specifically-tailored diffusion scheme for interaction systems, namely the interests burn-down process. The interests burn-down process delineates the decay of user interests towards candidate items, complemented by its reverse burn-up process that yields personalized recommendation for users. The inherent burn-down nature of this process adeptly models the diffusive user interests, aligning seamlessly with the requirements of CF tasks. We present a novel recommendation method StageCF to illustrate the superiority of this newly proposed diffusion process. Experimental results have demonstrated the effectiveness of StageCF against existing generative and diffusion-based baseline methods. Furthermore, comprehensive studies validate the functionality of interests burn-down process, shedding light on its capacity to generate personalized interactions.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [89] [Constraint-Aware Execution Planning for Hybrid Space-Ground Compute Workloads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04052)
*Subhadip Mitra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Constraint-Aware Execution（CAE）系统，解决 LEO 卫星数据量与下行能力不匹配的核心问题，通过轨道环境建模、成本驱动的计算放置、自适应 FEC 传输插入和贪心调度四个阶段，为给定工作负载生成确定性执行计划。系统已在生产环境中部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近地轨道（LEO）卫星产生的科学数据量远超过其下行链路能力（高约两个数量级），迫使运营商在每个工作负载上决策计算部署位置（星上 vs 地面）、如何通过狭窄的接触窗口穿越空-地边界，以及如何在噪声信道上维持传输保障。

Method: CAE 系统包含四个阶段：（1）轨道环境构建：基于 SGP4 传播模型，结合星蚀检测与地面站过境预测；（2）计算放置：通过成本模型比较星载资源消耗与传输开销；（3）传输插入：包含自适应前向纠错（FEC）和安全开销建模；（4）贪心首次适配调度：将任务调度至轨道窗口，同时满足功率、热控、计算和通信约束。

Result: CAE 在五种代表性工作负载模式上进行了评估，覆盖不同轨道 regime 的卫星，结果表明：（1）可在两秒内生成可行计划；（2）正确利用星上数据降维减少传输量；（3）自适应调整 FEC 和多路径分配以适应信道条件变化。

Conclusion: CAE 已部署为生产级 API，可基于任何目录卫星的实时双行元数据（TLE）计算执行计划，验证了系统的实用性和工程可行性。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellites increasingly carry compute hardware capable of on-board processing, yet each satellite generates roughly two orders of magnitude more data than it can downlink per orbit. This mismatch forces operators to decide, for every workload, which computation runs on-board and which runs on the ground, how intermediate data crosses the space-ground boundary through narrow contact windows, and how to maintain delivery guarantees over noisy channels. We present Constraint-Aware Execution (CAE), a planning system that takes a satellite identifier, a workload expressed as a directed acyclic graph of processing steps, and a set of orbital and resource constraints, and produces a deterministic, physically grounded execution plan. CAE operates in four phases: (1) orbital environment construction via SGP4 propagation with eclipse detection and ground station pass prediction, (2) compute placement using a cost model that compares on-board resource consumption against transfer overhead, (3) transfer insertion with adaptive forward error correction and security overhead modeling, and (4) greedy first-fit scheduling into orbital windows under power, thermal, compute, and communication constraints. We evaluate CAE against five representative workload patterns across satellites in distinct orbital regimes and demonstrate that the system produces feasible plans in under two seconds, correctly exploits onboard data reduction to minimize transfer volume, and adapts FEC and multi-pass allocation to varying channel conditions. CAE is deployed as a production API computing plans for any cataloged satellite using live two-line element data.

</details>


### [90] [Microbenchmark-Driven Analytical Performance Modeling Across Modern GPU Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04178)
*Aaron Jarmusch,Sunita Chandrasekaran*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对NVIDIA Blackwell (B200)和AMD CDNA3 (MI300A) GPU架构设计了基于微基准测试的解析性能模型。 Blackwell模型捕获了TMEM异步拷贝、TMA和第5代张量核心；CDNA3模型捕获了Infinity Cache层次结构、VGPR约束和占用率。验证结果显示B200上21个内核的MAE为1.31%，MI300A上27个内核的MAE为0.09%，远优于naive roofline基线（误差>95%）。模型进一步在H200和MI250X上验证，表明无需重大重构即可适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着GPU架构日益复杂（多级内存层次结构、矩阵单元、多精度格式），理论峰值性能与实际可达性能之间的差距不断扩大。现有naive roofline模型误差超过95%，无法准确预测现代GPU的实际性能，因此需要更精细的架构感知性能模型。

Method: 采用系统化微基准测试表征方法：为Blackwell建模TMEM（张量内存）、TMA（异步批量拷贝）和第5代张量核心；为CDNA3建模Infinity Cache层次结构、VGPR约束和occupancy。基于硬件参数（HBM带宽、容量、缓存配置）对模型进行校准，并将模型跨代际迁移到H200（Hopper）和MI250X（CDNA2）。

Result: 模型验证精度：Blackwell B200上21个kernel的MAE为1.31%，MI300A上27个kernel的MAE为0.09%，显著优于naive roofline基线（误差>95%）。模型可跨架构迁移，在H200和MI250X上验证有效，无需重大重构。

Conclusion: 本文提出的架构感知解析性能模型能够精确捕获现代GPU的复杂硬件特性，验证精度高且具有良好的跨代际可迁移性，可作为GPU性能优化和架构分析的可靠工具。计划在论文接收后开源所有模型和基准测试。

Abstract: Rapidly evolving GPU architectures featuring complex memory hierarchies, matrix units, and varied precision formats continue to widen the gap between theoretical peaks and achievable performance. We design and develop analytical performance models for NVIDIA Blackwell (B200) and AMD CDNA3 (MI300A) grounded in systematic microbenchmark characterization. For Blackwell, the model captures Tensor Memory (TMEM), asynchronous bulk copy (TMA), and 5th-generation tensor cores; for CDNA3, the model captures Infinity Cache hierarchy, VGPR constraints, and occupancy. Validation yields 1.31% MAE on B200 (21 kernels) and 0.09% on MI300A (27 kernels), while naive roofline baselines exceed 95% error on the same kernels. We further validate the models using Rodinia~3.1 and SPEChpc 2021 Tiny.The models are updated with HBM bandwidth, capacity, and cache parameters and applied to H200 (Hopper) and MI250X (CDNA2), indicating no major restructuring of the models are needed. All models and benchmarks will be released as open-source upon acceptance.

</details>


### [91] [ClusterLess: Deadline-Aware Serverless Workflow Orchestration on Federated Edge Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04310)
*Reza Farahani,Mario Colosi,Ilir Murturi,Stefan Nastic,Massimo Villari,Schahram Dustdar,Radu Prodan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: The recent convergence of edge computing, serverless execution, and Kubernetes (K8s) based container orchestration has enabled the processing of application workflows close to data sources. While effective within a single edge cluster, existing schemes do not generalize to federated multi edge environments, where multiple workflows execute concurrently under strict end to end (E2E) deadline constraints. This paper introduces ClusterLess, a deadline aware serverless workflow orchestration method for federated multi edge K8s clusters. ClusterLess manages the E2E lifecycle of workflow execution, including dependency analysis, execution mode selection, and resource aware placement. To this end, it integrates structured intra cluster orchestration with a leader selected, super master driven intercluster coordination layer, determining where and how each workflow function should be executed across the federated edge clusters. We implement ClusterLess using OpenFaaS as the serverless execution substrate and Argo for workflow management, and deploy it on a realistic testbed of six edge clusters comprising 64 heterogeneous edge nodes. Experimental results with concurrent serverless workflows, spanning 18 workload configurations across different input sizes and deadline classes, show that ClusterLess reduces workflow completion time by up to 40 %, increases deadline satisfaction from below 50 % to over 90 %, and confines deadline violations to single digit seconds compared to four baseline methods.

</details>


### [92] [Orchestrating Serverless Applications in the Edge Cloud Space Continuum: What Breaks and What is Next?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04316)
*Hadi Tabatabaee Malazi,Reza Farahani,Nitinder Mohan,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究如何将无服务器计算扩展到LEO卫星星座边缘云环境。指出现有服务器less编排的10个失效假设，提出时空执行、约束感知放置、去中心化状态正确性三类核心挑战，并给出针对性架构方案，以洪水响应为用例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统服务器less编排无法直接应用于LEO卫星星座环境。LEO系统具有时变接触图、间歇性链路可用性以及对能源、内存、通信和运营成本的严格约束，现有假设不再成立，需要重新设计编排框架。

Method: 识别现有无服务器编排中的十个失效假设，并将其组织为三个核心挑战：动态图上的时空执行、约束感知的功能放置与扩展、以及去中心化和延迟状态下的正确性与进展保障。基于这些挑战提出了一种支持强健高效无服务器执行的架构。

Result: 提出了针对LEO卫星边缘云连续体环境的三类核心挑战及相应架构，为时空动态环境下的无服务器计算提供了系统性解决方案。

Conclusion: 通过洪水响应用例验证了所提架构的可行性，为连续体边缘计算环境中的无服务器编排提供了系统性的解决思路。

Abstract: Serverless computing has matured into an effective execution model for edge cloud environments, enabling function level decomposition, demand driven scaling, and workflow execution across stable, well provisioned infrastructure. This success motivates extending it to the edge cloud space continuum, where Low Earth Orbit (LEO) constellations are increasingly explored as distributed compute substrates. However, existing serverless orchestration is not directly applicable in this setting, where LEO systems impose time varying contact graphs, intermittent link availability, and strict feasibility constraints on energy, memory, communication, and operational cost. This article identifies ten broken assumptions in existing serverless orchestration and organizes them into three core challenges: spatiotemporal execution over dynamic graphs, constraint aware function placement and scaling, and correctness and progress under decentralized and delayed state. It then proposes an architecture that enables robust and efficient serverless execution across the continuum, grounded in these challenges and demonstrated through a representative flood response use case.

</details>


### [93] [One Pool, Two Caches: Adaptive HBM Partitioning for Accelerating Generative Recommender Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04450)
*Wenjun Yu,Shuguang Han,Amelie Chi Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: HELM是一种针对生成式推荐系统推理的运行时资源管理系统，通过协同优化嵌入热缓存与KV缓存的HBM分配及请求路由，解决两者内存竞争导致的延迟问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐系统推理中，嵌入热缓存（EMB）与KV缓存对GPU HBM的直接竞争导致两者效率此消彼长；现有系统孤立地优化两者，忽视了最优EMB-KV分配比率在不同工作负载下可能偏移达0.35，使20-30%的延迟改进无法实现；在线重分配虽必要，但朴素方案会引入H2D重填流量导致P99 SLO违例。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：（1）自适应内存分配：采用三层PPO控制器（冻结基础策略、在线残差适配器、突发感知恢复控制器），实现32μs决策延迟且与离线最优比率偏差仅0.024-0.029；（2）EMB-KV感知调度：联合考虑KV驻留、嵌入局部性和节点负载进行请求路由，避免异构分配下的路由低效。

Result: 在32节点A100集群上使用三个生产规模数据集评估，HELM相比最佳静态策略降低P99延迟24-38%，在Steady、Trend和Burst工作负载下达到93.5-99.6%的SLO满意度，性能显著超越先进基线且不影响吞吐量。

Conclusion: HELM通过将自适应内存分配与EMB-KV感知的请求调度相结合，有效解决了生成式推荐系统中EMB与KV缓存之间的内存竞争问题，在不牺牲吞吐量的前提下显著降低了P99延迟并满足SLO要求。

Abstract: Generative Recommender (GR) inference places embedding hot caches (EMB) and KV caches in direct competition for limited GPU HBM: allocating more memory to one improves its efficiency but degrades the other. Existing systems optimize them in isolation, overlooking that the optimal EMB-KV allocation ratio can shift by up to 0.35 across workload regimes, leaving 20-30\% latency improvement unrealized. While online reallocation is required to close this gap, naive approaches introduce H2D refill traffic on the critical path, causing P99 SLO violations.
  To address this, we present HELM, which jointly manages HBM allocation and request routing at runtime through two key components: (1) Adaptive Memory Allocation, a three-layer PPO-based controller (frozen base policy, online residual adapter, and burst-aware recovery controller) that achieves $32\,\mathrm{μs}$ decision latency while staying within 0.024-0.029 of the offline-optimal ratio; and (2) EMB-KV-Aware Scheduling, which routes requests by jointly considering KV residency, embedding locality, and node load to avoid routing inefficiencies under heterogeneous allocations. Evaluations on three production-scale datasets over a 32-node A100 cluster show that HELM reduces P99 latency by 24-38\% over the best static policy and achieves 93.5-99.6\% SLO satisfaction across Steady, Trend, and Burst workloads, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines without sacrificing throughput.

</details>


### [94] [Coral: Cost-Efficient Multi-LLM Serving over Heterogeneous Cloud GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04357)
*Yixuan Mei,Zikun Li,Zixuan Chen,Shiqi Pan,Mengdi Wu,Xupeng Miao,Zhihao Jia,K. V. Rashmi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Coral是一个异构感知的多LLM服务系统，通过联合优化资源分配与服务策略，结合无损两阶段分解将在线求解时间从数小时缩短至数十秒，在多种GPU配置下实现显著的成本节省和吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM应用呈碎片化趋势，单一模型难以占据主导地位；同时云端存在大量中低端GPU及旧代GPU，其可用性好且性价比与高端硬件相当。如何高效利用这些异构资源并发服务多个LLM成为核心挑战。

Method: Coral采用两层分解方法：1）离线阶段进行批量优化建模；2）在线阶段基于离线结果进行快速自适应求解。该方法保留联合最优性的同时将在线求解时间从小时级降至秒级。

Result: 在6个模型和20种GPU配置上的实验表明：与最佳基线相比，Coral降低服务成本最多2.79倍；在资源稀缺条件下，吞吐量提升最多2.39倍。

Conclusion: Coral通过两阶段无损分解实现分钟级在线求解，在多模型异构GPU环境下实现高达2.79倍的成本降低和2.39倍的吞吐量提升，为碎片化LLM部署与异构资源利用提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: The usage of large language models (LLMs) has grown increasingly fragmented, with no single model dominating. Meanwhile, cloud providers offer a wide range of mid-tier and older-generation GPUs that enjoy better availability and deliver comparable performance per dollar to top-tier hardware. To efficiently harness these heterogeneous resources for serving multiple LLMs concurrently, we introduce Coral, an adaptive heterogeneity-aware multi-LLM serving system. The key idea behind Coral is to jointly optimize resource allocation and the serving strategy of each model replica across all models. To keep pace with shifting throughput demand and resource availability, Coral applies a lossless two-stage decomposition that preserves joint optimality while cutting online solve time from hours to tens of seconds. Our evaluation across 6 models and 20 GPU configurations shows that Coral reduces serving cost by up to 2.79$\times$ over the best baseline, and delivers up to 2.39$\times$ higher goodput under scarce resource availability.

</details>


### [95] [CCL-D: A High-Precision Diagnostic System for Slow and Hang Anomalies in Large-Scale Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04478)
*Yida Gu,Fakang Wang,Jianhao Fu,Zhenhang Sun,Qianyu Zhang,Hairui Zhao,Xingchen Liu,Yang Tian,Wenjing Huang,Zedong Liu,Yifan Chen,Jinwu Yang,Yueyuan Zhou,Qian Zhao,Haoxu Li,Tao Wang,Feng Yu,Zhan Wang,Guangming Tan,Dingwen Tao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CCL-D是一个用于大规模分布式训练中慢/挂起异常检测与定位的高精度诊断系统，通过实时探针和智能分析器的协同工作，在4000-GPU集群上将故障定位时间从数小时/天缩短至6分钟内，显著优于现有解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着训练规模增长，集合通信库(CCL)面临来自硬件、软件和环境因素复杂交互导致的异常问题，其中慢/挂起通信是最常见且诊断耗时最长的故障类型，传统诊断方法准确性低、效率差，往往需要数小时甚至数天才能定位根因。

Method: CCL-D采用双模块架构：1) 实时探针模块，使用轻量级分布式追踪框架监控跨层异常指标；2) 智能决策分析器，执行自动化异常检测与根因定位。该系统通过测量跨层异常指标来监测通信流量，精准识别故障GPU rank。

Result: 在4000-GPU集群上部署一年，CCL-D实现了对已知慢/挂起异常的近乎完全覆盖，并将故障rank定位时间缩短至6分钟以内。

Conclusion: CCL-D substantially outperforms existing solutions in diagnosing slow/hang anomalies in large-scale distributed training systems.

Abstract: As training scales grow, collective communication libraries (CCL) increasingly face anomalies arising from complex interactions among hardware, software, and environmental factors. These anomalies typically manifest as slow/hang communication, the most frequent and time-consuming category to diagnose. However, traditional diagnostic methods remain inaccurate and inefficient, frequently requiring hours or even days for root cause analysis. To address this, we propose CCL-D, a high-precision diagnostic system designed to detect and locate slow/hang anomalies in large-scale distributed training. CCL-D integrates a rank-level real-time probe with an intelligent decision analyzer. The probe measures cross-layer anomaly metrics using a lightweight distributed tracing framework to monitor communication traffic. The analyzer performs automated anomaly detection and root-cause location, precisely identifying the faulty GPU rank. Deployed on a 4,000-GPU cluster over one year, CCL-D achieved near-complete coverage of known slow/hang anomalies and pinpointed affected ranks within 6 minutes-substantially outperforming existing solutions.

</details>


### [96] [Toward a Risk Assessment Framework for Institutional DeFi: A Nine-Dimension Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05145)
*Eva Oberholzer,Valeriy Zamaraiev*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个扩展至9个维度的DeFi风险评估框架，在原有6个维度基础上新增可组合性风险、理解债务和时间风险动态三个维度，并引入透明度置信度修正量。通过对8000多个DeFi协议的本体论分析和12起重大DeFi事件（总计约25亿美元损失）的回顾性验证，证明了新增维度对完整根本原因表征的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DeFi风险评估方法强调协议特定参数优化或概念性分类法，缺乏可解释性、可组合性感知且结构独立的评估方法，无法满足机构级DeFi采用的风险评估需求。

Method: 扩展Moody's Analytics和Gauntlet的六维度分类法，新增三个维度（可组合性风险、理解债务、时间风险动态），并引入透明度置信度修正量；在基于本体论的协议智能基础设施上进行协议依赖关系结构分析；对12起重大DeFi事件进行回顾性分析。

Result: 12起事件中有5起至少需要一个新维度才能完成根本原因表征，其中包括数据集中两起系统性影响最高的事件，验证了新增维度的必要性。

Conclusion: 提出了一个包含9个维度的DeFi风险评估框架，通过基于本体论的协议智能基础设施对8000多个DeFi协议进行结构分析，并对2024-2026年12起重大DeFi安全事件（约25亿美元损失）进行回顾性验证，证明了新增维度的必要性和实用性。

Abstract: Decentralized finance (DeFi) protocols now intermediate over USD 100 billion in value, including regulated stablecoins and tokenized assets deployed as collateral, yet no widely adopted framework operationalizes risk assessment at the rigor institutional adoption demands. Existing approaches emphasize protocol-specific parameter optimization or conceptual taxonomies without providing explainable, composability-aware, and structurally independent assessment methodologies.
  We propose a nine-dimension DeFi risk assessment framework extending the six-dimension taxonomy introduced by Moody's Analytics and Gauntlet with three novel dimensions: composability risk, comprehension debt, and temporal risk dynamics. We additionally introduce a transparency confidence modifier separating assessment reliability from risk severity.
  The framework is grounded in structural analysis of protocol dependencies conducted through an ontology-based protocol intelligence infrastructure covering more than 8,000 DeFi protocols. We retrospectively analyze 12 major DeFi-related incidents from 2024-2026 representing approximately USD 2.5 billion in direct losses. Five of the 12 incidents require at least one novel dimension for complete root-cause characterization, including the two highest-systemic-impact events in the dataset.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [LCM: Lossless Context Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04050)
*Clint Ehrlich,Theodore Blackman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LCM（无损上下文管理），一种用于LLM记忆的确定性架构。通过将符号递归分解为引擎管理的递归上下文压缩和递归任务划分两个机制，LCM在保持无损检索的同时提供了终止保证。在Oolhong长上下文评估中，LCM增强的Volt智能体在32K到1M token的所有上下文长度上均超越Claude Code。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM记忆系统存在局限性，现有方法如RLM虽采用递归范式但缺乏确定性保证，且无法超越前沿编码智能体。需要在保持无损检索的同时解决终止保证和短任务效率问题。

Method: 将符号递归分解为两个确定性引擎管理机制：递归上下文压缩（层级摘要DAG自动压缩旧消息但保留所有原始消息的无损指针）和递归任务划分（引擎管理的并行原语如LLM-Map替代模型编写的循环）。

Result: LCM增强的编码智能体Volt在OOLONG长上下文评估中，在32K到1M token的每个上下文长度上均超越Claude Code，且递归上下文操作可超越具有原生文件系统访问权的前沿编码智能体。

Conclusion: LCM通过将符号递归分解为确定性引擎管理机制，在保持无损检索的同时牺牲了最大灵活性，换取了终止保证、短任务零成本连续性等优势，代表了一种从GOTO到结构化控制流的范式转变。

Abstract: We introduce Lossless Context Management (LCM), a deterministic architecture for LLM memory that outperforms Claude Code on long-context tasks. When benchmarked using Opus 4.6, our LCM-augmented coding agent, Volt, achieves higher scores than Claude Code on the OOLONG long-context eval, including at every context length between 32K and 1M tokens.
  LCM may be considered both a vindication and extension of the recursive paradigm pioneered by Recursive Language Models (RLMs). Our results demonstrate that recursive context manipulation can outperform not just conventional LLMs, but frontier coding agents with native file-system access.
  LCM departs from RLM by decomposing symbolic recursion into two deterministic, engine-managed mechanisms: recursive context compression, in which a hierarchical summary DAG automatically compacts older messages while retaining lossless pointers to every original; and recursive task partitioning, in which engine-managed parallel primitives like LLM-Map replace model-written loops. This trade-off, analogous to the move from GOTO to structured control flow in program-ming language design, sacrifices maximal flexibility for termination guarantees, zero-cost continuity on short tasks, and lossless retrievability of all prior state.

</details>


### [98] [Regularized Centered Emphatic Temporal Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04100)
*Xingguo Chen,Chaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Tianyu Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对离策略时序差分（TD）学习中的稳定性、投影几何与方差控制之间的权衡问题，提出了一种正则化情感时序差分学习（RETD）方法。该方法通过仅对辅助定心递归进行正则化来保持跟随迹，同时避免Naive中心化情感扩展中的耦合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离策略TD学习面临稳定性、投影几何和方差控制之间的结构性权衡。情感TD（ETD）通过跟随emphasis改善了投影几何，但跟随迹可能具有高方差。定心方法虽能消除TD误差的常见漂移项，但朴素的中心化扩展会引入辅助耦合，破坏ETD关键矩阵的正定性。

Method: 提出RETD方法，仅对辅助定心递归进行正则化，将耦合关键矩阵的右下块从1提升到1+c。推导了RETD核心矩阵，在保守的正则化条件下证明了收敛性，并在诊断性线性离策略预测任务上进行了评估。

Result: 实验表明RETD避免了朴素中心化情感学习的不稳定性，保持了有利的情感几何结构，并在诊断任务中展现出对正则化参数c的稳健中间区间。

Conclusion: RETD成功平衡了离策略TD学习中的权衡问题，通过选择性正则化实现了稳定性与投影几何的兼顾，为离策略强化学习提供了一种有效的算法框架。

Abstract: Off-policy temporal-difference (TD) learning with function approximation faces a structural tradeoff among stability, projection geometry, and variance control. Emphatic TD (ETD) improves the off-policy projection geometry through follow-on emphasis, but the follow-on trace can have high variance. We revisit this tradeoff through Bellman-error centering. Although centering naturally removes a common drift term from TD errors, we show that a naive centered emphatic extension introduces an auxiliary coupling that can destroy the positive-definiteness of the ETD key matrix. We propose \emph{Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning} (RETD), which preserves the follow-on trace and regularizes only the auxiliary centering recursion, corresponding to lifting the lower-right block of the coupled key matrix from \(1\) to \(1+c\). We derive the RETD core matrix, prove convergence under a conservative sufficient regularization condition, and evaluate the method on diagnostic linear off-policy prediction tasks. The experiments show that RETD avoids the instability of naive centered emphatic learning, preserves favorable emphatic geometry, and exhibits a robust intermediate regime for the regularization parameter \(c\) across the diagnostics.

</details>


### [99] [ANDRE: An Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04193)
*Iman Sharifi,Peng Wei,Saber Fallah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ANDRE是一个新型ILP框架，通过注意力机制驱动的逻辑运算符（替代传统的模糊运算符）来学习一阶逻辑规则。该方法在保持可解释性的同时，能够处理含噪和概率化数据，在多个基准测试上表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有符号化和神经符号化ILP方法在处理含噪和概率化场景时存在局限性：经典ILP依赖离散组合搜索，对不确定性敏感；可微分ILP方法通常依赖预定义规则模板或不准确的模糊运算符，导致梯度消失或逻辑结构近似不佳。

Method: 提出ANDRE框架，核心创新包括：(1) 使用注意力驱动的合取和析取运算符，逼近逻辑min-max语义；(2) 完全可微分的连续规则空间优化；(3) 支持在规则中软选择、否定或排除谓词，实现灵活规则归纳。

Result: 在经典ILP基准测试、大规模知识库和含概率谓词/标签噪声的合成数据集上，ANDRE达到了竞争性或更优的预测性能，能可靠地从不确定性中恢复正确符号规则，对中等标签噪声具有鲁棒性，显著优于现有可微分ILP方法。

Conclusion: 通过注意力机制替代传统逻辑运算符，ANDRE实现了准确、稳定且可解释的概率数据推理，为ILP在现实世界含噪场景中的应用提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) aims to learn interpretable first-order rules from data, but existing symbolic and neuro-symbolic approaches struggle to scale to noisy and probabilistic settings. Classical ILP relies on discrete combinatorial rule search and is brittle under uncertainty, while differentiable ILP methods typically depend on predefined rule templates or inaccurate fuzzy operators that suffer from vanishing gradients or poor approximation of logical structure when reasoning over probabilistic predicate valuations. This paper proposes an Attention-based Neuro-symbolic Differentiable Rule Extractor (ANDRE), a novel ILP framework that learns first-order logic programs by optimizing over a continuous rule space with attention-based logical operators. ANDRE replaces both rule templates and logical operators with fully differentiable, attention-driven conjunction and disjunction operators that approximate logical min-max semantics, enabling accurate, stable, and interpretable reasoning over probabilistic data. By softly selecting, negating, or excluding predicates within each rule, ANDRE supports flexible rule induction while preserving symbolic structure. Extensive experiments on classical ILP benchmarks, large-scale knowledge bases, and synthetic datasets with probabilistic predicates and noisy supervision demonstrate that ANDRE achieves competitive or superior predictive performance while reliably recovering correct symbolic rules under uncertainty. In particular, ANDRE remains robust to moderate label noise, substantially outperforming existing differentiable ILP methods in both rule extraction quality and stability.

</details>


### [100] [Pro$^2$Assist: Continuous Step-Aware Proactive Assistance with Multimodal Egocentric Perception for Long-Horizon Procedural Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04227)
*Lilin Xu,Bufang Yang,Siyang Jiang,Kaiwei Liu,Kaiyuan Hou,Yuang Fan,Hongkai Chen,Zhenyu Yan,Xiaofan Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pro²Assist是一个步态感知的主动式助手，通过AR眼镜的多模态数据持续跟踪用户任务进度，在长时程程序性任务中主动提供及时帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统主要提供被动式指导（响应用户查询）或局限于短时事件的有限主动帮助，缺乏对长时程多步骤程序性任务的持续性、主动性支持能力。

Method: 1) 利用AR眼镜的多模态数据实现基于运动的感知；2) 从多尺度时间动态和任务专家知识中提取步态导向的程序性上下文；3) 基于感官输入和程序性上下文进行连续推理以推断用户需求，并通过AR眼镜实时显示帮助信息。

Result: 在程序动作理解准确率上优于最佳基线超过21%；主动时机准确率最高达到基线的2.29倍。

Conclusion: 用户研究（20名参与者）表明90%认为Pro²Assist有用，验证了其在真实世界程序性任务帮助中的有效性。

Abstract: Procedural tasks with multiple ordered steps are ubiquitous in daily life. Recent advances in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have enabled personal assistants that support daily activities. However, existing systems primarily provide reactive guidance triggered by user queries, or limited proactive assistance for isolated short-term events rather than long-horizon procedural tasks. In this work, we introduce Pro$^2$Assist, a step-aware proactive assistant that continuously tracks fine-grained task progress and reasons over the user's evolving state to provide timely assistance throughout tasks. Pro$^2$Assist leverages multimodal data from augmented reality (AR) glasses to achieve motion-based perception. It then extracts step-oriented procedural context from multi-scale temporal dynamics and task-specific expert knowledge. Based on both sensory input and procedural context, Pro$^2$Assist performs continuous reasoning to infer user needs and display timely assistance on AR glasses. We evaluate Pro$^2$Assist using a dataset curated from public sources and a real-world dataset collected on our testbed with AR glasses. Extensive evaluations show that Pro$^2$Assist outperforms the best-performing baselines by over 21% in procedural action understanding accuracy, and it achieves up to 2.29x the proactive timing accuracy of baselines. A user study with 20 participants further shows that 90% find Pro$^2$Assist useful, indicating its effectiveness for real-world procedural assistance.

</details>


### [101] [Temporal Reasoning Is Not the Bottleneck: A Probabilistic Inconsistency Framework for Neuro-Symbolic QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04243)
*Tran Quang Liem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"LMs在时序推理中存在根本算法缺陷"的传统观点，提出失败根源在于文本到事件的表示层面，而非推理本身。通过引入基于概率不一致信号(PIS)的神经符号框架，将语义提取与符号推理严格解耦，在时序算术基准上达到100%准确率，在噪声QA任务上保持75.1%准确率并支持确定性失败定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流观点认为大语言模型在复杂时序推理任务中的失败源于自回归逻辑推理的固有缺陷。作者认为这一认知可能存在偏差，需要重新定位问题根源。

Method: 提出基于概率不一致信号(PIS)的神经符号问答框架：1) 将非结构化文本提升为显式事件图和区间约束；2) 利用证据深度学习从LLM隐藏状态中提取认知神经不确定性；3) 将符号信用区间与神经不确定性统一融合以检测结构断裂。

Result: 时序算术基准测试：4000/4000样本达到完美1.0准确率，零假阳性/假阴性。噪声注入QA任务：75.1%准确率，支持确定性、步骤级失败定位。

Conclusion: 时序问答的本质是结构对齐问题而非算法推理挑战。通过隔离表示瓶颈与推理基底，为可靠神经符号AI开辟了可验证的发展路径。

Abstract: Despite significant advances, large language models (LLMs) continue to exhibit brittle performance on complex temporal reasoning tasks. This failure mode is widely attributed to inherent deficits in autoregressive logical deduction. In this paper, we challenge this prevailing narrative, demonstrating that temporal reasoning is not the fundamental bottleneck; rather, the locus of failure lies in unstructured text-to-event representation. We introduce a novel neuro-symbolic question-answering framework governed by a Probabilistic Inconsistency Signal (PIS) that explicitly isolates perceptual errors from reasoning failures. By lifting unstructured text into explicit event graphs and interval constraints, our architecture strictly decouples semantic extraction from a symbolic reasoning engine. To robustly detect structural breaks, the PIS elegantly unifies symbolic credal intervals with epistemic neural uncertainty extracted via Evidential Deep Learning on LLM hidden states. Empirical evaluations reveal a striking paradigm shift: when provided with correct structural representations, our system's explicit proof traces achieve perfect 1.0 accuracy (4000/4000) and strictly zero false positives/negatives on temporal arithmetic benchmarks. On broader, noise-injected QA settings, the framework maintains a competitive 75.1\% accuracy while enabling deterministic, step-level failure localization. Ultimately, by isolating the representation bottleneck from the reasoning substrate, this work reframes temporal QA from an algorithmic reasoning challenge to a structural alignment problem, charting a verifiable path forward for reliable neuro-symbolic AI.

</details>


### [102] [Parallel Prefix Verification for Speculative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04263)
*Yuncheng Yao,Yuxuan Xia,Shengjie Wang,Danyang Zhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PARSE通过并行化语义级前缀验证来加速LLM推理，在单次前向传播中评估多个前缀的正确性，实现1.25×至4.5×的吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码方法受限于token级等价验证，导致接受长度短、加速有限。虽然语义级或段级验证可以显著提高接受粒度，但先前方法依赖顺序验证，引入显著开销，限制实际收益。

Method: PARSE引入并行前缀验证机制，使用自定义注意力掩码，使目标模型能够在单次前向传播中评估多个前缀的正确性，直接识别最大有效前缀，无需顺序段验证，计算高效。该方法与token级推测解码正交，可与EAGLE-3等方法组合使用。

Result: 在多个模型和基准测试上，PARSE相比目标模型实现1.25×至4.3×的吞吐量提升；与EAGLE-3组合使用时实现1.6×至4.5×的提升，精度损失可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 并行前缀验证是一种有效、通用的LLM推理加速方法，证明了从token级验证跃升至语义级并行验证的可行性。

Abstract: We introduce PARSE (PArallel pRefix Speculative Engine), a speculative generation framework that accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by parallelizing prefix verification on a semantic level. Existing speculative decoding methods are fundamentally limited by token-level equivalence: the target model must verify each token, leading to short acceptance lengths and modest speedups. Moving to semantic or segment-level verification can substantially increase acceptance granularity, but prior approaches rely on sequential verification, introducing significant overhead and limiting practical gains. PARSE introduces parallel prefix verification, enabling semantic-level verification without sequential checks. Given a full draft from a draft model, the target model evaluates correctness across multiple prefixes in a single forward pass using a custom attention mask, directly identifying the maximal valid prefix. This eliminates sequential segment verification, and makes verification compute-efficient. PARSE is orthogonal to token-level speculative decoding and can be composed with it for additional gains. Across models and benchmarks, PARSE delivers $1.25\times$ to $4.3\times$ throughput gain over the target model, and $1.6\times$ to $4.5\times$ when composed with EAGLE-3, all with negligible accuracy degradation. This demonstrates parallel prefix verification as an effective, general approach to accelerating LLM inference.

</details>


### [103] [Agent Island: A Saturation- and Contamination-Resistant Benchmark from Multiagent Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04312)
*Connacher Murphy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Static capabilities benchmarks suffer from saturation and contamination, making it difficult to track capabilities progress over time. We introduce Agent Island, a multiplayer simulation environment in which language-model agents compete in a game of interagent cooperation, conflict, and persuasion. The environment yields a dynamic benchmark designed to mitigate both saturation and contamination; new models can always outperform the current leading player in this winner-take-all game, and agents compete against other adaptive agents rather than face a fixed task set. We rank players with a Bayesian Plackett-Luce model, allowing us to quantify uncertainty in player skill. In 999 games involving 49 unique models, openai/gpt-5.5 dominates its peers with a posterior mean skill of 5.64, compared with 3.10 for the second-ranked model, openai/gpt-5.2, and 2.86 for the third-ranked model, openai/gpt-5.3-codex. We release the game logs as a dataset for analyses of model behavior. As an example, we investigate same-provider preference in final-round votes and find that models are 8.3 p.p. more likely to support a same-provider finalist than finalists from other providers. This preference is not uniform across providers: among separately estimated providers, the effect is strongest for OpenAI models and weakest for Anthropic models.

</details>


### [104] [The Scaling Properties of Implicit Deductive Reasoning in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04330)
*Enrico Vompa,Tanel Tammet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究深度受限Transformer中隐式推理与显式思维链(CoT)在Horn子句演绎推理上的扩展性质，发现通过去除虚假特征关联和算法对齐，深度模型中隐式推理可接近显式CoT性能，但深度外推仍需CoT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型中隐式推理（直接给出答案）vs 显式推理（链式思考）的机制尚不清晰，尤其在符号演绎任务中缺乏系统性分析，需要探究Transformer是否能通过隐式方式学习演绎算法的隐含表示。

Method: 采用控制变量法，系统性地解耦可证明性与虚假特征；通过算法对齐约束强化模型对Horn子句推理过程的隐式编码；测试不同图拓扑和宽度下的泛化性能。

Result: 当模型深度足够且使用双向前缀mask时，隐式推理在多种图结构和问题宽度上接近显式CoT的性能水平。

Conclusion: 深度外推（depth extrapolation）场景下，显式CoT仍是必要的；充分深的Transformer能够隐式捕获演绎算法的计算图，但这种隐式表示在超出训练分布的深度时失效。

Abstract: We investigate the scaling properties of implicit deductive reasoning over Horn clauses in depth-bounded Transformers. By systematically decorrelating provability from spurious features and enforcing algorithmic alignment, we find that in sufficiently deep models with a bidirectional prefix mask, implicit reasoning approaches explicit CoT performance across graph topologies and problem widths, though CoT remains necessary for depth extrapolation.

</details>


### [105] [When Context Hurts: The Crossover Effect of Knowledge Transfer on Multi-Agent Design Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04361)
*Saranyan Vigraham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在多智能体软件设计任务中，系统性验证了上下文注入的效果，发现"越多越好"的假设不成立。知识工件对不同任务产生截然相反的效果，其方向可由无上下文时的基线探索能力预测。这挑战了上下文增强的普遍性认知，建议将"零上下文试运行"作为诊断工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agent编排中普遍假设"上下文越多越好"，但这一假设尚未在多智能体软件设计中得到系统验证。

Method: 大规模实验：10个任务×7种上下文注入条件×2700+次运行，操控提示设计中的收敛压力以探测机制，并计算Pearson相关系数。

Result: 发现交叉效应：相同工件类型对某些任务提升设计探索高达20倍，但同时使其他任务退化达46%；无关文档表现可与相关文档相当或更优。基线探索（无上下文时的探索度）可预测效果方向，Pearson r=-0.82（p<0.001）。识别出两种收敛机制：训练数据先验驱动的收敛对工件干扰敏感，而显式指令驱动的收敛不敏感。

Conclusion: 上下文注入应是有条件的而非普遍的；在没有上下文基线探索中表现良好的任务（探索性好），知识工件会增强探索；而基线探索差的任务，知识工件反而会抑制探索。

Abstract: The prevailing assumption in agent orchestration is that more context is better. We test this on multi-agent software design across 10 tasks, 7 context-injection conditions, and over 2,700 runs, and find a crossover effect: the same artifact type improves design exploration on some tasks (up to 20$\times$ tradeoff coverage) and actively degrades it on others (up to 46% reduction). On several tasks, an irrelevant document performs as well as or better than every relevant artifact. The direction is predicted by a single measurable variable--baseline exploration without context--with Pearson $r = -0.82$ ($p < 0.001$). Probing the mechanism by manipulating convergence pressure through prompt design reveals two distinct regimes: convergence driven by training data priors (natural) responds to artifact disruption, while convergence driven by explicit instructions (induced) does not. The implication is that context injection should be conditional, not universal: one no-context trial is a cheap diagnostic that predicts whether knowledge artifacts will help or hurt a given task.

</details>


### [106] [Deployment-Relevant Alignment Cannot Be Inferred from Model-Level Evaluation Alone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04454)
*Varad Vishwarupe,Nigel Shadbolt,Marina Jirotka,Ivan Flechais*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文批判了当前机器学习对齐评估过度依赖模型级评测的问题，指出部署相关的对齐无法仅从模型级评估推断。作者通过对16个对齐基准的审计和跨3个前沿模型、4个脚手架的盲测实验，揭示了现有基准缺乏用户验证支持和过程可控性，且脚手架效果具有模型依赖性。最终提出系统级评估议程，包括对齐画像、固定脚手架协议和明确的推论距离报告模板。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐评估主要通过固定输入下的模型输出来评分，但这种方法无法支撑部署场景下的对齐声明。作者认为对齐声明应与证据收集的层级相匹配（模型级、响应级、交互级或部署级），现有方法存在根本性的层级错配问题。

Method: 研究一：对11个对齐基准进行结构化审计（扩展至16个），采用8维度评估量表进行双人编码（Cohen's κ = 0.87）。研究二：使用180个对话记录，对3个前沿模型和4种脚手架进行盲法跨模型压力测试。

Result: 审计发现所有基准都缺乏用户验证支持，过程可控性几乎不存在；少量交互基准（tau-bench、CURATe、Rifts、Common Ground）覆盖范围分散且受基准构建而非数据源驱动。压力测试显示相同验证脚手架对不同模型效果迥异（一个达到上限，另一个几乎不变），表明脚手架功效具有模型依赖性。

Conclusion: 提出系统级评估议程：用对齐画像替代单一分数；建立固定脚手架协议以实现可比的交互评估；制定报告模板明确评估证据与部署声明之间的推论距离。

Abstract: Alignment evaluation in machine learning has largely become evaluation of models. Influential benchmarks score model outputs under fixed inputs, such as truthfulness, instruction following, or pairwise preference, and these scores are often used to support claims about deployed alignment. This paper argues that deployment-relevant alignment cannot be inferred from model-level evaluation alone. Alignment claims should instead be indexed to the level at which evidence is collected: model-level, response-level, interaction-level, or deployment-level. Two studies support this position. First, a structured audit of eleven alignment benchmarks, extended to a sixteen-benchmark corpus, dual-coded against an eight-dimension rubric with Cohen's kappa = 0.87, finds that user-facing verification support is absent across every benchmark examined, while process steerability is nearly absent. The few interactional benchmarks identified, including tau-bench, CURATe, Rifts, and Common Ground, remain fragmented in coverage, and benchmark construction rather than data source determines what is measured. Second, a blinded cross-model stress test using 180 transcripts across three frontier models and four scaffolds finds that the same verification scaffold raises one model's verification support to ceiling while leaving another categorically unchanged. This shows that scaffold efficacy is model-dependent and that the gap identified by the audit cannot be closed at the model level alone. We propose a system-level evaluation agenda: alignment profiles instead of single scores, fixed-scaffolding protocols for comparable interactional evaluation, and reporting templates that make the inferential distance between evaluation evidence and deployment claims explicit.

</details>


### [107] [How Does Thinking Mode Change LLM Moral Judgments? A Controlled Instant-vs-Thinking Comparison Across Five Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04488)
*Sai Sourabh Madur*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了5个前沿LLM在即时与思考模式下的道德判断差异，发现聚合一致性无显著变化，但推理模式能显著缩小模型间在争议场景中的分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究主流LLM推理模式的启用是否会改变其道德判断结果，评估推理能力对模型伦理决策的影响。

Method: 采用100个道德判断场景，对比5个前沿推理训练LLM（Claude Sonnet 4.6、GPT 5.5、Gemini 3 Flash、DeepSeek V3.1、Qwen3.5 397B）在即时模式（instant mode）与思考模式（thinking mode）下的表现。使用Krippendorff's alpha衡量一致性。

Result: 聚合层面二元裁决一致性保持高位且统计无显著差异（alpha=0.78 vs 0.79）。分歧集中于21个"模型争议场景"，即时模式下近乎随机（alpha=0.08）；推理可将模型两两一致率从5.4/10提升至6.7/10。推理还减少了3/5模型的人口统计判断不一致性，且未增加任何模型的不一致性。

Conclusion: 尽管推理模式会改变部分伦理框架的自我标签，但在二元裁决层面影响有限。推理主要作用在于减少模型间的分歧集中度，特别是在争议性场景中提升一致性。研究提示：推理模式更多影响伦理推理过程而非最终裁决结论。

Abstract: We evaluate whether enabling provider-exposed reasoning mode changes moral judgments within the same model checkpoint. Across 100 moral-judgment scenarios and five frontier reasoning-trained LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4.6, GPT 5.5, Gemini 3 Flash, DeepSeek V3.1, and Qwen3.5 397B), aggregate binary-verdict agreement remains high and statistically indistinguishable between instant and thinking modes (Krippendorff's alpha = 0.78 vs. 0.79). However, disagreement is concentrated in 21 model-disputed scenarios, where instant-mode agreement is near chance (alpha = 0.08). On these scenarios, reasoning directionally narrows cross-model disagreement, increasing mean pairwise agreement from 5.4 to 6.7 out of 10. Reasoning also reduces demographic-judgment inconsistency in three of five models and does not increase it for any model. Across all five model families, reasoning changes self-labeled ethical frameworks more often than binary verdicts.

</details>


### [108] [From Parameter Dynamics to Risk Scoring : Quantifying Sample-Level Safety Degradation in LLM Fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04572)
*Xiao Wang,Yifei Zhang,YongKang Liu,Xiaocui Yang,Zihan Wang,Shi Feng,Daling Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了大语言模型（LLM）安全对齐微调过程中的脆弱性机制：良性微调会导致参数累积向危险对齐方向漂移，逐步削弱模型安全性。基于此发现，提出SQSD方法，通过测量参数更新在危险与安全方向上的投影差异来量化每个训练样本的安全降解风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的安全对齐极易被破坏——仅在少量良性样本上微调就可能抹去从数百万偏好示例中学到的安全行为。现有研究仅通过对比微调前后的参数和隐藏状态来解释此现象，忽视了微调过程中的动态演化机制。

Method: 提出样本级安全降解量化方法（SQSD）。该方法通过测量每个训练样本诱导的参数更新在危险方向与安全方向上的投影差异，计算连续风险评分，以量化各样本对安全降解的影响程度。

Result: 实验涵盖多个模型和数据集，验证了SQSD能有效量化样本级微调风险，并在模型架构、参数规模和参数高效微调方法上表现出强迁移性。

Conclusion: 安全降解的关键在于参数的累积漂移机制。通过分析参数动态演化可识别高风险样本，为安全微调提供了新的理论洞察和实践工具。

Abstract: Safety alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) is extremely fragile, as fine-tuning on a small number of benign samples can erase safety behaviors learned from millions of preference examples. Existing studies attempt to explain this phenomenon by comparing parameters and hidden states before and after fine-tuning, but overlook their dynamic evolution during fine-tuning. In this paper, we uncover a critical mechanism underlying safety degradation by analyzing parameter dynamics, where benign fine-tuning causes parameters to cumulatively drift toward danger-aligned directions, progressively undermining the model's safety. This finding suggests that samples contributing more to this drift has greater fine-tuning risks. Based on this insight, we propose a method of Sample-Level Quantification of Safety Degradation (SQSD), which quantifies the influence of each training sample on safety degradation. Specifically, SQSD computes continuous risk scores to samples by measuring their induced parameter updates' projection difference between danger and safety directions. Extensive experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate that SQSD effectively quantifies sample-level fine-tuning risks and exhibits strong transferability across model architectures, parameter scales, and parameter-efficient methods.

</details>


### [109] [SensingAgents: A Multi-Agent Collaborative Framework for Robust IMU Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04608)
*Naiyu Zheng,Tianlong Yu,Haochen Yin,Xiaoyi Fan,Xiping Hu,Zhimeng Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SensingAgents 是一个用于 IMU 人体活动识别的多智能体 LLM 系统，通过分析师、倡导者和决策智能体的协作，在零样本设置下达到 79.5% 准确率，显著优于现有单智能体和多智能体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的人体活动识别（HAR）模型面临三大挑战：过度依赖标注数据、位置特定模糊性以及缺乏透明推理能力。

Method: 提出 SensingAgents 多智能体系统，将 LLM 驱动的智能体分为三类角色：多位置专用分析师智能体（分析手臂、手腕、腰带、口袋等传感器的位置特定数据）、一对倡导者智能体（通过动态和静态辩证辩论解决传感器冲突）、以及决策智能体（确保传感器漂移或故障时的可靠性）。在 Shoaib 数据集上评估，在零样本设置下达到 79.5% 准确率，比现有智能体模型提升 29%，比深度学习基线提升 9.4%，尤其在多传感器数据冲突或噪声复杂的场景中表现优异。

Result: 79.5% 零样本准确率；比现有智能体模型提升 29%；比深度学习基线提升 9.4%；在冲突/噪声多传感器场景中表现优异。

Conclusion: 本研究突出了多智能体协作推理在提升普适感知系统鲁棒性和可解释性方面的潜力，为移动健康、智能环境和人机交互等领域提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) sensors is a cornerstone of mobile health, smart environments, and human-computer interaction. However, current deep learning-based HAR models often struggle with heavy reliance on labeled data, position-specific ambiguity, and a lack of transparent reasoning. Inspired by the advanced agents framework, which emulates a collaborative agent using Large Language Models (LLMs), we propose SensingAgents, a novel multi-agent system for robust IMU activity recognition. SensingAgents organizes LLM-powered agents into specialized roles: a group of Analyst Agents for position-specific sensor analysis (arm, wrist, belt, pocket), a pair of Advocate Agents that resolves sensor conflicts through dynamic and static dialectical debates, and a Decision Agent that ensures reliability under sensor drift or failure. Evaluation on the Shoaib dataset demonstrates that SensingAgents significantly outperforms state-of-the-art single-agent and multi-agent LLM models, achieving an accuracy of 79.5% in a zero setting--29% higher than existing agent models and 9.4% higher than deep learning baselines--particularly in complex scenarios where multi-sensor data is conflicting or noisy. Our work highlights the potential of multi-agent collaborative reasoning for advancing the robustness and interpretability of ubiquitous sensing systems.

</details>


### [110] [Reward-Decomposed Reinforcement Learning for Immersive Video Role-Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04733)
*Miao Wang,Yuling Shi,Yijiang Li,Yeheng Chen,Xiaodong Gu,Bin Li,Bo Gao,Yaduan Ruan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EBM-RL框架，通过将视觉语言模型的响应解耦为感知、推理和回答三个阶段，结合四种互补奖励信号，提升视频角色扮演对话中的场景氛围一致性和角色真实性，并在VideoQA基准上展现零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本角色扮演模型无法有效反映场景氛围和动态张力，难以满足VR游戏和交互式叙事等沉浸式应用需求。现有视觉语言模型在视频角色扮演场景中缺乏对视觉线索的系统性处理和情境适应性对话生成能力。

Method: 提出EBM-RL（Eye-Brain-Mouth强化学习）框架，基于GRPO算法将模型响应解耦为三个阶段：[perception]观察视觉线索、[think]形成内部解释、[answer]生成上下文适配对话。集成四种互补奖励：①CLIP场景-文本对齐奖励改善氛围和情感；②感知-认知奖励促进推理过程提升参考答案似然；③回答准确率奖励确保忠实度；④格式奖励强制结构化输出。

Result: EBM-RL在沉浸式角色扮演基准上显著优于纯文本角色扮演基线和更大规模的视觉语言模型，同时提升视觉氛围一致性和角色真实性。此外，在未进行额外微调的情况下，EBM-RL在域外VideoQA基准上也展现稳定的零样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: EBM-RL通过解耦架构和多样化奖励设计，有效解决了视频角色扮演中的氛围感知和动态张力建模问题，其零样本泛化能力证明了该方法的通用性。团队同时发布了视频角色扮演对话的开源数据集。

Abstract: Text-based role-playing models can imitate character styles, yet they often fail to reflect a scene's atmosphere and evolving tension, both essential for immersive applications such as Virtual Reality (VR) games and interactive narratives. We study video-grounded role-playing dialogue and introduce EBM-RL (Eye-Brain-Mouth Reinforcement Learning), a decoupled GRPO-based framework that explicitly separates observation ([perception]), reasoning ([think]), and utterance ([answer]). This structure promotes human-like sensory grounding by compelling the model to first attend to visual cues, then form internal interpretations, and finally generate context-appropriate dialogue.
  EBM-RL integrates four complementary rewards: (i) CLIP-based scene-text alignment to improve ambiance and emotion; (ii) a Perceptual-Cognitive reward that encourages [perception] and [think] processes that increase the likelihood of the reference response; (iii) answer accuracy to ensure faithfulness; and (iv) a dense format reward to enforce the desired structured output.
  Extensive experiments demonstrate that EBM-RL substantially outperforms text-only role-playing baselines and larger-scale vision-language models on our immersive role-playing benchmark, delivering simultaneous gains in visual-atmosphere consistency and character authenticity. Beyond the role-playing domain, EBM-RL also exhibits strong zero-shot generalization: without any additional fine-tuning, it consistently improves performance on out-of-domain VideoQA benchmarks. We additionally release an open-source dataset for video-grounded role-playing dialogue.

</details>


### [111] [DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap): A Controllable and Interactive Red-Teaming Platform for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04808)
*Zhaorun Chen,Xun Liu,Haibo Tong,Chengquan Guo,Yuzhou Nie,Jiawei Zhang,Mintong Kang,Chejian Xu,Qichang Liu,Xiaogeng Liu,Tianneng Shi,Chaowei Xiao,Sanmi Koyejo,Percy Liang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DTap是首个可控的交互式红队测试平台，用于评估AI智能体安全风险，涵盖14个现实领域和50+仿真环境，并提出自主红队智能体DTap-Red自动发现攻击策略，揭示了主流AI智能体的系统性漏洞模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体因高能力和灵活性被广泛应用于复杂工作流自动化，但面临严重安全风险。攻击者可轻易操纵智能体执行泄露API密钥、删除用户数据或发起未授权交易等有害操作。然而，用于大规模风险评估的真实、可控、可复现的测试环境仍严重不足。

Method: 提出DTap平台（14个领域、50+仿真环境，模拟Google Workspace、Paypal、Slack等真实系统）和DTap-Red自主红队智能体（系统性探索提示注入、工具注入、技能注入、环境注入等多种攻击向量，自动发现针对不同恶意目标的攻击策略），生成DTap-Bench大规模红队数据集（含可验证法官自动评估攻击结果）。

Result: 对基于多种骨干模型的主流AI智能体进行大规模评估，涵盖安全策略、风险类别和攻击策略，揭示了系统性的漏洞模式。

Conclusion: DTap为评估AI智能体安全风险提供了标准化、可扩展的测试框架，揭示的漏洞模式为开发更安全的下一代智能体提供了重要参考。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly deployed across diverse domains to automate complex workflows through long-horizon and high-stakes action executions. Due to their high capability and flexibility, such agents raise significant security and safety concerns. A growing number of real-world incidents have shown that adversaries can easily manipulate agents into performing harmful actions, such as leaking API keys, deleting user data, or initiating unauthorized transactions. Evaluating agent security is inherently challenging, as agents operate in dynamic, untrusted environments involving external tools, heterogeneous data sources, and frequent user interactions. However, realistic, controllable, and reproducible environments for large-scale risk assessment remain largely underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce the DecodingTrust-Agent Platform (DTap), the first controllable and interactive red-teaming platform for AI agents, spanning 14 real-world domains and over 50 simulation environments that replicate widely used systems such as Google Workspace, Paypal, and Slack. To scale the risk assessment of agents in DTap, we further propose DTap-Red, the first autonomous red-teaming agent that systematically explores diverse injection vectors (e.g., prompt, tool, skill, environment, combinations) and autonomously discovers effective attack strategies tailored to varying malicious goals. Using DTap-Red, we curate DTap-Bench, a large-scale red-teaming dataset comprising high-quality instances across domains, each paired with a verifiable judge to automatically validate attack outcomes. Through DTap, we conduct large-scale evaluations of popular AI agents built on various backbone models, spanning security policies, risk categories, and attack strategies, revealing systematic vulnerability patterns and providing valuable insights for developing secure next-generation agents.

</details>


### [112] [Strat-Reasoner: Reinforcing Strategic Reasoning of LLMs in Multi-Agent Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04906)
*Yidong He,Yutao Lai,Pengxu Yang,Jiarui Gan,Jiexin Wang,Yi Cai,Mengchen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Strat-Reasoner框架，通过递归推理范式和链式思维对比模块，提升大语言模型在多智能体游戏中的战略推理能力，采用群体相对强化学习方法优化策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在多智能体博弈游戏中面临挑战：(1) 其他智能体的非平稳性使得推理过程评估和信用分配困难；(2) 现有单智能体强化学习方法及其多智能体扩展未将其他智能体纳入推理过程，导致策略优化效果受限。

Method: (1) 提出递归推理范式，将其他智能体的推理过程整合到当前智能体的推理中；(2) 采用集中式链式思维(CoT)对比模块评估中间推理序列的质量；(3) 计算精确的混合优势并开发群体相对强化学习方法优化LLM策略。

Result: Strat-Reasoner在各类多智能体游戏中显著提升了大语言模型的战略能力，平均性能提升22.1%。

Conclusion: 通过整合其他智能体推理过程并结合集中式评估机制，Strat-Reasoner能有效提升LLM在多智能体博弈中的战略推理表现，为复杂多智能体交互场景提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) excel in certain reasoning tasks, they struggle in multi-agent games where the final outcome depends on the joint strategies of all agents. In multi-agent games, the non-stationarity of other agents brings significant challenges on the evaluation of the reasoning process and the credit assignment over multiple reasoning steps. Existing single-agent reinforcement learning (RL) approaches and their multi-agent extensions fail to address these challenges as they do not incorporate other agents in the reasoning process. In this work, we propose Strat-Reasoner, a novel RL-based framework that improves LLMs' strategic reasoning ability in multi-agent games. We introduce a novel recursive reasoning paradigm where an agent's reasoning also integrates other agents' reasoning processes. To provide effective reward signals for the intermediate reasoning sequences, we employ a centralized Chain-of-Thought (CoT) comparison module to evaluate the reasoning quality. Finally, we compute an accurate hybrid advantage and develop a group-relative RL approach to optimize the LLM policy. Experimental results show that Strat-Reasoner substantially improves strategic abilities of underlying LLMs, achieving 22.1\% average performance improvements across various multi-agent games.

</details>


### [113] [A Foundation Model for Zero-Shot Logical Rule Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04916)
*Yin Jun Phua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NRI是一种用于零样本规则归纳的预训练模型，与传统归纳逻辑编程(ILP)方法的转导性不同，NRI使用领域无关的统计属性（如类别条件比率、熵、共现）来表示文字，实现跨变量身份和计数的泛化，无需重训练。通过乘积T范数松弛使规则执行可微分，支持端到端训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统归纳逻辑编程(ILP)方法是转导性的：所学参数绑定于特定谓词，每个新任务都需要重新训练。本文旨在解决这一局限性，探索类似基础模型的符号推理方法。

Method: NRI由统计编码器和基于并行槽位的解码器组成。并行解码保持逻辑析取的置换不变性；乘积T范数松弛使规则执行可微分，支持端到端仅依据预测精度训练。

Result: 在规则恢复、标签噪声和虚假相关性的鲁棒性、以及迁移到真实世界基准数据集的零样本能力等方面进行了评估。

Conclusion: 该工作为符号推理的基础模型开辟了可能性。

Abstract: Inductive Logic Programming (ILP) learns interpretable logical rules from data.
