Existing methods are transductive: their learned parameters are bound to specific predicates and require retraining for each new task. We introduce Neural Rule Inducer (NRI), a pretrained model for zero-shot rule induction. Rather than encoding literal identities, NRI represents literals using domain-agnostic statistical properties such as class-conditional rates, entropy, and co-occurrence, which generalize across variable identities and counts without retraining. The model consists of a statistical encoder and a parallel slot-based decoder. Parallel decoding preserves the permutation invariance of logical disjunction; an autoregressive decoder would instead impose an arbitrary clause order. Product T-norm relaxation makes rule execution differentiable, allowing end-to-end training on prediction accuracy alone. We evaluate NRI on rule recovery, robustness to label noise and spurious correlations, and zero-shot transfer to real-world benchmarks, and we believe this work opens up the possibility of foundation models for symbolic reasoning. Code and the reference checkpoint are available at https://github.com/phuayj/neural-rule-inducer.

</details>


### [114] [On-line Learning in Tree MDPs by Treating Policies as Bandit Arms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04979)
*Anvay Shah,Ramsundar Anandanarayanan,Sharayu Moharir,Shivaram Kalyanakrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究树状马尔可夫决策过程（T-MDPs）中的在线学习问题，应用于序贯博弈中对抗静态对手的决策抽象。核心贡献是将 bandit 算法（Lucb 和 Ucb）应用于 T-MDPs，通过设计基于共享数据的置信界，实现多项式时间和空间复杂度，并获得以终端状态间隔项（gap term）求和为上界的样本复杂度和遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: T-MDPs 作为序贯博弈（完美记忆）对抗静态对手的天然抽象，其在线学习问题缺乏高效算法支持。现有 bandit 方法应用于策略空间时面临维度灾难（策略数量指数级），需要新技术突破。

Method: 将每个策略视为一个臂，应用 Lucb 和 Ucb 算法；关键创新：设计基于策略间共享数据的置信界，实现多项式内存和每步计算。

Result: 获得实例依赖的上界，样本复杂度和遗憾以各终端状态的 gap 项求和表示（非指数级策略数求和）。

Conclusion: 提出的算法在一系列隐藏信息博弈套件上经验性地优于现有替代方案，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: A Tree Markov Decision Problem (T-MDP) is a finite-horizon MDP with a starting state $s_{1}$, in which every state is reachable from $s_{1}$ through exactly one state-action trajectory. T-MDPs arise naturally as abstractions of decision making in sequential games with perfect recall, against stationary opponents. We consider the problem of on-line learning in T-MDPs, both in the PAC and the regret-minimisation regimes. We show that well-known bandit algorithms -- \textsc{Lucb} and \textsc{Ucb} -- can be applied on T-MDPs by treating each policy as an arm. The apparent technical challenge in this approach is that the number of policies is exponential in the number of states. Our main innovation is in the design of confidence bounds based on data shared by the policies, so that the bandit algorithms can yet be implemented with polynomial memory and per-step computation. We obtain instance-dependent upper bounds on sample complexity and regret that sum a ``gap term'' from every terminal state, rather than every policy. Empirically, our algorithms consistently outperform available alternatives on a suite of hidden-information games.

</details>


### [115] [Uno-Orchestra: Parsimonious Agent Routing via Selective Delegation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05007)
*Zhiqing Cui,Haotong Xie,Jiahao Yuan,Cheng Yang,Hanqing Wang,Yuxin Wu,Yifan Wu,Siru Zhong,Tao Yu,Yifu Guo,Siyu Zhang,Xinlei Yu,Qibing Ren,Usman Naseem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Uno-Orchestra，一种统一的多智能体LLM编排策略，通过强化学习从真实工作流交互中联合学习任务分解深度、工作器选择和推理预算分配，在13个基准测试上达到77.0% macro pass@1，准确率比最强工作流基线高约16%，同时成本降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统依赖刚性编排，仅支持扁平化路由或手工设计的任务分解，无法联合优化分解深度、工作器选择和推理预算分配。

Method: 提出Uno-Orchestra统一编排策略：(1)选择性任务分解；(2)将子任务分配到适配的(model, primitive)配对；(3)通过基于真实工作器交互的RL轨迹联合学习分解决策与分配决策。

Result: 在13基准测试套件（涵盖数学、代码、知识、长上下文、工具调用）上对比22个基线，Uno-Orchestra达到77.0% macro pass@1，比最强工作流基线高约16%，且每查询成本降低约一个数量级。

Conclusion: Uno-Orchestra推进了选择性委托的精度-效率前沿，实现了任务分解深度、工作器选择与推理预算的联合优化。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) multi-agent systems typically rely on rigid orchestration, committing either to flat per-query routing or to hand-engineered task decomposition, so decomposition depth, worker choice, and inference budget are not jointly optimized under one objective. We introduce Uno-Orchestra, a unified orchestration policy that selectively decomposes a task and dispatches each subtask to an admissible (model, primitive) pair, with both decisions learned together from curated RL trajectories grounded in real worker interactions. Against 22 baselines on a 13-benchmark suite spanning math, code, knowledge, long-context, and agentic tool-use, Uno-Orchestra reaches 77.0% macro pass@1, roughly 16% above the strongest workflow baseline, at roughly an order of magnitude lower per-query cost, advancing the accuracy-efficiency frontier of selective delegation.

</details>


### [116] [Position: Embodied AI Requires a Privacy-Utility Trade-off](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05017)
*Xiaoliang Fan,Jiarui Chen,Zhuodong Liu,Ziqi Yang,Peixuan Xu,Ruimin Shen,Junhui Liu,Jianzhong Qi,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是关于嵌入式AI（EAI）系统中隐私保护的立场论文。论文指出当前EAI解决方案在各阶段（指令、感知、规划、交互）独立优化，缺乏对隐私泄露的全局考虑，在敏感环境中部署时会造成系统性隐私危机。为此，论文提出SPINE框架，将隐私作为跨阶段耦合的动态控制信号，通过多准则隐私分类矩阵协调阶段边界敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前EAI系统从模拟环境向真实世界家庭及敏感环境快速过渡，但现有方案在各阶段独立优化，未考虑高频部署下的隐私耦合风险。论文认为隐私应被视为生命周期级的架构约束，而非局部特征，以避免碎片化隐私补丁的不足。

Method: 提出SPINE框架：1）将EAI流程分解为多个阶段；2）建立多准则隐私分类矩阵，跨阶段协调上下文敏感性；3）将隐私作为动态控制信号贯穿整个EAI生命周期。

Result: 通过仿真和真实案例研究初步验证了隐私约束如何向下游传播并重塑系统行为，概念验证了碎片化隐私方案的不足。

Conclusion: 隐私保护需要从局部特征提升为系统级架构约束；SPINE框架为未来安全且功能性的嵌入式AI系统研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Embodied AI (EAI) systems are rapidly transitioning from simulations into real-world domestic and other sensitive environments. However, recent EAI solutions have largely demonstrated advancements within isolated stages such as instruction, perception, planning and interaction, without considering their coupled privacy implications in high-frequency deployments where privacy leakage is often irreversible. This position paper argues that optimizing these components independently creates a systemic privacy crisis when deployed in sensitive settings, thereby advancing the position that privacy in EAI is a life cycle-level architectural constraint rather than a stage-local feature. To address these challenges, we propose Secure Privacy Integration in Next-generation Embodied AI (SPINE), a unified privacy-aware framework that treats privacy as a dynamic control signal governing cross-stage coupling throughout the entire EAI life cycle. SPINE decomposes the EAI pipeline into various stages and establishes a multi-criterion privacy classification matrix to orchestrate contextual sensitivity across stage boundaries. We conduct preliminary simulation and real-world case studies to conceptually validate how privacy constraints propagate downstream to reshape system behavior, illustrating the insufficiency of fragmented privacy patches and motivating future research directions into secure yet functional embodied AI systems. We detail the SPINE framework and case studies at https://github.com/rminshen03/EAI_Privacy_Position.

</details>


### [117] [LongSeeker: Elastic Context Orchestration for Long-Horizon Search Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05191)
*Yijun Lu,Rui Ye,Yuwen Du,Jiajun Wang,Songhua Liu,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对长程搜索智能体的上下文膨胀问题，提出自适应上下文管理原则，设计了包含5个原子操作的Context-ReAct范式，并基于此微调得到LongSeeker智能体，在多项搜索基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程搜索智能体面临上下文快速膨胀的挑战，朴素地积累所有中间内容会导致成本激增和错误风险增加。

Method: 提出Context-ReAct范式，集成推理、上下文管理和工具调用；设计了5个原子操作（Skip、Compress、Rollback、Snippet、Delete）实现弹性上下文编排；基于该范式微调得到LongSeeker（基于Qwen3-30B-A3B，在10k合成轨迹上训练）。

Result: LongSeeker在BrowseComp上达到61.5%、BrowseComp-ZH上达到62.5%，显著超越Tongyi DeepResearch（43.2%、46.7%）和AgentFold（36.2%、47.3%）。

Conclusion: LongSeeker在四个搜索基准上显著优于对比方法（如Tongyi DeepResearch和AgentFold），证明了自适应上下文管理能让智能体在长程推理中实现更可靠、更高效的搜索。

Abstract: Long-horizon search agents must manage a rapidly growing working context as they reason, call tools, and observe information. Naively accumulating all intermediate content can overwhelm the agent, increasing costs and the risk of errors. We propose that effective context management should be adaptive: parts of the agent's trajectory are maintained at different levels of detail depending on their current relevance to the task. To operationalize this principle, we introduce Context-ReAct, a general agentic paradigm for elastic context orchestration that integrates reasoning, context management, and tool use in a unified loop. Context-ReAct provides five atomic operations: Skip, Compress, Rollback, Snippet and Delete, which allow the agent to dynamically reshape its working context, preserving important evidence, summarizing resolved information, discarding unhelpful branches, and controlling context size. We prove that the Compress operator is expressively complete, while the other specialized operators provide efficiency and fidelity guarantees that reduce generation cost and hallucination risk. Building on this paradigm, we develop LongSeeker, a long-horizon search agent fine-tuned from Qwen3-30B-A3B on 10k synthesized trajectories. Across four representative search benchmarks, LongSeeker achieves 61.5% on BrowseComp and 62.5% on BrowseComp-ZH, substantially outperforming Tongyi DeepResearch (43.2% and 46.7%) and AgentFold (36.2% and 47.3%). These results highlight the potential of adaptive context management, showing that agents can achieve more reliable and efficient long-horizon reasoning by actively shaping their working memory.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [118] [Endogenous Regime Switching Driven by Scalar-Irreducible Learning Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04054)
*Sheng Ran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了标量可约与不可约动力学的分类框架，指出大多数现有ML系统属于前者，而后者可通过快变量与慢结构的反馈机制实现无需外部干预的内生性制度转换，为构建真正自主学习的智能系统提供了新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习框架难以实现内生性制度切换，制度转换通常依赖外部施加而非内部生成，限制了真正自主智能的涌现。

Method: 引入标量可约/不可约动力学的分类体系，使用最小动力学模型展示快变量与慢结构适应之间的反馈机制如何产生内生性制度转换。

Result: 证明标量不可约动力学能够通过快变量与慢结构适应之间的反馈，在无外部调度的情况下产生持续的内生性制度转换。

Conclusion: 提出了一种新的动力学范式，为实现内部组织而非外部预设的自适应行为的自主学习系统提供了潜在路径。

Abstract: Achieving endogenous regime switching is crucial for the emergence of autonomous intelligence, yet remains a central challenge for existing machine learning frameworks, where such transitions are typically externally imposed. In this work, we introduce a classification that distinguishes scalar-reducible dynamics, which can be expressed as gradient flows driven by a scalar objective, from scalar-irreducible dynamics that cannot be reduced to such a form. While most existing machine learning systems operate within the scalar-reducible class, we demonstrate that scalar-irreducible dynamics naturally enable internally generated regime switching through feedback between fast dynamical variables and slow structural adaptation. Using a minimal dynamical model, we illustrate how this mechanism produces sustained endogenous regime transitions without external scheduling. Our results suggest a new dynamical paradigm for regime exploration and provide a potential route toward autonomous learning systems whose adaptive behavior is organized internally rather than externally prescribed.

</details>


### [119] [A Self-Attentive Meta-Optimizer with Group-Adaptive Learning Rates and Weight Decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04055)
*JiangBo Zhao,ZhaoXin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MetaAdamW uses a Transformer-based attention mechanism to dynamically adapt learning rates and weight decay per parameter group, achieving better performance and efficiency than AdamW across diverse tasks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Adaptive optimizers like AdamW apply uniform hyperparameters across all parameter groups, ignoring heterogeneous optimization dynamics across layers and modules, leading to suboptimal convergence.

Method: Integrate lightweight Transformer encoder that operates on statistical features (gradient norms, momentum norms, correlations) from parameter groups to dynamically modulate per-group learning rates and weight decay; use meta-learning objective combining gradient alignment, loss decrease, and generalization gap; extend homoscedastic uncertainty weighting with task-specific priorities.

Result: MetaAdamW consistently outperforms standard AdamW baseline in validation loss, accuracy, or perplexity, reducing training time by up to 17.11% or improving performance by up to 11.08%; ablation studies validate component effectiveness.

Conclusion: MetaAdamW outperforms AdamW across multiple domains (time series forecasting, language modeling, machine translation, image classification, sentiment analysis), reducing training time by up to 17.11% or improving performance by up to 11.08%, with acceptable computational overhead.

Abstract: Adaptive optimizers like AdamW apply uniform hyperparameters across all parameter groups, ignoring heterogeneous optimization dynamics across layers and modules. We address this limitation by proposing MetaAdamW - a new optimizer that integrates a self-attention mechanism to dynamically modulate per-group learning rates and weight decay. The modulation factors are produced by a lightweight Transformer encoder that operates on statistical features (gradient norms, momentum norms, correlations) extracted from each parameter group. To train the attention module, we introduce a meta-learning objective that combines gradient alignment, loss decrease, and generalization gap. A key novel contribution is the extension of homoscedastic uncertainty weighting (HUW) with task-specific priorities that directly scale the regularization terms - enabling domain knowledge to guide automatic loss balancing. Extensive experiments on five diverse tasks-time series forecasting (ETT), language modeling (WikiText-2), machine translation (Multi30k), image classification (CIFAR-10), and sentiment analysis (IMDB) - demonstrate that MetaAdamW consistently outperforms the standard AdamW baseline in terms of validation loss, accuracy, or perplexity. Depending on the task, MetaAdamW either reduces overall training time (by up to 17.11%) or improves performance (by up to 11.08%) while introducing only moderate overhead; in some cases, it can also mitigate issues of insufficient convergence caused by premature early stopping. Ablation studies validate the effectiveness of each component, including feature versions, grouping strategies, and the proposed priority-injected uncertainty weighting.

</details>


### [120] [Transformation Categorization Based on Group Decomposition Theory Using Parameter Division](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04056)
*Takayuki Komatsu,Yoshiyuki Ohmura,Yasuo Kuniyoshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Representation learning seeks meaningful sensory representations without supervision and can model aspects of human development. Although many neural networks empirically learn useful features, a principled account of what makes a representation "good" remains elusive. We study unsupervised categorization of transformations between pairs of inputs under algebraic constraints. Classical disentanglement favors mutually independent factors and fails when factors are coupled. Our prior Galois-theoretic approach decomposes a group via normal subgroups by learning a product of two transformations with one factor constrained to a normal subgroup, covering both commutative and non-commutative cases. That method, however, relied on auxiliary assumptions (e.g., motion and isometry restrictions) not required by decomposition theory, and ablations did not separate theory-based from auxiliary effects. We propose parameter division for a single transformation: we split its parameter into components, impose homomorphism constraints mapping the full transformation to one component, and identify the normal subgroup as the set of transformations when that component is fixed to the identity. This formulation drops the previous auxiliary assumptions and applies more broadly. We evaluate on image pairs involving rotation, translation, and scale; ablations show that group-decomposition constraints drive appropriate categorization.

</details>


### [121] [LUCAS-MEGA: A Large-Scale Multimodal Dataset for Representation Learning in Soil-Environment Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04323)
*Kuangdai Leng,Simon Jeffery,Panos Panagos,Tarje Nissen-Meyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LUCAS-MEGA是一个大规模多模态土壤数据集，融合68个数据源、超70,000样本和1,000+特征，通过SoilFuser多智能体融合 pipeline实现标准化和统一，再通过SoilFormer自监督Transformer进行预训练，实现了stable training、strong predictive performance和uncertainty-aware predictions，并可复现established soil processes。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Soil science is hindered by fragmented and heterogeneous datasets, limiting modeling to small-scale predictions rather than large-scale representation learning.

Method: SoilFuser: a multi-agent, human-in-the-loop pipeline that standardizes heterogeneous formats, resolves unit/codebook inconsistencies and erroneous values, incorporates NLP annotations, and harmonizes multimodal attributes into a unified ML-ready feature space. SoilFormer: a multimodal tabular transformer pretrained via self-supervised feature masking.

Result: Stable training, strong predictive performance, and uncertainty-aware representations that recover relationships consistent with established soil processes.

Conclusion: LUCAS-MEGA is released as an open-access resource with agent-friendly APIs for structured querying, enabling scalable, data-driven soil modeling workflows.

Abstract: Understanding soil is fundamental to agriculture, carbon cycling, and environmental sustainability, yet progress is limited by fragmented and heterogeneous datasets that constrain modeling to small-scale predictive settings rather than high-dimensional representation learning. We introduce LUCAS-MEGA, a large-scale multimodal dataset constructed through systematic data fusion of European soil-environment observations, with the LUCAS survey as its backbone. The fused dataset comprises over 70,000 samples and more than 1,000 features spanning physical, chemical, environmental, biological, and visual attributes, aggregated from 68 source datasets. To enable integration at scale, we develop SoilFuser, a multi-agent, human-in-the-loop data fusion pipeline that standardizes heterogeneous data formats and measurement protocols, resolves inconsistencies and invalid entries (e.g., unit inconsistencies, codebook mismatches, and erroneous values), incorporates natural language annotations, and harmonizes multimodal attributes and metadata into a unified, machine learning-ready feature space. The resulting dataset captures key characteristics of real-world soil observations, including multimodality, uneven feature coverage, and heterogeneous uncertainty. To demonstrate the usability of LUCAS-MEGA for data-driven modeling, we pretrain a multimodal tabular transformer (SoilFormer) using a self-supervised objective based on feature masking, achieving stable training, strong predictive performance, and representations that support uncertainty-aware prediction. We further show that the learned representations recover relationships consistent with established soil processes. LUCAS-MEGA is released with open access and is accompanied by composable, agent-friendly APIs that support structured querying and data-driven workflows.

</details>


### [122] [Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation for LLM-Driven Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04057)
*Zhen Liu,Yuhan Liu,Jingwen Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文针对LLM辅助NAS中的功能纠缠问题，提出SPARK方法通过因子条件化编辑实现更精准的架构修改，在CLRS-DFS上取得28.1倍采样效率提升和22.9%的OOD准确率改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经架构搜索(NAS)面临关键挑战：需在昂贵评估下整合既有架构知识的同时探索新设计。虽然大语言模型(LLM)有潜力将丰富的架构和编码先验转化为可执行代码，但由于功能纠缠现象——单一编辑可能无意中耦合多个交互功能因素，导致局部修改产生非局部行为和性能偏移——使LLM的实际应用受限。

Method: 提出SPARK方法，通过显式选择待修改的功能因子并基于该因子条件化编辑来激活相关先验知识，从而减少纠缠副作用，实现更精准的架构修改。

Result: 在CLRS-DFS上实现了28.1倍采样效率的架构进化加速，OOD准确率相对提升22.9%。

Conclusion: SPARK通过因子条件化编辑使LLM知识在功能纠缠条件下可用，实现了更有针对性、更可靠的架构修改，为LLM辅助NAS提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: This paper focuses on a key challenge in Neural Architecture Search (NAS): integrating established architectural knowledge while exploring new designs under expensive evaluations. Large language models (LLMs) are a promising assistant for NAS because they can translate rich architectural and coding priors into executable code edits. However, in practice, seemingly local revisions often propagate into non-local behavioral and performance shifts because a single edit can inadvertently couple multiple interacting functional factors, a phenomenon we refer to as functional entanglement. To make LLM knowledge usable under such entanglement, we propose Structured Progressive Knowledge Activation (SPARK), which activates relevant priors by explicitly selecting the functional factor to modify and conditioning the edit on that factor. This factor-conditioned editing reduces entangled side effects and yields more targeted, reliable architecture modifications. On CLRS-DFS, SPARK achieves a 28.1x sample-efficient architecture evolution speedup and yields a 22.9 percent relative improvement in OOD accuracy.

</details>


### [123] [Continual Distillation of Teachers from Different Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04059)
*Nicolas Michel,Maorong Wang,Jiangpeng He,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大模型存储需求日益增长的问题，提出持续蒸馏（CD）范式，使学生模型可从不断变化的教师模型流中学习，但无需保留早期教师。引入外部无标签数据实现未见知识迁移（UKT），同时识别并解决顺序蒸馏中的未见知识遗忘（UKF）问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型规模持续增长，部分模型所需存储空间已超过许多大规模数据集，亟需新的知识迁移范式来应对这一挑战。

Method: 提出自外部数据蒸馏（SE2D）方法，通过在外部数据上保留logits来稳定异构教师间的学习；同时利用外部无标签数据实现跨领域知识迁移。

Result: SE2D方法有效减少了UKF现象，提升了跨域泛化能力。

Conclusion: 持续蒸馏是处理大规模模型知识迁移的有效范式，外部数据的使用和logits保留机制是解决知识遗忘问题的关键。代码已开源。

Abstract: Deep learning models continue to scale, with some requiring more storage than many large-scale datasets. Thus, we introduce a new paradigm: Continual Distillation (CD), where a student learns sequentially from a stream of teacher models without retaining access to earlier teachers. CD faces two challenges: teacher training data is unavailable, and teachers have varying expertise. We show that external unlabeled data enables Unseen Knowledge Transfer (UKT), allowing the student to acquire information from domains not present in the training data, while known to the teacher. We also show that sequential distillation causes Unseen Knowledge Forgetting (UKF) when transferred knowledge is lost after training on later teachers. To better trade off between UKT and UKF, we propose Self External Data Distillation (SE2D), a method that preserves logits on external data to stabilize learning across heterogeneous teachers. Experiments on multiple benchmarks show that SE2D reduces UKF and improves cross-domain generalization. The code and implementation for this work are publicly available at: https://github.com/Nicolas1203/continual_distillation.

</details>


### [124] [Lookahead Drifting Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04060)
*Guoqiang Zhang,Kenta Niwa,W. Bastiaan Kleijn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出lookahead drifting model，通过顺序计算多个drifting项并利用先前项的信息，捕获更高阶梯度信息来改进分布映射任务的模型训练，在图像生成任务上取得了优于基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统drifting model虽然通过一步神经功能评估(NFE)在ImageNet上取得了SOTA图像生成性能，但仍有提升空间，需要更好的梯度利用方式来改进模型训练。

Method: 在每个训练迭代中顺序计算一组drifting项，利用先前计算的drifting项、正样本和模型输出来计算后续项，后续计算的drifting项能够捕获更高阶的梯度信息，最后将模型输出推向所有drifting项的加权求和方向进行优化。

Result: 在toy示例和CIFAR10数据集上的实验表明，该方法性能优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，lookahead drifting model在toy示例和CIFAR10数据集上优于基线模型，验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Recently, a new paradigm named \emph{drifting model} has been proposed for mapping distributions, which achieves the SOTA image generation performance over ImageNet via one-step neural functional evaluation (NFE). The basic idea is to compute a drifting term at each training iteration and then push the output of the model towards the direction of the drifting term. In this paper, we propose a \emph{lookahead drifting model}. At each training iteration, we compute a set of drifting terms sequentially. Each drifting term is calculated by making use of previously computed ones as well as the positive samples and the output of the model. %One key step is to properly scale the drifting terms so that their magnitudes are in a comparable range. In principle, the drifting terms obtained at a later stage capture higher order gradient information towards the positive samples. At each training iteration, the model is optimized by pushing its output towards the direction of the (weighted) summation of the drifting terms. Experimental results on toy examples and CIFAR10 demonstrate the better performance of the new method than the baseline.

</details>


### [125] [Investigating Trustworthiness of Nonparametric Deep Survival Models for Alzheimer's Disease Progression Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04063)
*Jacob Thrasher,Kaitlyn Heintzelman,Peter Martone,David Kotlowski,Binod Bhattarai,Donald Adjeroh,Prashnna Gyawali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对AD进展分析中的深度学习生存模型的公平性进行系统研究，提出两个新指标(Time-Dependent Concordance Impurity和Kaplan-Meier Fairness)，揭示了现有模型在性别、种族、教育等敏感属性上存在显著偏差，为实现公正可靠的AD预测模型奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AD是进行性神经退行性疾病，当前深度学习生存模型缺乏公平性考量，可能对边缘化群体产生不公平预测，亟需系统性的公平性研究。

Method: 采用非参数生存模型进行AD进展分析，通过时间依赖一致性纯度和Kaplan-Meier公平性两个新指标量化敏感属性(性别、种族、教育程度)偏差，并进行特征重要性分析。

Result: 研究发现深度学习生存模型虽能有效辅助临床决策，但普遍存在对敏感属性的显著偏差，为未来改进方向提供了重要参考。

Conclusion: 深度学习生存模型虽可辅助临床决策，但存在显著偏差。未来需关注模型公平性研究，解决对弱势群体的不公平预测问题。

Abstract: Alzheimer's Dementia (AD) is a progressive neurodegenerative disease marked by irreversible decline, making reliable modeling of its progression essential for effective patient care. Progression-aware methods such as survival analysis are therefore crucial tools for the early detection and monitoring of AD. Recent advancements in deep learning have demonstrated remarkable performance in survival tasks, but alarmingly fewer studies have been conducted in the domain of AD. Further, the studies that do exist do not consider learned bias within the model itself, which could result in unfair and unreliable predictions toward certain marginalized groups. As such, we conduct a rigorous study of fairness in AD progression analysis along with a thorough feature importance study to determine the characteristics which are most important for reliable AD predictions. Furthermore, we propose two novel fairness metrics, called Time-Dependent Concordance Impurity and Kaplan-Meier Fairness, to quantify bias with respect to sensitive attributes such as sex, race, and education in nonparametric survival models. Our study demonstrates that while deep learning powered survival models are robust tools which can aid clinicians in AD care decisions, they often exhibit considerable bias, representing important avenues for future research.

</details>


### [126] [A Regulatory Governance Framework for AI-Driven Financial Fraud Detection in U.S. Banking: Integrating OCC, SR 11-7, CFPB, and FinCEN Compliance Requirements for Model Development, Validation, and Monitoring Lifecycles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04076)
*Mohammad Nasir Uddin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出了RGF-AFFD监管治理框架，旨在解决美国金融机构AI欺诈检测系统面临的四框架碎片化合规问题。框架采用三层级治理架构，通过LSTM+XGBoost集成模型（ROC-AUC 0.9289）实现高性能检测，并通过RDT-FG监管数字孪生元模型将模型指标转化为监管特定健康评分，实现OCC、SR 11-7、CFPB和FinCEN四项监管要求的统一满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 美国金融机构在部署AI欺诈检测系统时面临四个监管框架（OCC Bulletin 2011-12、SR 11-7、CFPB AI公告、FinCEN BSA/SAR要求）构成的碎片化合规景观，缺乏将监管要求与模型开发、验证和监控实践相连接的综合治理生命周期。

Method: 采用三层级治理架构，基于IEEE-CIS数据集(590,540笔交易)和ULB基准数据集(284,807笔交易)进行基准测试，对六种架构进行消融分析、时间漂移分析、SHAP可解释性分析和BISG公平性分析。核心创新包括LSTM+XGBoost集成模型和RDT-FG监管数字孪生元模型，该元模型将评估指标转化为四个监管机构特定的健康评分和一个综合监管适应度指数。

Result: LSTM+XGBoost集成模型达到ROC-AUC 0.9289（F1: 0.6360），收益成本比为6:1。XGBoost展现出最强的时间稳定性（delta-AUC = -0.0017 vs LSTM的-0.0626）。RDT-FG元模型可生成针对四家监管机构的具体健康评分和综合监管适应度指数。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了第一个集成部署蓝图，同时满足OCC、SR 11-7、CFPB和FinCEN四项监管要求，包含社区银行实施案例和四项循证政策建议。框架通过RDT-FG监管数字孪生元模型实现持续合规监控，为AI驱动金融欺诈检测提供了系统性的监管治理解决方案。

Abstract: U.S. financial institutions deploying AI-based fraud detection face a fragmented compliance landscape spanning four regulatory frameworks -- OCC Bulletin 2011-12, SR 11-7, the CFPB AI circular, and FinCEN BSA/SAR requirements -- with no integrated governance life cycle connecting these requirements to model development, validation, and monitoring practice. This paper presents the Regulatory Governance Framework for AI-Driven Financial Fraud Detection (RGF-AFFD), a three-tier governance architecture empirically anchored in a multi-study empirical program. Using the IEEE-CIS dataset (590,540 transactions) and ULB benchmark (284,807 transactions), we benchmark six architectures including an LSTM+XGBoost ensemble, and conduct ablation, temporal drift, SHAP interpretability, and BISG fairness analyses. The LSTM+XGBoost ensemble achieves ROC-AUC of 0.9289 (F1: 0.6360) with a benefit-cost ratio of 6:1. XGBoost demonstrates the strongest temporal stability (delta-AUC = -0.0017 versus -0.0626 for LSTM). The RDT-FG Regulatory Digital Twin meta-model translates metrics into four regulator-specific health scores and a composite Regulatory Fitness Index for continuous compliance monitoring. The RGF-AFFD is the first integrated deployment blueprint to simultaneously satisfy OCC, SR 11-7, CFPB, and FinCEN requirements, supported by a community bank implementation vignette and four evidence-based policy recommendations.

</details>


### [127] [EdgeRazor: A Lightweight Framework for Large Language Models via Mixed-Precision Quantization-Aware Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04062)
*Shu-Hao Zhang,Le-Tong Huang,Xiang-Sheng Deng,Xin-Yi Zou,Chen Wu,Nan Li,Shao-Qun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EdgeRazor是一个针对大语言模型(LLM)的轻量级混合精度极低比特量化框架，通过混合精度量化感知蒸馏、自适应特征蒸馏和熵感知KL散度三个模块，实现1.88比特量化性能超越3比特方法，存储压缩5倍，解码加速15.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大模型部署到资源受限边缘设备的需求下，传统量化方法存在局限：后训练量化(PTQ)在4比特以下性能严重下降，量化感知训练(QAT)计算资源需求大，而量化感知蒸馏需要手动选择蒸馏特征且依赖教师模型数据。

Method: 提出EdgeRazor框架，包含三个核心模块：(1)混合精度量化感知蒸馏实现细粒度精度控制；(2)自适应特征蒸馏从16位教师模型提取知识；(3)熵感知KL散度在人工标注和蒸馏数据集上训练，前向-反向平衡由教师输出分布决定。

Result: 在基础模型、指令微调模型和多模态LLM上验证：1.88比特EdgeRazor性能超越所有3比特方法，比领先的2比特PTQ方法高11.3分，训练预算仅为领先QAT方法的1/4-1/10；1.58比特的Qwen3-0.6B存储从1.41GB降至0.28GB，解码加速15.1倍。

Conclusion: EdgeRazor在所有比特宽度下均实现更高压缩比，为边缘设备部署极低比特LLM提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed an increasing interest in deploying LLMs on resource-constrained devices, among which quantization has emerged as a promising lightweight technique that converts full-precision model weights and activations into lower-bit formats. Existing weight quantization approaches can be roughly divided into three categories: Post-Training Quantization (PTQ) that calibrates quantized parameters on a small dataset without retraining but suffers from severe performance degradation below 4-bit, Quantization-Aware Training (QAT) that searches low-bit parameters using surrogate gradients but demands substantial computational resources, and Quantization-Aware Distillation that integrates QAT with knowledge transfer from a full-precision teacher but manually selects features to distill and relies heavily on teacher-specific data. In this paper, we propose EdgeRazor, a lightweight framework for LLMs with mixed-precision and extremely low-bit weight quantization. The EdgeRazor framework contains three modules: Mixed-Precision Quantization-Aware Distillation for the fine-grained control of precision, Adaptive Feature Distillation that derives an $n$-bit student from its 16-bit teacher, and Entropy-Aware KL Divergence on both human-annotated and distilled datasets, whose forward-reverse balance is determined solely by the teacher's output distribution. Empirical investigations of EdgeRazor are conducted on base, instruction-tuned, and multimodal LLMs. Notably, EdgeRazor with 1.88-bit surpasses all contenders with the 3-bit precision, especially outperforms the leading 2-bit PTQ methods by 11.3 points, within a 4-10$\times$ lower training budget than the leading QAT approach. EdgeRazor delivers higher compression ratios at all bit width; the 1.58-bit Qwen3-0.6B reduces storage from 1.41 GB to 0.28 GB while accelerating decoding by 15.1$\times$ relative to the 16-bit baseline.

</details>


### [128] [Position: the Stochastic Parrot in the Coal Mine. Model Collapse is a Threat to Low-Resource Communities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04127)
*Devon Jarvis,Richard Klein,Benjamin Rosman,Steven James,Stefano Sarao Mannelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 模型崩溃指生成模型在先前模型输出上训练时出现的性能退化现象，该现象威胁AI民主化努力，因其降低训练效率并使数据分布偏离边缘模式，从而对低资源和边缘化社区造成不成比例的损害


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI生成内容的激增，生成模型在先前模型输出上训练时会出现性能退化；大语言模型倾向于复现训练数据中的常见模式、依赖庞大数据集并产生高昂环境成本，这些因素共同导致数据退化、文化偏见强化和资源浪费

Method: 立场论文，综合分析环境与文化两方面影响

Result: 模型崩溃会降低训练效率，并将数据分布扭曲，使其偏离分布的尾部区域

Conclusion: 提出了行动号召，并概述了缓解模型崩溃效应的初步方向

Abstract: Model collapse, the degradation in performance that arises when generative models are trained on the outputs of prior models, is an increasing concern as artificially generated content proliferates. Related critiques of large language models have highlighted their tendency to reproduce frequent patterns in training data, their reliance on vast datasets, and their substantial environmental cost. Together, these factors contribute to data degradation, the reinforcement of cultural biases, and inefficient resource use. In this position paper we aim to combine these views and argue that model collapse threatens current efforts to democratize AI. By reducing training efficiency and skewing data distributions away from the tails of their support, model collapse disproportionately impacts low-resource and marginalized communities. We examine both the environmental and cultural implications of this phenomenon, situate our position within recent position papers on model collapse, and conclude with a call to action. Finally, we outline initial directions for mitigating these effects.

</details>


### [129] [Improving Medical VQA through Trajectory-Aware Process Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04064)
*Halil Ibrahim Gulluk,Olivier Gevaert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合DTW过程奖励的两阶段训练框架，提升医学VQA模型的推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学VQA数据集缺乏推理解释，推理能力对可靠医学VQA至关重要。

Method: 使用COMCTS算法生成推理轨迹，LLM作为验证法官。两阶段训练框架：监督微调(SFT)后接GRPO，采用基于DTW的过程奖励，通过句子 transformer嵌入推理步骤并计算向量序列间的动态时间规整距离来衡量生成推理与真实推理的相似度。

Result: DTW过程奖励+精确匹配奖励一致优于仅SFT训练：平均准确率从0.598提升至0.689，BERTScore从0.845提升至0.881，ROUGE-L从0.665提升至0.748。

Conclusion: 通过结合DTW过程奖励和精确匹配奖励的两阶段训练框架，显著提升医学VLM的推理能力，证明过程监督对医学VLM推理训练的重要性。

Abstract: Reasoning capabilities are crucial for reliable medical visual question answering (VQA); however, existing datasets rarely include reasoning explanations.
  We address this by generating reasoning trajectories for six medical VQA benchmarks using the COMCTS algorithm with open-source vision-language models, with an LLM serving as the verification judge.
  Building on these generated datasets, we propose a two-stage training framework: supervised fine-tuning followed by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a novel process-based reward.
  While standard approaches rely solely on exact-match rewards for final answers, we introduce a trajectory-aware reward that measures the similarity between generated and ground-truth reasoning processes.
  Specifically, we embed reasoning steps using sentence transformers and compute the Dynamic Time Warping (DTW) distance between the resulting vector sequences.
  Experiments across six benchmarks demonstrate that combining the DTW-based process reward with exact-match reward consistently outperforms SFT-only training, raising mean accuracy from 0.598 to 0.689, mean BERTScore from 0.845 to 0.881, and mean ROUGE-L from 0.665 to 0.748.
  Our results highlight the importance of process supervision in training reasoning-capable medical VLMs.
  We make our code and generated reasoning datasets publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/MICCAI-R1-MED-VQA-code-B14B/

</details>


### [130] [YOTOnet: Zero-Shot Cross-Domain Fault Diagnosis via Domain-Conditioned Mixture of Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04528)
*Zesen Wang,Zihao Wu,Yue Hu,Yang Gao,Fuzhen Xuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出YOTOnet（You Only Train Once），一种基于深度学习的机械故障诊断模型，旨在解决跨域/跨设备场景下的领域偏移问题。YOTOnet由三个核心组件构成：物理感知不变特征提取器、领域条件稀疏专家模块和双头分类系统。在5个公开轴承数据集上的30个跨数据集协议验证表明，该方法展现出明显的小样本学习效果——训练数据集从1个增至4个时，F1分数从0.5339提升至0.705。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于深度学习的机械故障诊断模型存在严重的领域偏移问题，导致模型在不同设备或不同运行工况之间泛化能力较差，限制了模型的实际部署和应用。传统方法需要针对每个新场景重新训练或微调模型，缺乏跨域泛化能力。

Method: YOTOnet采用三组件架构：(1) 物理感知不变特征提取器（Invariant Feature Distiller）：利用多尺度膨胀卷积和基于FFT的时频融合提取领域无关特征；(2) 领域条件稀疏专家模块（DC-MoE）：通过可学习的门控机制自适应地将输入路由到专用处理器，无需外部元数据；(3) 双头分类系统：配备辅助监督信号。

Result: 在5个公开轴承数据集（CWRU、MFPT、XJTU、OTTAWA、HUST）上通过30个跨数据集协议进行验证，YOTOnet相比现有最优方法表现出显著优势。关键发现是存在明显的规模效应——平均测试F1从使用1个训练数据集的0.5339提升至使用4个数据集的0.705，尤其是从3个数据集增加到4个数据集时有明显增益。

Conclusion: 基础模型（Foundation Model）的原则可应用于工业故障诊断领域，实现鲁棒的"一次训练，多次部署"（train-once deployment）。多数据集联合训练能够显著提升模型的跨域泛化能力，为工业设备的通用故障诊断提供了新的技术路径。

Abstract: Mechanical equipment forms the critical backbone of modern industrial production, yet domain shift severely limits the generalization of deep learning based fault diagnosis models across different equipment and operating conditions.Inspired by the success of foundation models in achieving zero-shotgeneralization, we propose YOTOnet (You Only Train Once), a novel architecture specifically designed for cross-domain fault diagnosis in mechanical equipment.YOTOnet comprises three core components: (1) a physics-aware Invariant Feature Distiller that extracts domain-agnostic representations using multi-scale dilated convolutions and FFT-based time-frequency fusion,(2) Domain-Conditioned Sparse Experts (DC-MoE) that adaptively route inputs to specialized processors via learned gating without external meta-data, and (3) a dual-head classification system with auxiliary supervision.Extensive validation on five public bearing datasets (CWRU, MFPT, XJTU,OTTAWA, HUST) through 30 cross-dataset protocols demonstrates the superiority of YOTOnet compared with other state-of-the-art methods. Critically, we observe a clear scaling effect-average test F1 improves from 0.5339(1 training dataset) to 0.705 (4 datasets), with a clear gain when moving from 3 to 4 datasets. These findings provide empirical evidence that foundation model principles can enable robust, train-once deployment for industrial fault diagnosis.

</details>


### [131] [LAWS: Learning from Actual Workloads Symbolically -- A Self-Certifying Parametrized Cache Architecture for Neural Inference, Robotics, and Edge Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04069)
*Gregory Magarshak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LAWS是一种自认证推理缓存架构，通过从实际部署中构建经过认证的专家函数库来实现高效推理。核心创新在于形式化误差边界可在部署时无需ground truth进行验证，同时统一了MoE和KV缓存两种范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型模型推理中的计算效率问题，通过学习实际工作负载来构建可认证的缓存/近似系统。动机是传统方法缺乏部署时的形式化保证，且MoE和缓存方法缺乏统一理论框架。

Method: 1) 构建PLT(概率语言Trie)定义输入空间分区；2) 训练覆盖各分区的专家函数；3) 基于Lipschitz常数和嵌入直径建立可验证的误差边界；4) 支持无匹配路由策略保证覆盖率单调递增。

Result: 证明LAWS是MoE和KV前缀缓存的超集；提出专家库增长率O(2^H log N)；舰队学习收敛加速Omega(K)；给出空中更新带宽界；误差界为ε_fit + 2Λ(W)C_E。

Conclusion: LAWS提供了统一的推理缓存理论框架，理论上可保证任意输入的形式化误差上界。文中两个未证明的猜想涉及获取最优性和Lipschitz增长多项性，待后续工作验证。

Abstract: We introduce LAWS (Learning from Actual Workloads Symbolically), a self-certifying inference caching architecture that builds a growing library of certified expert functions from deployment observations. Each expert covers a region of input space defined by a node in the Probabilistic Language Trie (PLT) of the base model and carries a formal error bound holding uniformly over all inputs. The central result is a self-certification theorem: for any input x, the LAWS approximation error is bounded by epsilon_fit + 2*Lambda(W)*C_E, where Lambda(W) is the model Lipschitz constant, C_E is the maximum embedding diameter, and epsilon_fit is the expert training error -- all checkable at deployment time without ground truth. We prove that LAWS generalizes both Mixture-of-Experts and KV prefix caching as special cases and is strictly more expressive than any fixed-K MoE or finite cache. Further results include a monotone hit rate theorem (any-match routing ensures coverage only increases), an expert library growth rate of O(2^H log N) where H is workload entropy, a fleet learning convergence theorem with Omega(K) speedup for K-unit fleets, and an over-the-air update bandwidth bound. We conjecture that LAWS is acquisition-optimal among stationary online caching algorithms and that the effective Lipschitz constant on the training distribution grows polynomially rather than exponentially in depth. Applications are developed for LLM inference, robotic control, and multi-agent edge deployment.

</details>


### [132] [Graph-SND: Sparse Aggregation for Behavioral Diversity in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05020)
*Shawn Ray*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Graph-SND通过图结构替换完全图平均，将SND的计算复杂度从二次方降至线性或亚线性，同时保持语义等价性，实验验证了该方法在保持精度的同时实现数量级加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统神经多样性(SND)通过所有配对距离的平均来测量多智能体强化学习中的行为异质性，但每次计算复杂度为O(n²)，存在可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 将完全图平均替换为任意图G上边的加权平均，提出三个变体：完全图恢复原SND、固定稀疏图实现O(|E|)复杂度、随机边采样使用Horvitz-Thompson估计。理论证明了扩展器的转发指数畸变边界、低秩距离结构下的谱细化，以及随机d-正则图的概率界。

Result: 在VMAS上验证了恢复性、无偏性、浓度和时钟扩展；在500次迭代100个智能体的PPO运行中，Bernoulli-0.1 Graph-SND保持追踪完整SND，每调用指标时间减少约10倍；500个智能体的GPU计时遵循预测的speedup；随机d-正则扩展器在Θ(n log n)边数下达到SND^u_G/SND ∈ [0.9987, 1.0013]；DiCo多样性控制中保持设定点追踪，同时成本降低约9.5倍。

Conclusion: Graph-SND是一个实用的稀疏替代方案，在保持原SND语义的同时显著降低了计算成本，支持被动测量和闭环多样性控制。

Abstract: System Neural Diversity (SND) measures behavioral heterogeneity in multi-agent reinforcement learning by averaging pairwise distances over all $\binom{n}{2}$ agent pairs, making each call quadratic in team size. We introduce Graph-SND, which replaces this complete-graph average with a weighted average over the edges of an arbitrary graph $G$. Three regimes follow: $G=K_n$ recovers SND exactly; a fixed sparse $G$ defines a localized diversity measure at $O(|E|)$ cost; and random edge samples yield an unbiased Horvitz-Thompson estimator and a normalized sample mean with $O(1/\sqrt{m})$ concentration in the sampled edge count $m$. For fixed sparse graphs we prove forwarding-index distortion bounds for expanders and a spectral refinement under low-rank distance structure; for random $d$-regular graphs we prove an unconditional probabilistic $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(D_{\max}/\sqrt{n})$ bound. On VMAS we verify recovery, unbiasedness, concentration, and wall-clock scaling, with a PettingZoo TVD panel checking non-Gaussian transfer. In a 500-iteration $n=100$ PPO run, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND tracks full SND while reducing per-call metric time by about $10\times$, and frozen-policy GPU timing up to $n=500$ follows the predicted $\binom{n}{2}/|E|$ speedup. Random $d$-regular expanders empirically achieve $\mathrm{SND}_{G}^{\mathrm{u}}/\mathrm{SND} \in [0.9987, 1.0013]$ at $Θ(n \log n)$ edges. In DiCo diversity control at $n=50$, Bernoulli-$0.1$ Graph-SND preserves set-point tracking with paired reward differences indistinguishable from zero across nine matched cells while cutting per-call metric cost by ${\sim}9.5\times$. Together, these results show that the SND aggregation bottleneck can be removed without changing the metric's semantics, yielding a drop-in sparse alternative that scales beyond complete-graph SND and supports both passive measurement and closed-loop diversity control.

</details>


### [133] [FlatASCEND: Autoregressive Clinical Sequence Generation with Continuous Time Prediction and Association-Based Pharmacological Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04071)
*Chris Sainsbury,Feng Dong,Andreas Karwath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlatASCEND是一个1450万参数的自回归临床序列模型，用于生成患者条件下的多步轨迹。标准评估指标无法区分其与平凡基线的差异，模型价值在于患者特定条件生成。实验表明患者条件化放大了药理学机制效应，但9/10的关联仍为混淆驱动，无因果区分能力。直接偏好优化反而破坏所有正确关联。生成证据在ICU短视野数据中最强，跨站点零样本迁移性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归模型虽能预测临床事件，但生成响应干预令牌的患者条件多步轨迹、以及验证轨迹是否保留已知药理学关联的能力尚未得到充分研究。这是生成式临床预测模型实用化的关键问题。

Method: FlatASCEND采用扁平复合令牌（flat composite tokens）和零膨胀对数正态时间头（zero-inflated log-normal time head），参数量14.5M。使用提示打乱（prompt-shuffle）消融和事件用户框架（incident-user framework）在MIMIC-IV数据集上进行评估。

Result: 标准分布指标（Jaccard 0.889-0.954）无法区分模型与平凡基件。患者条件化放大了机制性药理学效应（类固醇→葡萄糖、利尿剂→钾离子效应增强2.0-2.2倍），但混淆驱动关联保持不变（胰岛素→葡萄糖为0.9倍）。事件用户框架显示4/10恢复正确机制方向，2个复现治疗-上下文关联，4个错误（9/10显著）。直接偏好优化将正确率从3/3降至0/3。ICU数据生成效果最佳；门诊时间保真度较弱（INSPECT上中位数10天 vs 154天），跨站点零样本迁移性能下降。

Conclusion: FlatASCEND在患者条件化生成方面具有价值，但当前模式——在残留混淆下部分恢复药理学关联——与学习观测关联而非因果区分一致。直接偏好优化因奖励与评估共享结果域而出现奖励 exploitation（奖励过度利用）。模型在短视野ICU数据上效果最强，跨站点泛化能力有限。

Abstract: Autoregressive models can predict clinical events, but generating patient-conditioned multi-step trajectories that respond to intervention tokens and testing whether those responses preserve known pharmacological associations has received limited attention. We present FlatASCEND, a 14.5M-parameter autoregressive clinical sequence model using flat composite tokens and a zero-inflated log-normal time head. Standard distributional metrics (Jaccard 0.889-0.954) do not distinguish FlatASCEND from trivial baselines; the model's value lies in conditional generation from patient-specific prefixes. A prompt-shuffle ablation shows patient-specific conditioning amplifies mechanistic pharmacological effects (2.0-2.2x for steroid to glucose, diuretic to potassium) while leaving confounding-driven associations unchanged (0.9x for insulin to glucose). An incident-user framework assesses directional consistency against prior pharmacological knowledge on MIMIC-IV (N=500 per comparison): 4/10 recover correct mechanistic directions, 2 reproduce treatment-context associations, 4 are incorrect (9/10 significant, Wilcoxon p<0.05). This pattern - partial recovery under residual confounding - is consistent with learned observational associations without causal distinction. Direct preference optimisation with surrogate reward destroys all correct associations (3/3 to 0/3), illustrating reward exploitation when reward and evaluation share an outcome domain. Generative evidence is strongest for short-horizon ICU data; outpatient temporal fidelity is weaker (median 10 vs 154 days on INSPECT), and zero-shot cross-site transfer degrades without adaptation.

</details>


### [134] [Sparse Autoencoder Decomposition of Clinical Sequence Model Representations: Feature Complexity, Task Specialisation, and Mortality Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04072)
*Chris Sainsbury,Feng Dong,Andreas Karwath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统性地将稀疏自编码器（SAE）应用于EHR基础模型（FlatASCEND），探索其在临床预测任务中的解释性和预测性能。研究发现SAE特征呈现渐进式抽象特征（从token检测到语义聚类），但在临床泄漏安全窗口下，稠密表示反而优于SAE特征。SAE特征可复现性低（21%），delta-mode可降低扰动噪声但效果不显著，表明SAE在EHR领域的实际应用仍面临挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAE已在大型语言模型和蛋白质语言模型中得到应用，但尚未系统性地应用于电子健康记录（EHR）基础模型。该研究旨在探索SAE能否帮助解释EHR基础模型的内部工作机制，并评估其在临床预测任务中的实际效用。

Method: 在FlatASCEND（14.5M参数的 自回归临床序列模型）上训练TopK SAEs，在10个残差流提取点进行分解。评估数据集包括门诊场景的INSPECT和ICU场景的MIMIC-IV。采用全序列简单线性探针和泄漏安全时间窗口两种设置进行对比，并使用delta-mode干预方法减少SAE扰动噪声。

Result: 1. 特征渐进式抽象：层0特征近乎完美的token检测器（45.7%单例），层6特征跨越约30种token类型（0.5%单例）。2. 探针级别差异：SAE特征在离散事件预测（死亡率）上优于稠密表示，稠密表示在连续量预测（住院时长）上更优。3. 泄漏安全窗口下：稠密表示在所有测试场景中匹配或超越SAE特征（eICU-CRD 48h AUC: SAE 0.871 vs 稠密 0.880；MIMIC-IV: 0.836 vs 0.914；INSPECT 1/3年: 0.697 vs 0.800）。4. Delta-mode干预使SAE扰动噪声降低86倍。5. 特征可复现性仅21%。

Conclusion: 该研究系统性探索了SAE在EHR基础模型中的应用，发现SAE特征在离散事件预测（死亡率）上优于稠密表示，但在连续变量预测（住院时长）上表现较差。在临床相关的泄漏安全窗口设置下，稠密表示反而更具优势。SAE特征的跨随机种子可复现性仅为21%，且delta-mode干预虽然降低了86倍扰动噪声，但效果并不具有统计显著性。该工作为SAE在临床预测任务中的应用提供了重要基准，但实际临床部署需谨慎。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have been applied to large language models and protein language models, but not systematically to electronic health record (EHR) foundation models. We train TopK SAEs on FlatASCEND, a 14.5-million-parameter autoregressive clinical sequence model, at all 10 residual stream extraction points on INSPECT (outpatient) and MIMIC-IV (ICU). SAE decomposition reveals progressive abstraction across transformer depth: layer-0 features are near-perfect token detectors (45.7% singleton), while layer-6 features span approximately 30 token types across multiple clinical categories (0.5% singleton). Under full-sequence simple linear probes, SAE features outperform dense representations for discrete event prediction (mortality) while dense representations outperform for continuous magnitude prediction (length of stay) - a probe-level representational phenomenon that does not extend to clinically relevant leakage-safe windows, where dense representations match or exceed SAE features across all tested settings (eICU-CRD 48-hour AUC: SAE 0.871 versus dense 0.880; base model zero-shot, SAE dictionaries trained on eICU activations; MIMIC-IV: 0.836 versus 0.914; INSPECT 1-year/3-year: 0.697 versus 0.800). A delta-mode intervention method reduces SAE perturbation noise by 86x, enabling cleaner feature-level experiments, though the resulting perturbation effects are larger than random controls in 3 of 4 conditions but not formally significant. Feature reproducibility across random seeds is 21%, and individual features should be interpreted as illustrative rather than stable.

</details>


### [135] [Confronting Label Indeterminacy in Automated Bail Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04073)
*Cor Steging,Tadeusz Zbiegień*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究探讨了在保释决策数据中标签不确定性问题的处理方法，以宾夕法尼亚州统一司法系统为案例，评估了五种处理标签不确定性的方法对三个机器学习模型的影响。研究发现所有方法都依赖无法验证的假设，且对模型预测行为的影响可能超过模型本身的选择，解释性AI分析进一步揭示了这些影响延伸至模型内部决策过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 保释被拒绝时，反事实结果（被告是否会出庭）无法被观测，导致历史保释数据存在结构性标签不确定性。在此类数据上构建自动化系统存在引入偏差和强化反馈循环的风险。这是一个关于法律决策支持系统中如何处理缺失标签的核心问题。

Method: 采用宾夕法尼亚州统一司法系统的保释决策数据进行案例研究，评估三种机器学习模型在五种标签不确定性处理方法下的表现，包括一种由保释决策动态驱动的新型标签插补方法。使用可解释AI分析来揭示这些处理方法对模型内部决策过程的影响。

Result: 所有处理方法都依赖无法验证的假设，但都显著影响模型的预测行为。这种影响有时甚至超过模型本身选择的影响。可解释AI分析进一步揭示这些效应延伸至模型的内部决策机制。

Conclusion: 在法律决策场景中，标签不确定性是一个被低估但关键的方法论挑战。不同处理假设对模型的影响表明，研究者和实践者需要更审慎地对待数据标签问题，并从法律视角评估这些方法的合法性。

Abstract: Bail decisions present a fundamental challenge for data-driven decision support systems. When bail is denied, the counterfactual outcome of whether the defendant would have appeared in court remains unobserved. As a result, historical bail data embed structural label indeterminacy: future decisions are influenced by past decisions whose outcomes are only partially knowable. Building automated systems on such data risks introducing bias and reinforcing feedback loops. This raises a core question for machine-learning systems intended to assist judicial actors: how should cases in which bail was denied be treated during model development? In a case study of bail decisions from the Unified Judicial System of Pennsylvania, we evaluate five contemporary approaches to handling label indeterminacy across three machine learning models, including a novel label imputation method motivated by the dynamics of bail decisions. Each method relies on unverifiable assumptions, yet all influence the models' predictive behaviour, sometimes even more so than the choice of model itself. Explainable AI analysis further reveals that these effects extend to the models' internal decision-making processes as well. Finally, we consider the notion of label indeterminacy from a legal perspective and assess the legitimacy of these approaches in the context of bail decision-making.

</details>


### [136] [Conditional outlier detection for clinical alerting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05124)
*Milos Hauskrecht,Michal Valko,Shyam Visweswaran,Iyad Batal,Gilles Clermont,Gregory Cooper*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A data-driven anomaly detection method using electronic health records can effectively identify potentially erroneous patient-management actions with acceptable false alert rates.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Patient safety concerns; hypothesis that unusual patient-management actions (relative to past patients) may indicate potential medical errors that warrant alerting.

Method: Data-driven approach using EHR data from 4,486 post-cardiac surgical patients, validated by expert panel opinions to evaluate detection performance.

Result: Anomaly-based alerting achieved reasonably low false alert rates; stronger anomalies demonstrated higher correlation with alert rates.

Conclusion: Anomaly-based alerting shows promise for improving patient safety by identifying potentially erroneous patient-management actions, with low false alert rates.

Abstract: We develop and evaluate a data-driven approach for detecting unusual (anomalous) patient-management actions using past patient cases stored in an electronic health record (EHR) system. Our hypothesis is that patient-management actions that are unusual with respect to past patients may be due to a potential error and that it is worthwhile to raise an alert if such a condition is encountered. We evaluate this hypothesis using data obtained from the electronic health records of 4,486 post-cardiac surgical patients. We base the evaluation on the opinions of a panel of experts. The results support that anomaly-based alerting can have reasonably low false alert rates and that stronger anomalies are correlated with higher alert rates.

</details>


### [137] [Human-AI Co-Mentorship in Project-Based Learning: A Case Study in Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.05144)
*Freyaa Chawla,Ahan Chawla,Rishi Singh,Joe Germino,Grigorii Khvatskii*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一项关于AI工具辅助高中生参与金融科技研究项目的教学案例研究。项目采用基于工作流设计的教学方法，结合每日站会机制，使缺乏AI和金融背景的高中生能够在AI工具辅助下完成ETF价格预测模型的开发，证明了AI工具在教育场景中的 Mentoring 潜力。

TLDR: A pedagogical case study on using AI tools to mentor high school students in a fintech research project on ETF price prediction. By emphasizing workflow design and daily stand-ups, students with limited backgrounds successfully built models with real-world applications, demonstrating the mentoring potential of AI tools in educational settings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习模式遵循"先课堂讲授后实践"的顺序，这种模式对于时间和资源要求较高。作者认为AI工具可以加速学习过程，使高中生能够专注于更高阶的问题定义和解决，从而弥合知识背景不足与实际项目参与之间的差距。

Motivation: Traditional learning follows a sequential "learn first, apply later" approach, which is resource-intensive. The authors argue that AI tools can accelerate the learning process, enabling high school students to focus on higher-order problem formulation and solving, thereby bridging the gap between limited background and meaningful project participation.

Method: 采用基于工作流设计（workflow design）的教学方法，而非传统的先课堂讲授后实践的模式。高中生在研究生导师的指导下，使用AI驱动工具识别并执行问题解决的各个步骤，通过迭代方式开发代码，并利用每日站会进行调试和概念性问题的解答。学生可根据个人兴趣（计算机科学或金融）深入研究特定领域。

Method: A workflow-design-based teaching approach was adopted, contrasting with the traditional "learn first, apply later" model. High school students, under the guidance of graduate mentors, used AI-driven tools to identify and execute steps in problem-solving, developed code iteratively, and used daily stand-ups for debugging and addressing conceptual questions. Students explored depth in their areas of interest (CS or finance) based on personal preferences.

Result: 尽管参与者进入项目时AI和金融背景有限，他们成功利用AI工具构建了具有实际应用价值的模型，并在各自感兴趣的领域（计算机科学或金融）深入钻研，在2025年夏季取得了显著进展。

Result: Despite entering with limited AI and finance backgrounds, participants successfully built meaningful models with real-world applications using AI tools, and achieved significant progress in their areas of interest (CS or finance) over summer 2025.

Conclusion: 这项研究证明了AI工具可以有效用于指导高中生，使他们在技术资质有限的情况下仍能构建具有实际应用价值的模型。每日站会机制被证明是培养问题定义和概念理解能力的有效教学手段。

This paper reflects on a AI research project carried out by a team of high-school and early-undergraduate students under the mentorship of graduate researchers and ably assisted by AI tools. We share our experience in not only on the learning experience for the high school students, but also on how AI tools accelerated the process that enabled the high school students to focus on higher order problem formulation and solution. Although the participants entered the project with limited background in both AI and finance, they showed strong enthusiasm for technical market analysis and ETF price prediction. Traditional learning settings would first teach the necessary methods in a classroom setting and only later let students apply them. In contrast, our project emphasized workflow design: students identified the sequence of steps needed to address the problem and then used AI-driven tools to execute each step.
  We note that the high school students developed the necessary code through iterating with the AI tools, and we used our daily stand-ups to debug and answer conceptual questions. Each of the student was able to dig deeper into their area of interest whether computer science or finance, while collaboratively making a significant advance over the summer of 2025. This project was an important pedagogical exercise on how AI tools can be used for mentoring high school students, allowing them to focus on their specific interests and using the daily stand-ups to focus on problem definition and conceptual understanding. Despite their limited technical qualifications, the students were able to leverage AI tools to build meaningful models with real-world application.

Abstract: This paper reflects on a AI research project carried out by a team of high-school and early-undergraduate students under the mentorship of graduate researchers and ably assisted by AI tools. We share our experience in not only on the learning experience for the high school students, but also on how AI tools accelerated the process that enabled the high school students to focus on higher order problem formulation and solution. Although the participants entered the project with limited background in both AI and finance, they showed strong enthusiasm for technical market analysis and ETF price prediction. Traditional learning settings would first teach the necessary methods in a classroom setting and only later let students apply them. In contrast, our project emphasized workflow design: students identified the sequence of steps needed to address the problem and then used AI-driven tools to execute each step.
  We note that the high school students developed the necessary code through iterating with the AI tools, and we used our daily stand-ups to debug and answer conceptual questions. Each of the student was able to dig deeper into their area of interest whether computer science or finance, while collaboratively making a significant advance over the summer of 2025. This project was an important pedagogical exercise on how AI tools can be used for mentoring high school students, allowing them to focus on their specific interests and using the daily stand-ups to focus on problem definition and conceptual understanding. Despite their limited technical qualifications, the students were able to leverage AI tools to build meaningful models with real-world application.

</details>


### [138] [Balanced Aggregation: Understanding and Fixing Aggregation Bias in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04077)
*Zhiyuan Zeng,Jiameng Huang,Zhangyue Yin,Jiashuo Liu,Ziniu Li,Bingrui Li,Yuhao Wu,Yining Zheng,Ge Zhang,Wenhao Huang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a central paradigm for improving reasoning and code generation in large language models, and GRPO-style training is widely adopted for its simplicity and effectiveness. However, an important design choice remains underexplored: how token-level policy gradient terms are aggregated within each sampled group. Standard GRPO uses sequence aggregation, while recent work has advocated token aggregation as a better alternative. We show that these two rules induce different optimization biases: token aggregation introduces sign-length coupling, while sequence aggregation implicitly downweights longer responses through sequence-level equal weighting. To address this tension, we propose \textbf{Balanced Aggregation (BA)}, a simple drop-in replacement that computes token-level means separately within the positive and negative subsets and then combines them with sequence-count-based weights. Experiments with Qwen2.5-Math-7B and Qwen3-1.7B on DAPO-17k and Polaris, evaluated on six reasoning and coding benchmarks, show that BA consistently improves training stability and final performance over standard token and sequence aggregation. Our analysis further shows that the relative effectiveness of token and sequence aggregation is largely governed by response-length variation and the positive-negative length gap, highlighting aggregation as a critical design dimension in GRPO-style RLVR.

</details>


### [139] [Validity-Calibrated Reasoning Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04078)
*Khouloud Saadi,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有效性校准推理蒸馏框架，将推理蒸馏重新定义为局部学习信号分配问题。通过比较师生模型在相同前缀下的下一步动作，基于相对局部有效性动态调节蒸馏更新强度，避免刚性轨迹模仿。实验表明这种原则性、局部校准的学习信号分配方式优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理蒸馏方法依赖静态师生层级，将蒸馏视为轨迹模仿。然而推理过程中间步骤具有局部欠约束性——全局正确性约束最终答案，但不唯一确定每个中间步骤，轨迹模仿方法与推理结构存在内在错配。

Method: 提出局部有效性校准机制：将学生与教师在相同前缀下的下一步动作进行对比，根据其相对局部有效性动态调节蒸馏更新强度，实现情境自适应的监督信号分配。

Result: 在数学推理、代码生成和指令遵循三类基准测试中，该方法持续优于强基线蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 有效性校准推理蒸馏为大型语言模型推理蒸馏提供了新范式——通过动态评估局部有效性而非静态模仿轨迹来分配学习信号，从而更符合推理过程的内在结构特性。

Abstract: Reasoning distillation aims to transfer multi-step reasoning capabilities from large language models to smaller, more efficient ones. While recent methods have shown promising gains, they typically rely on static teacher-student hierarchies and frame distillation as trajectory imitation. This is misaligned with the structure of reasoning, where intermediate steps are often locally under-specified: global correctness constrains the final answer, but does not uniquely determine each intermediate move. We propose validity-calibrated reasoning distillation, a framework that treats reasoning distillation as a problem of local learning-signal allocation rather than path alignment. Instead of enforcing token-level imitation, we compare the student's and teacher's proposed next-step actions under the same prefix and use their relative local validity to modulate the strength of the distillation update. This yields a dynamic, context-dependent supervision mechanism that preserves the teacher's structural guidance while adapting update strength to local reasoning quality. Across mathematical reasoning, code generation, and instruction-following benchmarks, our method consistently outperforms strong distillation baselines. These results indicate that effective LLM reasoning distillation is governed not by rigid trajectory imitation, but by principled, locally calibrated allocation of learning signal.

</details>


### [140] [Enhancing the interpretability of spatially variable N2O model predictions with soft sensors during wastewater treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04082)
*Mohammad Raeisi Gahrouei,Pedram Ramin,Vincenzo A. Riggio,Carlos Domingo-Felez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Model-based solutions for nitrous oxide (N2O) emissions from wastewater treatment plants (WWTP) are informed by operational datasets designed to control nutrient levels in liquid waste, coupled with dedicated campaigns for N2O measurements. We analysed how machine learning (ML) models predict disturbances to WWT operation and spatially variable N2O emissions. A real dataset was investigated to validate the modelling framework from N2O emissions predicted by four ML models (R2 = 0.79 - 0.89). Monitoring campaigns for N2O were simulated with a plant-wide mechanistic model to include additional sensors, site-level N2O datasets, and wastewater disturbances (n = 16). ML models were highly accurate (0.97 +- 0.02, n = 80), but the feature importance depended on the model, the scenario and the N2O measurement scale (reactor vs. WWTP). We argue that N2O soft sensor model predictions are limited to the measuring location and the methodological uncertainty of the dataset, which affect the interpretability of the model. Lastly, the analysis of the mechanistic model structure exposed interactions between autotrophic and heterotrophic pathways over nitric oxide which can overestimate aerobic nitrite production and bias the N2O pathway contributions.

</details>


### [141] [Ensuring Reliability in Programming Knowledge Tracing: A Re-evaluation of Attention-augmented Models and Experimental Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04727)
*Jaewook Kim,Hyeoncheol Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究发现，当前编程知识追踪(PKT)模型报告的性能提升可能受到模型配置和序列构建实践的显著影响。通过规范化评估协议（如固定超参数、控制序列长度），在CodeWorkout数据集上的重新评估表明，注意力增强模型与标准DKT之间的性能差距显著缩小，表明架构复杂度的增加并不一致地转化为更好的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PKT模型虽然报告了较强的实证性能，但其可靠性可能对细微的实现和实验设计选择敏感。本研究旨在重新审视代表性PKT模型，揭示其报告的性能提升是否真正来自模型改进而非实验设计问题。

Method: 1. 识别注意力维度设置中影响性能估计的问题；2. 证明忽视ServerTimestamp的不当学生尝试排序会违反时间因果性并导致过度乐观的结果；3. 通过网格搜索在单个指定折上选择超参数，然后在交叉验证中统一固定；4. 系统分析作业级特征和最大序列长度的影响；5. 在CodeWorkout数据集上重新评估PKT模型。

Result: 在受控且一致的设置下，注意力增强模型与标准DKT之间的性能差距显著缩小；增加的架构复杂性并不一致地转化为更好的性能。

Conclusion: 本研究揭示了PKT模型评估中的关键问题，并为编程知识追踪的可靠和可比较评估提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Programming Knowledge Tracing (PKT) has recently advanced through hybrid approaches that integrate attention-based feature modeling for code representation with RNN-based sequential prediction. While these models report strong empirical performance, their reliability can be sensitive to subtle implementation and experimental design choices. This study revisits representative PKT models and shows that reported gains can be substantially influenced by model configuration and sequence construction practices. We identify issues in attention dimension settings that affect performance estimates, and demonstrate that improper ordering of student attempts, such as ignoring ServerTimestamp, can violate temporal causality and lead to overly optimistic results. To ensure consistent evaluation, hyperparameters are selected via grid search guided by a single designated fold and then fixed uniformly across all folds during cross-validation. We further analyze the role of assignment-wise characteristics and systematically explore the impact of maximum sequence length. Using this protocol, we re-evaluate PKT models on the CodeWorkout dataset. Our results show that, under controlled and consistent settings, the performance gap between attention-enhanced models and standard DKT is significantly reduced, and increased architectural complexity does not consistently translate into superior performance. Beyond individual model comparisons, this work provides practical guidance for reliable and comparable evaluation in programming knowledge tracing.

</details>


### [142] [AsymmetryZero: A Framework for Operationalizing Human Expert Preferences as Semantic Evals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04083)
*Tadhg Looram,Lucas Nuzzi,Kyle Waters,Steven Dillmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AsymmetryZero通过"评估契约"机制将人类专家偏好可操作化为语义评估，实现模型与智能体评估的统一执行。研究表明紧凑模型陪审团在大幅降低成本和延迟的情况下，可达到与前沿模型陪审团相当的任务级表现，为实际场景中的后训练评估提供了高效且可靠的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习后训练的核心瓶颈在于将人类专家需求忠实编码为评估机制。现有RL流程依赖精确匹配目标或开放式偏好判断，但现实任务常具主观性、程序性和领域特定性，难以通过这些方式有效编码，亟需可操作的评估框架。

Method: 提出AsymmetryZero框架，将任务表示为稳定的评估契约（包含评分内容、判定方式、聚合规则），并通过Inspect（模型评估）和Harbor框架（智能体评估）双引擎执行。实验比较了5模型前沿陪审团与5模型紧凑陪审团在4个前沿级求解器上的表现，分析契约级一致率、内部分歧率及成本效益。

Result: 紧凑陪审团与前沿陪审团的契约级一致率为75.9%-89.6%（严格共同子集一致率77.8%-92.1%），但内部分歧显著更高（3-2分裂率28.7%-32.4% vs 前沿6.1%-11.5%）。成本方面，紧凑陪审团将每契约判定成本降至前沿的4.2%-5.6%，延迟降至21.7%-27.1%，而聚合任务级结果通常保持稳定。

Conclusion: AsymmetryZero成功将人类专家偏好可操作化为语义评估，通过稳定的评估契约机制实现了模型评估与智能体评估的兼容执行。研究证明紧凑模型陪审团可在大幅降低成本（4.2%-5.6%）和延迟（21.7%-27.1%）的同时，保持与前沿模型陪审团相当的聚合任务级结果，为后训练中的评估设计提供了务实解决方案。

Abstract: Much of the focus in RL today is on evaluation design: building meaningful evals that serve simultaneously as benchmarks and as well-defined reward signals for post-training. Yet, many real-world tasks are governed by subjective, procedural, and domain-specific requirements that are difficult to encode as exact-match targets or open-ended preference judgments frequently used in RL pipelines today. In this work, we present AsymmetryZero, a framework for operationalizing human expert preferences as semantic evals. AsymmetryZero represents each task as a stable evaluation contract that makes grading criteria explicit: what is being graded, how each criterion is judged, and how criterion-level decisions are aggregated into a task outcome. The same contract can be executed using Inspect for model-only evaluations, as well as the Harbor Framework for agentic evaluations, enabling comparable scores and shared audit artifacts across both settings. We argue that the central challenge in post-training today is the faithful encoding of expert requirements into the evaluation itself. To that end, we present a study using Harbor that holds task contracts fixed and compares a five-model frontier jury against a five-model compact jury across four frontier-class solvers (Claude Opus 4.6, GPT-5.4, Grok-4.20, Gemini-3.1-Pro). We find that criterion-level frontier-vs-compact agreement ranges from $75.9\%$ to $89.6\%$ (strict common-subset agreement: $77.8\%$ to $92.1\%$), while compact juries exhibit substantially higher internal dissent (3--2 split rate $28.7\%$--$32.4\%$) than frontier juries ($6.1\%$--$11.5\%$). Verifier traces further show that compact juries reduce per-criterion judging cost to roughly $4.2\%$--$5.6\%$ of frontier and latency to roughly $21.7\%$--$27.1\%$, even as aggregated task-level outcomes often remain comparatively stable.

</details>


### [143] [FASQ: Flexible Accelerated Subspace Quantization for Calibration-Free LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04084)
*Ye Qiao,Yian Wang,Zhiheng Chen,Hyoukjun Kwon,Sitao Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FASQ通过将乘积量化应用于LLM权重矩阵，实现了无需校准的连续可调压缩（27-49%原始FP16大小），在保持甚至超越固定位宽方法准确率的同时，成为首个在单张消费级GPU上超越FP16解码速度的压缩方法，这得益于其专门设计的定制CUDA内核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有标量量化方法受限于固定位宽（如8/4/3-bit），仅提供离散的压缩节点，且通常需要校准数据，无法灵活覆盖27-49%模型大小这一中间压缩区间，导致在精度-效率权衡上存在空白。

Method: 提出FASQ（Flexible Accelerated Subspace Quantization）框架，将乘积量化（product quantization）应用于LLM权重矩阵，通过调节子向量大小（sub-vector size）和码本基数（codebook cardinality）两个参数，实现连续可调的压缩空间；为解决乘积量化的推理开销，设计了定制CUDA内核——解码阶段采用无查找表的直接计算GEMV，预填充阶段采用输出驻留双缓冲LUT GEMM，两者均支持split-K并行策略。

Result: 在Meta-Llama-3-8B上，FASQ以37-42%模型大小实现67.1-67.7的平均准确率，优于4-bit GPTQ和AWQ；推理性能方面，RTX 3090上解码速度达45.2 tok/s（有效4-bit，2.56x内存压缩）和51.8 tok/s（有效3-bit，2.80x内存压缩），均超过FP16基线（43.9 tok/s），吞吐量分别是AWQ的1.6-1.8x、GPTQ的2.5x、RTN的4.3-5x。

Conclusion: FASQ是首个超越FP16解码速度的压缩方法，在单张消费级GPU上实现了无需校准的压缩、连续可控的模型体积（27-49%原始FP16大小）、灵活的精度-性能权衡，以及高效的实时推理能力。其核心创新在于将乘积量化应用于LLM权重矩阵，通过子向量大小和码本基数的两个参数连续调节压缩比例，同时设计了专门的CUDA内核（无LUT的direct-compute GEMV用于解码、基于LUT的double-buffered GEMM用于预填充）以确保推理效率。

Abstract: Compressing large language models (LLMs) for deployment on commodity GPUs remains challenging: conventional scalar quantization is limited to fixed bit-widths (e.g., 8/4/3-bit), offers only a few discrete compression points, and typically requires calibration data. We present FASQ (Flexible Accelerated Subspace Quantization), a calibration-free framework that applies product quantization to LLM weight matrices. By tuning two parameters, sub-vector size and codebook cardinality, FASQ exposes a continuous design space spanning 27-49% of the original FP16 model size, filling compression gaps that fixed-bit schemes cannot reach. On Meta-Llama-3-8B, FASQ surpasses 4-bit GPTQ and AWQ in accuracy (67.1-67.7 avg.) at 37-42% model size, with consistent results on Qwen3-8B and Qwen3.5-9B-Base. To make product quantization practical at inference time, we design custom CUDA kernels: a LUT-free direct-compute GEMV for decode and an output-stationary double-buffered LUT GEMM for prefill, both with split-K parallelism. On an RTX~3090, FASQ achieves 45.2 tok/s decode at effective 4-bit (2.56x memory reduction) and 51.8 tok/s at effective 3-bit (2.80x), both surpassing FP16 tensor-core performance (43.9 tok/s) and delivering 1.6 to 1.8x the throughput of AWQ, 2.5 to 2.5x of GPTQ, and 4.3 to 5x of RTN. FASQ is the only compressed method that accelerates decode beyond FP16, offering calibration-free compression, continuous size-quality trade-offs, and real-time inference on a single consumer GPU.

</details>


### [144] [HERCULES: Hardware-Efficient, Robust, Continual Learning Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04103)
*Matteo Gambella,Fabrizio Pittorino,Manuel Roveri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出HERCULES框架，系统综述了面向边缘AI的神经架构搜索(NAS)方法，涵盖效率、鲁棒性和持续学习三个核心目标，并定义了实现这些目标的"十二项艰巨任务"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NAS仅关注硬件感知效率，已无法满足现实部署需求。现代边缘AI NAS需要同时满足三重目标：资源受限环境下的效率、环境变化下的鲁棒性、以及连续任务适应能力（无灾难性遗忘）。现有研究往往孤立处理这些目标，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 1) 提出三维度NAS方法分类学；2) 整合现有方法为HERCULES统一框架；3) 定义"十二项艰巨任务"作为多目标NAS研究的路线图；4) 识别现有研究的关键缺口。

Result: 建立了效率-鲁棒性-持续学习三维度的NAS方法分类体系，揭示这三个维度虽常被独立研究，但实际相互增强；提出从算法、架构到软硬件协同设计的综合路线图。

Conclusion: 实现"可部署终身学习AI系统"需要在搜索空间探索与多目标NAS计算成本之间取得平衡，需整合算法、架构和硬件-软件协同设计三个层面的创新。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) has emerged as a powerful framework for automatically discovering neural architectures that balance accuracy and efficiency. However, as AI transitions from static benchmarks to real-world deployment, the traditional focus on hardware-aware efficiency is no longer sufficient. We observe that modern NAS methods, especially those that target edge AI, are evolving to address a triple objective: Efficiency, Robustness, and Continual Learning. While efficiency ensures feasibility in resource-constrained environments, robustness guarantees reliability under environmental variabilities, and continual learning enables adaptation to sequential tasks without catastrophic forgetting. We propose a taxonomy of NAS approaches through this triple lens, distinguishing between methods targeting resource optimization, environmental resilience, and architectural plasticity. This unified perspective reveals that these axes, though often studied in isolation, are mutually reinforcing. Building on this taxonomy, we map the current landscape of these NAS methods into a new framework called Hardware-Efficient, Robust, and ContinUal LEarning Search (HERCULES). We define the desiderata, the twelve labours of HERCULES, addressing the non-trivial challenge of balancing an adequate search-space exploration with the immense computational costs of a multi-objective NAS, accounting for these crucial objectives of current AI systems. By identifying critical gaps in existing research, this survey outlines a roadmap toward integrated algorithmic, architectural, and hardware-software co-design for truly deployable, lifelong-learning AI systems.

</details>


### [145] [Simultaneous CNN Approximation on Manifolds with Applications to Boundary Value Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04126)
*Hanfei Zhou,Lei Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper develops convolutional neural network (CNN) methods for simultaneous approximation and elliptic boundary value problems on compact Riemannian manifolds. We establish simultaneous Sobolev approximation results for single- and multichannel CNNs, showing that manifold functions and their derivatives can be approximated with rates governed by the intrinsic dimension and the smoothness gap, rather than by the ambient dimension, thereby mitigating the curse of dimensionality. Building on this approximation theory, we propose a physics-informed CNN (PICNN) framework specially designed for boundary value problems. The main numerical issue is a boundary-norm mismatch: standard PINNs usually impose boundary data through low-order, often L2-type, penalties, whereas elliptic stability requires Sobolev trace control. We address this by introducing a spectral boundary loss based on the boundary Laplace-Beltrami operator, which represents trace errors as weighted frequency energies and relates truncation error to boundary eigenvalue decay. This avoids smooth auxiliary constructions required by exact boundary enforcement and singular double integrals arising in Sobolev-Slobodeckij penalties, while enabling implementations based on Fast Fourier Transforms (FFTs) or precomputed spectral bases on structured boundaries. Numerical experiments demonstrate improved accuracy, convergence, and stability over standard PINNs.

</details>


### [146] [Constrained Extreme Gradient Boosting for Adapting Reduced-Order Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04130)
*Melika Baghi,Xiao Liu,Kamran Paynabar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出cXGBoost框架，利用Grassmann流形几何表示和梯度提升树回归预测参数相关的POD基函数，通过范数约束保证逆映射有效性和子空间几何结构完整性。实验验证表明该方法能准确预测参数依赖基函数，在非线性区间具有良好的鲁棒性，适用于高维参数化系统的可扩展可靠降阶建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: High-fidelity simulations (CFD, FEA) are prohibitively expensive for parametric studies, optimization, and real-time control. Projection-based reduced-order models (ROMs) reduce cost but suffer performance degradation under parameter variation, necessitating adaptive basis construction approaches.

Method: Proposes Constrained Extreme Gradient Boosting (cXGBoost), a constrained ensemble learning framework for predicting Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) bases as functions of system parameters. Leverages Grassmann manifold geometric representation of subspaces mapped to Euclidean space for efficient regression using gradient boosting trees. Imposes norm constraint during training to ensure valid inverse mapping and preserve geometric structure of predicted subspaces.

Result: Method accurately predicts parameter-dependent bases while maintaining robustness across nonlinear regimes, validated on four numerical examples including fluid dynamics and wave propagation problems.

Conclusion: Proposed cXGBoost framework demonstrates potential for scalable and reliable reduced-order modeling of high-dimensional parametric systems by combining geometric learning with constrained ensemble methods. Evaluated on fluid dynamics and wave propagation problems, showing robust performance across nonlinear regimes.

Abstract: High-fidelity simulations, such as computational fluid dynamics and finite element analysis, are essential for modeling complex engineering systems but are often prohibitively expensive for tasks including parametric studies, optimization, and real-time control. Projection-based reduced-order models (ROMs) alleviate this cost by projecting the governing dynamics onto low-dimensional subspaces. However, their performance can deteriorate under parameter variation, motivating the need for adaptive basis construction.
