In this work, we propose a constrained ensemble learning framework, termed Constrained Extreme Gradient Boosting (cXGBoost), for predicting Proper Orthogonal Decomposition (POD) bases as functions of system parameters. The approach leverages a geometric representation of subspaces on the Grassmann manifold, which are mapped to a Euclidean space to enable efficient regression using gradient boosting trees. A norm constraint is imposed during training to ensure the validity of the inverse mapping and preserve the geometric structure of the predicted subspaces. The proposed method is evaluated on four numerical examples, including fluid dynamics and wave propagation problems, demonstrating its ability to accurately predict parameter-dependent bases while maintaining robustness across nonlinear regimes. These results highlight the potential of combining geometric learning with constrained ensemble methods for scalable and reliable reduced-order modeling of high-dimensional parametric systems.

</details>


### [147] [Enabling Real-Time Training of a Wildfire-to-Smoke Map with Multilinear Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04164)
*Zachary Morrow,Joseph Crockett,John D. Jakeman,Dan J. Krofcheck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A computationally efficient data-driven approach using multilinear operators and PCA to predict smoke concentration fields from time-since-ignition, achieving speedups of 2x+ over traditional methods while maintaining accuracy.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Accurately forecasting smoke impacts over long time scales is critical for public health and grid management, but full coupled fire-atmosphere simulations are computationally infeasible for many scenarios, while simpler fire models lack smoke transport capabilities.

Method: Data-driven multilinear operators: (1) PCA to decompose time-since-ignition and smoke concentration fields; (2) Learn a mapping from powers of input coefficients to output coefficients; (3) Forward prediction in <1ms per call on CPU.

Result: On aerosol optical depth proxy: achieves equal accuracy to Monte Carlo sampling with <50% of coupled model calls. On smoke detection (Upper Rio Grande Watershed, 2015 Australia bushfire): IoU=65%, AUC=0.95 (vs baseline IoU=15%, AUC=0.61); training <30s CPU, inference <1ms.

Conclusion: The proposed multilinear operator approach achieves comparable accuracy to Monte Carlo sampling while requiring significantly fewer coupled model calls, and substantially outperforms existing smoke classification methods (IoU: 65% vs 15%, AUC: 0.95 vs 0.61).

Abstract: Wildfires are a major producer of fine particulate matter, impacting human health and the electrical grid. Accurately forecasting smoke impacts over long time scales incorporates fuel treatment strategies, natural fuel succession, and stochastic events like lightning strikes. However, predicting smoke for each fuel distribution with a forward simulation of a coupled fire-atmosphere model is computationally infeasible. Moreover, relatively simple fire models are tractable to run in many long-time scenarios but do not capture smoke transport. We use data-driven multilinear operators to predict a smoke concentration field from knowledge of the time since ignition for two quantities of interest: aerosol optical depth and smoke detection. Our method first computes the principal components of time-since-ignition and smoke concentration fields and then learns a map from powers of the input coefficients to the output coefficients. We apply our learned operator to smoke prediction in the Upper Rio Grande Watershed. After collecting training data, learning the approximation weights on a CPU takes less than 30 seconds, and each forward call takes less than 1 ms. On a proxy for aerosol optical depth, we obtain equal accuracy to Monte Carlo sampling with fewer than half as many coupled model calls. For smoke detection, we obtain an intersection-over-union (IoU) of 65% and an area under the receiver operating characteristic curve (AUC) of 0.95 on holdout data. Our method is significantly more accurate than the most similar published smoke classifier, which obtains an IoU and AUC of 0.15 and 0.61, respectively, on a 2015 bushfire in Australia.

</details>


### [148] [A Provably Convergent and Practical Algorithm for Gromov--Wasserstein Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04175)
*Ling Liang,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对大规模Gromov-Wasserstein最优传输（GWOT）的非凸优化问题，提出了一种带有可验证性条件的非精确投影梯度框架，证明了其收敛性，为GWOT提供了既简洁可扩展又具有严格收敛保证的实用方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模GWOT面临两大挑战：(1)目标函数非凸；(2)投影到传输多面体只能近似求解。现有投影梯度实现与收敛理论之间存在差距——理论分析通常假设精确投影，但实际难以实现。

Method: 针对平方损失GWOT，提出非精确投影梯度框架，采用基于可行性残差的可验证非精确投影条件。该条件可直接计算，无需知道精确投影点。基于此可实现条件证明收敛性。

Result: 在可验证条件下，证明序列收敛到稳定点（需温和的容差衰减条件），或至少子序列收敛。该方法兼具投影梯度法的简洁性和稀疏性，同时提供严格的收敛保证。

Conclusion: 投影梯度法可成为GWOT的一种有原则、可扩展且可靠的方法，弥合了理论收敛保证与实际计算可行性之间的差距。

Abstract: Gromov--Wasserstein optimal transport (GWOT) aligns metric measure spaces by matching their within-domain relational structures, but large-scale GWOT remains challenging because its objective is nonconvex and projection onto the transport polytope is often solved only approximately in practice. This leads to a gap between practical projected-gradient implementations and convergence theory, which typically assumes exact projections. For squared-loss GWOT, we propose an inexact projected-gradient framework with a verifiable feasibility-residual-based inexact condition for the projection subproblem. This condition is directly computable and avoids unknown quantities such as the exact projection point. Under this implementable condition, we prove subsequential convergence to stationary points and, with a mild tolerance-decay condition, convergence of the whole sequence. The resulting method retains the simplicity and sparsity of projected-gradient schemes while providing rigorous convergence guarantees, turning projected-gradient methods into a principled and scalable approach for GWOT with provable reliability.

</details>


### [149] [Constraint-Enhanced Reinforcement Learning Based on Dynamic Decoupled Spherical Radial Squashing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04185)
*Qijun Liao,Zhaoxin Yu,Jue Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DD-SRad方法解决强化学习部署中异构关节执行器速率约束问题，通过为每个执行器独立计算位置自适应半径，实现对真实可行域的紧密对齐。该方法以零运行时求解开销支持精确策略梯度反传，在MuJoCo和IsaacLab仿真中验证了最高任务收益和零约束违反，并提供从硬件规格表到安全部署的完整路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: When deploying RL policies to physical robots, actuator rate constraints (hard limits on joint movement speed per control step) are unavoidable. These constraints are highly heterogeneous across joints due to differences in motor inertia, power bandwidth, and transmission stiffness. Existing methods (QP projection and spherical parameterization) impose isotropic ball-shaped constraints, which exponentially under-cover the true feasible set as heterogeneity increases, leading to suboptimal policy performance.

Method: The paper proposes Dynamic Decoupled Spherical Radial Squashing (DD-SRad), which resolves the mismatch between isotropic constraints and heterogeneous per-joint limits by computing a position-adaptive radius independently for each actuator. This approach achieves tight alignment with the true per-joint feasible region (high-dimensional box in action-increment space). DD-SRad supports exact policy gradient backpropagation with zero runtime solver overhead.

Result: On MuJoCo benchmarks, DD-SRad achieves the highest task return with zero constraint violation, matching the unconstrained upper bound. It demonstrates 30%-50% improvement in constraint-space coverage compared to spherical baselines. High-fidelity IsaacLab simulations with Unitree H1 and G1 humanoid robots validate end-to-end optimality parameterized directly from official joint specifications, confirming safe deployment from hardware datasheets.

Conclusion: DD-SRad provides a theoretically sound and practically validated solution for heterogeneous actuator rate constraints in physical robot deployment. The method enables exact policy gradient computation with zero runtime overhead, achieving optimal task performance while guaranteeing constraint satisfaction. The validated pathway from hardware datasheets to safe RL deployment represents a significant advance for real-world robotics applications.

Abstract: When deploying reinforcement learning policies to physical robots, actuator rate constraints -- hard limits on how fast each joint can move per control step -- are unavoidable. These limits vary substantially across joints due to differences in motor inertia, power bandwidth, and transmission stiffness, creating pronounced heterogeneity that existing methods fail to handle geometrically: the per-joint feasible region forms a high-dimensional box in action-increment space, yet QP projection and spherical parameterization methods impose isotropic ball-shaped constraints, exponentially under-covering the true feasible set as heterogeneity grows. This paper proposes Dynamic Decoupled Spherical Radial Squashing (DD-SRad), which resolves this mismatch by computing a position-adaptive radius independently for each actuator, achieving tight alignment with the true per-joint feasible region. DD-SRad satisfies per-step hard constraints with probability~1, preserves well-conditioned gradients throughout training, and admits exact policy gradient backpropagation with zero runtime solver overhead. MuJoCo benchmark experiments demonstrate the highest task return at zero constraint violation -- matching the unconstrained upper bound -- with 30%--50% improvement in constraint-space coverage over spherical baselines. High-fidelity IsaacLab simulations with Unitree H1 and G1 humanoid robots confirm end-to-end optimality parameterized directly from official joint specifications, validating a systematic pathway from hardware datasheets to safe deployment.

</details>


### [150] [Deep Wave Network for Modeling Multi-Scale Physical Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04198)
*Alexander I. Khrabry,Edward A. Startsev,Andrew T. Powis,Igor D. Kaganovich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出Deep Wave Network (DW-Net)架构，通过串联堆叠多个U-Net编码器-解码器波并利用波内波间skip connections增加有效深度，系统性探索了深度对精度-成本权衡的影响。在多个2D和3D流体力学基准测试中，DW-Net持续改进Pareto前沿，在低误差区域实现最多3倍的训练加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理科学应用中，深度学习模型常在固定尺寸下比较或分离准确率与计算成本进行评估，这种方式具有误导性，因为架构在宽度和深度变化时表现出不同的精度-成本缩放特性；U-Net架构通常仅调整宽度而固定深度，限制了系统性探索。

Method: 提出Deep Wave Network (DW-Net)架构，通过串联堆叠多个编码器-解码器"波"来增加有效深度；波内部和波之间均设skip connections实现跨尺度细化；训练多个宽度变体并比较精度-GPU时间的Pareto前沿。

Result: DW-Net模型持续改进了单波U-Net的Pareto前沿，可在对等成本下获得更高精度或等精度下降低成本，在相同训练设置下低误差区域的训练时间最多减少3倍。

Conclusion: DW-Net通过堆叠多个编码器-解码器波实现了更优的精度-成本权衡，在保持相同训练设置的情况下，低误差区域的训练时间最多可减少3倍。

Abstract: Performance of deep learning models is strongly governed by architectural capacity, with width and depth as primary controls. However, in physical-science applications, models are often compared at a single fixed size or by separating accuracy and computational cost, which can be misleading since architectures exhibit different accuracy-cost scaling as width and depth vary. This issue is particularly relevant for U-Net-type encoder-decoder models, widely used for multi-scale gas, fluid, and plasma dynamics due to their ability to represent features across spatial scales. A U-Net constructs a multi-resolution representation via an encoder that progressively reduces spatial resolution, followed by a decoder that restores it for prediction. Skip connections link corresponding encoder and decoder features, preserving fine-scale information and improving optimization. In practice, U-Net width is routinely tuned, while depth is typically kept fixed (a set number of down/up-sampling stages with few convolutions per stage), limiting systematic exploration of depth for improving the accuracy-cost trade-off. We address this limitation by increasing effective depth through stacking multiple encoder-decoder "waves" in series, with skip connections both within and across waves to enable progressive cross-scale refinement. We call this architecture a Deep Wave Network (DW-Net). Training data, optimization, and schedules are kept identical across models. Instead of evaluating single configurations, we train multiple width variants of each architecture and compare accuracy vs. GPU time Pareto fronts. Across several 2D and 3D flow benchmarks, DW-Net models consistently improve the Pareto frontier over single-wave U-Nets, achieving higher accuracy at matched cost or similar accuracy at reduced cost, and reaching low-error regimes with up to 3x less training time under identical training settings.

</details>


### [151] [Sequential Strategic Classification with Multi-Stage Selective Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04202)
*Ziyuan Huang,Lina Alkarmi,Mingyan Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper introduces a sequential multi-stage model of strategic classification where agents take improvement or gaming actions across multiple classification levels with increasing difficulty. The work characterizes optimal agent behavior and provides classifier design principles that incentivize genuine effort over manipulation in the long run.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Prior work on strategic classification shows fundamental impossibility results when only designing classifiers, and focuses on one-shot or repeated interactions with the same classifier. Real-world decisions are sequential with multiple potentially different classifiers.

Method: Multi-stage stochastic model with selective classifiers that can abstain at low confidence, distinguishing improvement actions (authentic) from gaming actions (superficial), with promotion/demotion dynamics across classification levels.

Result: Fully characterized optimal instantaneous action under selective classifiers; compared long-term properties and utility of no-improvement vs no-gaming myopic policies; derived design principles favoring genuine effort.

Conclusion: The paper provides design principles for sequences of classifiers that incentivize genuine effort over the long run, benefiting the no-gaming policy.

Abstract: Strategic classification studies the problem where self-interested individuals or agents manipulate their response to obtain favorable decision outcomes made by classifiers, typically turning to dishonest actions when they are less costly than genuine efforts. Prior works have demonstrated a fundamental inability to get out of this conundrum by only focusing on the design of a classifier. We note that prior work also heavily focuses on either one-shot settings or repeated interaction with the same classifier. Real-world decision making is often multi-stage, involving a sequence of potentially different classifiers as an agent progresses. This paper introduces a sequential, stochastic, multi-stage model of strategic classification, by capturing how agents adapt their behavior, through improvement actions (enhancing both observable features and true attributes) and gaming actions (enhancing only observable features), over multiple levels of classification with increasing difficulty as well as reward. For each level, we adopt a selective classifier that can abstain from making a prediction at low confidence. Consequently, a positive (resp. negative) outcome leads to promotion (resp. demotion) of the agent to the next higher (resp. lower) level, while abstention keeps the agent at the same level. We fully characterize the agent's optimal instantaneous action under selective classifiers and compare the long-term properties and utility of the agent repeatedly following an optimal myopic policy of either no-improvement (never choose the improvement action) or no-gaming (never choose the gaming action). We further examine design principles over the sequence of classifiers that yield higher long-term utility for the latter policy, thereby effectively incentivizing genuine effort in the long run.

</details>


### [152] [Climate-based Pre-screening of Self-sustaining Regreening Opportunities in Drylands: A Case Study for Saudi Arabia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04206)
*Katja Froehlich,Jonathan Klein,Ibrahim S. Elbasyoni,Julian D. Hunt,Yoshihide Wada,Dominik L. Michels*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究针对旱地生态恢复中的选址问题，提出了一个整合气候与遥感数据的可扩展预筛选框架。该框架使用机器学习生成气候适宜性评分，结合植被指数识别出沙特阿拉伯13个优先恢复地点，恢复潜力为当前植被覆盖的2.5倍，为水资源受限地区的生态恢复提供成本效益高的选址方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旱地大规模生态恢复工作常依赖长期灌溉，在水资源短缺地区缺乏可持续性。关键挑战是识别人类活动干预较少的区域中本土植被可自然维持的地点，以减少昂贵的野外调查成本。

Method: 开发了一个可扩展的预筛选框架，整合气候数据和遥感数据。利用机器学习模型（基于专家策划的参考站点训练）生成了气候适宜性评分（CSS）。使用沙特阿拉伯多年ERA5-Land数据生成了国家尺度的预测图，结合植被指数识别气候适宜但植被覆盖不足的区域，通过多标准筛选确定优先区域。

Result: 通过多标准筛选确定了13个优先恢复地点。气候相似的完整生态系统提供了恢复目标基准，表明植被覆盖平均增加2.5倍是现实可行的恢复目标。

Conclusion: 该框架缩小了搜索空间，降低了成本，并为水资源受限地区的生态恢复规划提供了支持。

Abstract: Large-scale restoration in drylands is widely promoted to address land degradation and biodiversity loss, yet many efforts rely on long-term irrigation, limiting sustainability in water-scarce regions. A key challenge is identifying locations where native vegetation can persist without intensive management while minimizing costly field campaigns. A scalable pre-screening framework is presented that integrates climate and remote sensing data to enable cost-efficient site selection in arid environments using Saudi Arabia as a case study. A Climate Suitability Score (CSS), derived from machine learning models trained on expert-curated reference sites, captures complex climatic dependencies on vegetation persistence. Using multi-year ERA5-Land data for Saudi Arabia, national-scale prediction maps are generated and combined with vegetation indices to identify areas where climate is favorable, but vegetation remains underdeveloped. Multi-criteria screening reduces candidates to thirteen priority locations. Climatically analogous intact ecosystems provide benchmarks for restoration targets and indicate that an average 2.5 fold increase in vegetation coverage is a realistic target for restoration efforts. Overall, this approach narrows the search space, reduces costs, and supports resilient ecosystem recovery planning in water-limited regions.

</details>


### [153] [Predict-then-Diffuse: Adaptive Response Length for Compute-Budgeted Inference in Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04215)
*Michael Rottoli,Subhankar Roy,Stefano Paraboschi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Predict-then-Diffuse通过预测响应长度来解决扩散语言模型的固定长度约束问题，在保持输出质量的同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D-LLM虽然能实现完全并行的token生成，提供显著的吞吐量优势和GPU利用率，但需要预先确定固定响应长度。若响应长度设置过大，会在无意义的padding token上浪费计算资源；若设置过小，则会导致输出截断，需重新计算引入不可预测的延迟峰值。

Method: 提出了Predict-then-Diffuse框架，核心组件为AdaRLP自适应响应长度预测器，根据输入查询预测最优响应长度。同时引入数据驱动的安全机制，以轻微的padding开销换取对预测不足的保护。多数据集验证表明该方法显著降低计算成本（FLOPs），且对倾斜数据分布具有鲁棒性。

Result: Predict-then-Diffuse相比默认D-LLM推理机制和基于启发式的基线方法，显著降低了计算成本（FLOPs），且对倾斜数据分布具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Predict-then-Diffuse通过先预测响应长度再执行推理的方式，有效平衡了计算成本与输出质量，为D-LLM的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Large Language Models (D-LLMs) represent a promising frontier in generative AI, offering fully parallel token generation that can lead to significant throughput advantages and superior GPU utilization over traditional autoregressive paradigm. However, this parallelism is constrained by the requirement of a fixed-size response length prior to generation. This architectural limitation imposes a severe trade-off: oversized response length results in computational waste on semantically meaningless padding tokens, while undersized response length cause output truncation requiring costly re-computations that introduce unpredictable latency spikes. To tackle this issue, we propose Predict-then-Diffuse, a simple and model-agnostic framework, that enables compute-budgeted inference per input query by first estimating the response length and then using it to run inference with D-LLM. At its core lies a Adaptive Response Length Predictor (AdaRLP) auxiliary predictor that predicts the optimal response length given an input query. As a measure against under-predicting the response length and re-running inference with a higher response length, we introduce a data-driven safety mechanism, which trades a negligible padding overhead. As a whole, our framework limits the significant waste of computation on padding tokens and preserves output quality. Experimental validation on multiple datasets demonstrate that Predict-then-Diffuse significantly reduces computational costs (FLOP) compared to the default D-LLM inference mechanism and baselines based on heuristics, while being robust to skewed data distributions.

</details>


### [154] [Jordan-RoPE: Non-Semisimple Relative Positional Encoding via Complex Jordan Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04217)
*Yaobo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Relative positional encodings determine which functions of query-key lag can enter the primitive attention logit. RoPE supplies a rotary phase, while ALiBi supplies an additive distance bias. Motivated by group-theoretic views of linear translation-invariant positional encodings, we study a non-semisimple case in which a complex rotary eigenvalue and a nilpotent response live in the same defective Jordan block. The resulting relative operator generates oscillatory-polynomial features such as $e^{-γd}\cos(ωd)$, $e^{-γd}\sin(ωd)$, $d e^{-γd}\cos(ωd)$, and $d e^{-γd}\sin(ωd)$, for causal lag $d=i-j\geq 0$. Thus the construction realizes a distance-modulated phase basis $d e^{iωd}$, rather than merely adding a separate distance channel to RoPE.
  We formulate Exact Jordan-RoPE as a non-semisimple one-parameter representation, give its real block form, and specify the contragredient query action required by non-orthogonal positional maps. We also distinguish this exact representation from stabilized variants whose bounded shear improves numerical behavior but breaks the exact group law. Kernel-level diagnostics and a Jordan-friendly synthetic language-model task show that the coupled Jordan basis is useful when the target contains distance-modulated phase interactions. On a small WikiText-103 byte language model, a scaled-exact variant improves over RoPE and direct-sum baselines within the Jordan family, while RoPE+ALiBi remains strongest overall. The evidence is structural rather than a broad performance claim.

</details>


### [155] [Adaptive Consensus in LLM Ensembles via Sequential Evidence Accumulation: Automatic Budget Identification and Calibrated Commit Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04236)
*Roberto Medina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Large Language Model ensembles improve reasoning accuracy up to a performance boundary; beyond it, additional deliberation degrades accuracy. Static-budget methods cannot detect this boundary. Extended-thinking architectures compound the problem: a wrong answer after 120k tokens is indistinguishable from a correct one. We introduce DASE (Deliberative Adaptive Stopping Ensemble), a stopping heuristic for iterative ensemble deliberation that commits early on genuine consensus and applies a global-frequency fallback on fragmented evidence. Two configurations are evaluated: a persistence heuristic and DASE-Spatial (arena half-width W). Three contributions. (1) DASE produces a commit-type routing partition complementary to verbalized single-call confidence. On a contamination-controlled corpus (AIME 2010-2023, N=254, 3 seeds), a 120B ensemble achieves a 24.8 pp routing gap (right-wall 97.1% vs. left-wall 73.6%), statistically equivalent to Opus 4.6 Standard verbalized confidence at coverage-matched threshold (25.7 pp gap; bootstrap CI on difference: [-12.0, +10.3] pp, p=0.873). The two mechanisms disagree on 27% of routing assignments, establishing them as complements rather than substitutes; every DASE decision is accompanied by a machine-readable deliberation record. (2) Adaptive stopping, not injection bandwidth, drives accuracy gains. On AIME-300, bandwidth accounts for only 0.3 pp (ns); on GPQA-Extended, 4.4 pp bandwidth versus 5.0 pp stopping effect. DASE-Spatial ties Debate-Dense at its optimal budget using one-tenth the injection bandwidth and identifies that budget automatically; W=8 (65.0%) significantly outperforms W=4 (59.3%) on AIME-300 (adj p=0.0042). (3) Injection-based methods exhibit a retrospective accuracy-vs-inference inverted-U on both benchmarks; this pattern is hypothesis-generating for future work.

</details>


### [156] [Hierarchical Support Vector State Partitioning for Distilling Black Box Reinforcement Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04254)
*Senne Deproost,Mehrdad Asadi,Ann Nowé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SVSP方法，通过线性SVM将状态空间分割为多个可解释的子策略，在大幅减少子策略数量（82.1%）的同时提升了性能（+7.4% over VSP, +2.8% over TD3），为黑盒强化学习策略的蒸馏提供了一种更灵活的可解释方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的黑盒强化学习策略难以解释，需要一种方法来提取可人类理解的子策略表示，同时保持性能。

Method: 使用线性支持向量机对状态-动作对的蒸馏数据集进行分割，从而构建原始策略的紧凑且结构化的表示。

Result: SVSP相比VSP平均回报提升7.4%，相比原始TD3策略提升2.8%，且所需子策略数量比VSP减少82.1%。

Conclusion: SVSP为知识蒸馏提供了一种更灵活的形式，使得决策边界和替代模型都可以在原始黑盒行为的边界内进行选择。该方法为可解释AI和策略压缩领域提供了新的思路。

Abstract: We introduce State Vector Space Partitioning (SVSP), a novel method to mimic a black box reinforcement learning policy using a set of human-interpretable subpolicies. By partitioning a distillation dataset of state action pairs with linear support vector machine splits, SVSP constructs a compact and structured representation of the original policy. Our method improves mean return by +7.4\% over previous critic driven state partitioning attempts such as Voronoi State Partitioning (VSP) and +2.8\% over the original TD3 policy, while reducing the number of required subpolicies against VSP by 82.1\%. Our results pave the path towards a more flexible form of distillation where both the decision boundary and surrogate models can be chosen within a margin of the original black box behavior.

</details>


### [157] [HUGO-CS: A Hybrid-Labeled, Uncertainty-Aware, General-Purpose, Observational Dataset for Cold Spray](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04257)
*Stephen Price,Kyle Miller,Marco Musto,Kenneth Kroenlein,James Saal,Kyle Tsaknopoulos,Elke A. Rundensteiner,Danielle L. Cote*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HUGO-CS数据集，包含4,383个冷喷涂实验（144个特征，来自1,124篇文献），比之前最大数据集（137个样本）大30倍。该数据集通过HUGO框架（结合LLM自动标注与人工标注）提取，并发布于GitHub开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷喷涂技术因固相制造能力广泛应用于零件修复与制造，但工艺优化困难，原因包括：参数相互依赖、缺乏大规模机器可读数据支撑建模。现有文献虽包含大量相关实验，但结果报告不一致（常在表格和图表中）且单位不统一，难以规模化利用。

Method: 设计并利用HUGO（Hybrid-labeled, Uncertainty-aware, General-purpose, Observational）提取框架：结合LLM自动标注与有针对性的人工标注细化处理。采用层级风险缓解（HRM）策略路由LLM输出，将高错误风险记录送往人工审核，低风险记录自动标注。后处理包括：合并分类描述符、将原料化学成分映射为结构化连续成分、标准化各单位。

Result: 4,383个实验数据点中，1,765个经手工标注。完全手动提取平均每文档需91分钟，HUGO框架显著提升效率。数据集包含144个特征，来自1,124个文献源，规模达此前最大数据集的30倍。

Conclusion: HUGO框架成功实现从科学文献中大规模提取实验数据，HUGO-CS数据集为冷喷涂工艺建模与优化提供了重要资源。所有代码及完整数据集以CC-BY许可证发布于GitHub。

Abstract: Cold spraying is an increasingly common approach for repairing and manufacturing components due to its solid-state manufacturing capabilities. However, process optimization remains difficult due to many interdependent parameters and the lack of large-scale, machine-readable data to support modeling. While the scientific literature contains many relevant experiments, results are inconsistently reported (often in tables and figures) and use non-uniform units, limiting utilization at scale. To address these limitations, this work presents HUGO-CS, a literature-derived dataset of 4,383 cold-spray experiments with 144 features from 1,124 sources, exceeding the previous largest dataset (137 samples) by 30x. With completely manual extraction requiring an average of 91 minutes per document, this work designs and leverages a Hybrid-labeled, Uncertainty-aware, General-purpose, Observational extraction framework, called HUGO, to support this extraction. HUGO combines automated LLM-based labeling with targeted manual label refinement to handle this experimental result extraction process from scientific literature. To balance labeling efficiency with extraction accuracy, HUGO introduces a Hierarchical Risk Mitigation (HRM) to route LLM outputs with a high risk of potential errors for manual review, while retaining low-risk records as auto-labeled. Lastly, HUGO post-processing consolidates categorical descriptors, maps reported feedstock chemistries into structured continuous compositions, and normalizes units across sources. Of the 4,383 reported experiments, 1,765 are hand-labeled, providing a high-quality labeled subset for benchmarking, error analysis, and higher-fidelity data points. All code to replicate this work, along with the complete HUGO-CS dataset, are released under a CC-BY license at https://github.com/sprice134/HUGO.

</details>


### [158] [Explaining and Preventing Alignment Collapse in Iterative RLHF](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04266)
*Etienne Gauthier,Francis Bach,Michael I. Jordan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究迭代部署中RLHF的反馈循环问题，发现标准RLHF会因策略过度利用奖励模型的盲点而导致"对齐崩溃"。提出前瞻策略优化(FPO)方法，通过正则化策略对奖励模型参数更新的影响来缓解此问题，并在Llama-3.2-1B上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF通常假设奖励模型(RM)是静态的，但在迭代部署中，策略生成的数据会重新训练RM，形成反馈循环。标准RLHF完全忽略了策略对RM未来参数的潜在影响。

Method: 基于Stackelberg博弈框架，将策略的真实优化梯度分解为标准策略梯度和一个捕捉策略对RM未来参数影响的参数 steering 项。通过一阶近似实现FPO的可扩展版本。

Result: 标准迭代RLHF会遭受"对齐崩溃"：策略系统性地利用RM的盲点，产生低质量但高奖励的输出，而这些输出会强化其所利用的错误。FPO能有效防止对齐崩溃。

Conclusion: FPO通过恢复缺失的steering项，在受控环境和LLM对齐 pipeline（Llama-3.2-1B）上都证明了能防止对齐崩溃，为迭代RLHF提供了有效的机制设计干预方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) typically assumes a static or non-strategic reward model (RM). In iterative deployment, however, the policy generates the data on which the RM is retrained, creating a feedback loop. Building on the Stackelberg game formulation of this interaction, we derive an analytical decomposition of the policy's true optimization gradient into a standard policy gradient and a parameter-steering term that captures the policy's influence on the RM's future parameters. We show that standard iterative RLHF, which drops this steering term entirely, suffers from alignment collapse: the policy systematically exploits the RM's blind spots, producing low-quality, high-reward outputs whose feedback reinforces the very errors it exploits. To mitigate this, we propose foresighted policy optimization (FPO), a mechanism-design intervention that restores the missing steering term by regularizing the policy's parameter-steering effect on RM updates. We instantiate FPO via a scalable first-order approximation and demonstrate that it prevents alignment collapse on both controlled environments and an LLM alignment pipeline using Llama-3.2-1B.

</details>


### [159] [A Mean Curvature Approach to Boundary Detection: Geometric Insights for Unsupervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04274)
*Alexandre L. M. Levada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MCBP是一种基于几何机器学习的新型边界检测框架，通过计算局部k近邻补丁的离散形状算子来估计点态平均曲率，无需显式流形参数化。曲率自然对应聚类间过渡区域、几何不规则性和低密度界面。该方法采用自适应百分位阈值实现多尺度边界提取，并通过曲率驱动将数据分解为平滑子集和边界子集。实验表明该方法在复杂高维场景中显著提升聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维数据中精确边界检测是核心挑战，尤其在非线性和密度异质场景下。传统密度方法存在局限性，需要从几何角度重新建模数据边界结构。

Method: 基于形状算子的离散近似，从k近邻局部补丁估计曲率；采用自适应百分位阈值实现多尺度边界提取；通过曲率驱动分解为平滑区域和边界区域。

Result: 在合成数据集和真实数据集上显著提升聚类性能，尤其在复杂高维场景中效果突出；增强了下游无监督算法的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: MCBP为几何机器学习提供了具体贡献，证明了曲率感知分析可作为连接微分几何与数据驱动建模的统一范式。

Abstract: Accurate boundary detection in high-dimensional data remains a central challenge in unsupervised learning, particularly in the presence of non-linear structures and heterogeneous densities. In this work, we introduce Mean Curvature Boundary Points (MCBP), a novel geometric framework grounded in Geometric Machine Learning that departs from traditional density-based approaches by explicitly modeling the intrinsic curvature of the data manifold. The method relies on a discrete approximation of the shape operator, estimated from local k-nearest neighbor patches, to compute pointwise mean curvature without requiring explicit manifold parametrization. The key insight of MCBP is to use mean curvature as a principled descriptor of boundary structure: high-curvature regions naturally correspond to transitions between clusters, geometric irregularities, and low-density interfaces. This yields a unified geometric interpretation of boundary, outlier, and transition points. We further introduce an adaptive percentile-based thresholding scheme that enables multiscale boundary extraction without relying on ad hoc density parameters. Beyond detection, we propose a curvature-driven data decomposition that separates samples into smooth (low-curvature) and boundary (high-curvature) subsets, effectively acting as a non-linear geometric filtering mechanism. This representation enhances cluster separability and improves the robustness of downstream unsupervised algorithms. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that MCBP consistently improves clustering performance, particularly in complex and high-dimensional scenarios. These results position MCBP as a concrete contribution to Geometric Machine Learning, highlighting the potential of curvature-aware analysis as a unifying paradigm bridging differential geometry and data-driven modeling.

</details>


### [160] [Gradient Flow Structure and Quantitative Dynamics of Multi-Head Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04279)
*Ayan Pendharkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了多头自注意力机制的动态理论框架，证明在适当条件下自然的多头能量泛函沿平坦和球面动力学非递减，识别了径向阴影项作为单调性的关键障碍，并在线性化标量多头系统中推导了聚类临界逆温度的闭式解，发现异质头呈现超加性聚类率以及ReLU与softmax注意力在聚类时间上的分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自注意力聚类理论仅停留在单头设置，多头场景因几何干扰（头间干涉）无法沿用标准单调性论证，导致多个开放问题悬而未决。论文旨在构建统一的多头自注意力动力学理论，解释聚类与稳定性机制。

Method: (1) 定义多头能量泛函并分析其在平坦/球面动力学下的单调性；(2) 引入"径向阴影项"概念识别单调性障碍；(3) 提出保证单调性的充分条件并证明对近似正交性的鲁棒性；(4) 在等角 token 配置的标量多头简化系统下求解临界逆温度；(5) 建立熵产生恒等式追踪平衡过程。

Result: 关键结果：① 多头能量泛函在给定条件下非递增；② 径向阴影项（头输出在 token 方向上的投影）是破坏单头单调性的根本原因，即使在正交性假设下依然存在；③ 临界逆温度存在闭式表达式；④ 异质头展现超加性聚类率；⑤ 线性化动力学中 ReLU 注意力比 softmax 注意力聚类更快；⑥ 注意力熵在聚类过程中单调增加。

Conclusion: 研究提供了多头注意力动力学的统一视角，阐明了 transformer 模型中聚类与稳定性的底层机制，理论框架为理解现代 transformer 架构提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: Transformer self-attention can be interpreted as a gradient flow on the unit sphere, in which tokens evolve under softmax interaction potentials and tend to form clusters. While prior work has established clustering behavior for single-head attention, the multi-head setting remains less understood due to geometric interference between heads, which invalidates standard monotonicity arguments.
  In this work, we develop a theoretical framework for multi-head self-attention dynamics and resolve several open questions. We show that, under suitable conditions on the score matrices, a natural multi-head energy functional is non-decreasing along both flat and spherical dynamics. We identify the key obstruction to per-head monotonicity as radial shadow terms, which are projections of each head's output onto token directions, persisting even under orthogonality assumptions. We introduce a sufficient condition ensuring monotonicity and establish robustness to approximate orthogonality.
  In a simplified scalar-head regime with equiangular token configurations, we derive a closed-form expression for the critical inverse temperature governing clustering behavior, and show that heterogeneous heads exhibit super-additive clustering rates. In this regime, we also prove a separation in clustering time between ReLU and softmax attention in the linearized dynamics. Finally, we establish an entropy production identity and show that attention entropy increases monotonically toward equilibrium as clustering progresses.
  Our results provide a unified perspective on the dynamics of multi-head attention and clarify the mechanisms underlying clustering and stability in transformer models.

</details>


### [161] [Hardware-Aware Neural Feature Extraction for Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04282)
*Francesco Tosini,Simone Pedroni,Christian Veronesi,Pietro Bartoli,Marco Paracchini,Marco Marcon,Diana Trojaniello*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Gideon是一个硬件感知的神经特征提取器，通过知识蒸馏和可微分NAS在严格内存约束下搜索架构，并将量化稳定性作为首要优化目标。在微控制器STM32N6上实现111 fps推理速度和低于1.5 MB内存占用，INT8量化几乎不影响精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉SLAM是空间计算系统的核心组件，但将学习型局部特征提取器部署在微控制器级硬件上面临内存、带宽和量化约束的挑战。现有基于FLOPs的效率指标无法捕捉实际系统级瓶颈，导致现代神经描述符的实用部署受阻。

Method: 结合SuperPoint教师模型的关系知识蒸馏与可微分神经架构搜索(DNAS)，在严格内存和算子约束下优化；将量化稳定性和动态范围紧凑性作为核心设计目标；采用仿射层替代BatchNorm以提升INT8量化鲁棒性。

Result: 在STM32N6上实现9.003 ms单帧推理延迟(111 fps)，内存占用低于1.5 MB；INT8量化仅产生可忽略的性能损失，甚至在某些情况下匹配全精度表现。

Conclusion: 通过软硬件协同设计，成功在STM32N6微控制器上实现了111 fps推理速度、低于1.5 MB内存占用的鲁棒视觉特征提取，INT8量化几乎无性能损失，证明了在嵌入式硬件约束下实现鲁棒学习特征提取的可行性。

Abstract: Visual SLAM is a core component of spatial computing systems, yet deploying learned local feature extractors on microcontroller-class hardware remains challenging due to memory, bandwidth, and quantization constraints. While modern neural descriptors provide strong robustness, their practical adoption is often hindered by system-level bottlenecks that are not captured by FLOP-based efficiency metrics. In this work, we introduce Gideon, a hardware-aware neural feature extractor explicitly designed for resource-constrained devices. Our approach combines relational knowledge distillation from a SuperPoint teacher with differentiable neural architecture search (DNAS) under strict memory and operator constraints. Unlike conventional design pipelines, we treat quantization stability and dynamic-range compactness as first-class objectives. We show that architectural choices such as replacing Batch Normalization with affine layers significantly improve INT8 robustness, and that descriptor dimensionality directly governs quantization resilience. Deployed on STM32N6, Gideon achieves 9.003 ms inference time (111 fps) while remaining below a 1.5 MB memory footprint. Remarkably, INT8 quantization induces negligible degradation and occasionally matches full-precision performance. These results demonstrate that robust learned feature extraction can be reconciled with embedded hardware constraints through holistic hardware-algorithm co-design.

</details>


### [162] [Leveraging Pretrained Language Models as Energy Functions for Glauber Dynamics Text Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04291)
*Tarun Kathuria,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: We present a discrete diffusion-based language model using Glauber dynamics from statistical physics. Our main insight is that instead of trying to train a discrete state space diffusion model using Glauber dynamics with a uniform transition kernel as the forward process, one can set up an ``energy function'' based on pretrained causal/masked language models. When viewed as the stationary distribution, this energy function allows us to significantly improve the quality of the generated text. Incorporating UL2 as the pretrained model into our diffusion pipeline, we outperform prior diffusion based LMs and perform competitively with autoregressive models of comparable model sizes. Furthermore, our models are competitive with or outperform prior diffusion models and GPT-2 style auto-regressive models on zero-shot common sense reasoning tasks as well as planning and search tasks like Sudoku and Zebra puzzles.

</details>


### [163] [LLMs Uncertainty Quantification via Adaptive Conformal Semantic Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04295)
*Hamed Karimi,Vaishali Meyappan,Reza Samavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ACSE方法，通过语义聚类和共形校准来量化LLM在提示级别的输出不确定性，以解决现有方法忽略语义相似性导致的不确定性估计问题。在TriviaQA数据集上，AUROC达到0.88，显著优于基于token熵的0.65方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的过度自信（尤其在幻觉情况下）阻碍其在安全关键场景的部署，需要可靠的不确定性估计方法。现有的不确定性量化方法通常关注词法或概率度量，忽略了具有相似含义的不同响应之间的语义差异。

Method: 提出自适应共形语义熵(ACSE)方法，通过以下步骤量化不确定性：1)对同一提示的多个多样化响应进行语义聚类；2)基于每个聚类的语义特征自适应调整不确定性评分；3)使用共形校准应用决策规则来接受/拒绝提示，提供有限样本、无分布假设的误差率保证。

Result: 在多种LLM和数据集上的广泛实验表明，ACSE在判别性能、共形保证和概率校准指标上均一致优于最新的不确定性量化基线方法。在TriviaQA数据集上，AUROC达到0.88，相比token熵方法的0.65有显著提升。

Conclusion: ACSE通过语义分散度测量和自适应评分机制，能更准确地量化LLM输出不确定性，为安全部署LLM提供了理论保证和实践有效的解决方案。

Abstract: LLMs' overconfidence, particularly when hallucinating, poses a significant challenge for the deployment of the models in safety-critical settings and makes a reliable estimation of uncertainty necessary. Existing approaches for uncertainty quantification typically prioritize lexical or probabilistic measures; however, these techniques often ignore the semantic variance of different responses with similar meaning. In this paper, we propose Adaptive Conformal Semantic Entropy (ACSE), a method for estimating prompt-level uncertainty by adaptively measuring semantic dispersion in LLMs outputs. Our uncertainty scoring function is based on clustering semantic entropy of multiple diverse responses to the same prompt. The function adaptively adjusts the uncertainty score based on semantic features of each cluster. To ensure statistical reliability of our score, we use conformal calibration to apply a decision rule to accept/abstain the prompts, providing a finite-sample, distribution-free guarantee such that the error rate among the accepted responses remains bounded by a user-specified tolerance. Our extensive experimental evaluations using different LLMs and datasets, demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art uncertainty quantification baselines using discriminative performance, conformal guarantees, and probabilistic calibration indicators. As a highlight, for TriviaQA dataset, AUROC of our approach is 0.88 compared to 0.65 produced by the token entropy approach.

</details>


### [164] [Memory as a Markov Matrix: Sample Efficient Knowledge Expansion via Token-to-Dictionary Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04308)
*Kaustubh Pethkar,Ziyang Xiong,Zuofeng Shang,Yingcong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于马尔可夫过程的框架来持续更新大语言模型，通过将token生成建模为马尔可夫链，将新知识注入转化为扩展状态空间问题，从而实现零遗忘的增量学习。理论上证明了新token学习的样本复杂度与映射字典大小成线性关系，实践上通过最小参数更新的embedding-tuning算法验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题。现有基于参数更新的方法存在两个根本局限：1）新知识增量增大时遗忘不可避免；2）模型更新不可逆。本文质疑对大规模权重更新的必要性，转而探索更轻量的知识注入方式。

Method: 将自回归语言生成建模为token上的马尔可夫过程，模型记忆由马尔可夫转移矩阵表示。新知识/新token的融入对应状态空间扩展，保持现有转移矩阵不变即保证原有知识保留。提出基于token-to-dictionary映射策略的embedding-tuning算法，仅更新少量embedding参数。

Result: 理论上证明了新token学习的样本复杂度上界与映射到的已有token数量成线性关系。实验验证了该方法能实现零遗忘，同时参数更新量显著低于全参数微调方法。

Conclusion: 本文从理论和实验两方面验证了马尔可夫框架下增量学习的可行性，表明对于获取少量新知识，大规模权重更新并非必需。embedding-tuning作为一种最小参数更新策略，能有效避免灾难性遗忘。

Abstract: Continual incorporation of new knowledge is essential for the long-term evolution of large language models (LLMs). Existing approaches typically rely on parameter-update algorithms to mitigate catastrophic forgetting, yet they suffer from fundamental limitations: 1) forgetting is unavoidable as the amount of newly injected knowledge grows; and 2) model updates are often irreversible. As modern LLMs become increasingly expressive, it is natural to question whether large-scale weight updates are necessary for acquiring a small amount of new knowledge. In this work, we propose a principled framework that models autoregressive language generation as a Markov process over tokens, where model memory is represented by a Markov transition matrix. Under this formulation, incorporating new knowledge/tokens corresponds to extending the state space, and preserving existing transitions guarantees retention of previously learned knowledge. We then prove a sample complexity bound for incorporating new tokens via a token-to-dictionary mapping strategy. In particular, for learning the transition behavior of each new token, the required number of samples scales linearly with the number of existing tokens it is mapped to. To realize this mapping, we propose an embedding-tuning algorithm that requires minimal parameter updates and induces zero forgetting. Experimental results further demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and validate our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [165] [DeFed-GMM-DaDiL: A Decentralized Federated Framework for Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04324)
*Rebecca Clain,Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma,Fred Ngole Mboula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DeFed-GMM-DaDiL，一种去中心化的联邦学习方法，用于多源域适应。各客户端使用高斯混合模型(GMM)建模数据，通过标记的Wasserstein重心来近似共享的GMM原子，实现隐私保护的跨域知识迁移。实验表明该方法在目标域存在类别缺失时仍能保持稳定的表示学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多源域适应在去中心化场景下面临异构数据整合和隐私保护的双重挑战。传统联邦学习方法依赖中心服务器，存在单点故障和隐私泄露风险。现有去中心化方法难以有效处理多个相关但异构的源域到同一目标域的知识迁移问题。

Method: DeFed-GMM-DaDiL将GMM-DaDiL框架扩展为完全去中心化的联邦架构。各客户端独立使用高斯混合模型对其本地数据集建模，通过共享的可学习GMM原子(atom)的标记Wasserstein重心进行联合近似，实现分布式协作学习。

Result: 实验结果表明：(1)跨客户端的共享表示具有稳定性和一致性；(2)能有效重构目标域中缺失的类别；(3)在多源域适应基准数据集上达到具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: DeFed-GMM-DaDiL成功实现了无中心服务器的隐私保护型多源域适应，在类别缺失场景下展现出鲁棒的表示学习能力，为去中心化联邦学习在领域自适应任务中的应用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Decentralized multi-source domain adaptation seeks to transfer knowledge from multiple heterogeneous and related source domains to an unlabeled target domain in a decentralized setting. We address this challenge through a fully decentralized federated approach, DeFed-GMM-DaDiL, an extension of the GMM-Dataset Dictionary Learning (DaDiL) framework. Each client models its dataset as a Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM), and the federation jointly approximates them via labeled Wasserstein barycenters of shared, learnable GMM atoms. This design enables adaptation without a central server while preserving clients' privacy. We empirically study the stability of the learned representations in scenarios where the target domain has missing classes. Empirical results demonstrate that DeFed-GMM-DaDiL maintains stable and consistent shared representations across clients, effectively reconstructs missing classes, and achieves competitive performance on multi-source domain adaptation benchmarks.

</details>


### [166] [Learning-based Statistical Refinement for Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04332)
*Rihuan Ke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于学习的统计精炼方法，用于改进已有去噪器的结果。该方法无需知道精确噪声分布、干净图像或标定数据，仅利用噪声数据本身的统计信息，通过贝叶斯框架评估去噪结果与噪声统计的一致性，从而优化去噪效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去噪方法依赖精确的图像与噪声建模，实际中因模型不完美、噪声假设不可靠或数据质量低，去噪结果往往不是最优的。特别是在缺乏干净图像样本且未知噪声分布的情况下，去噪结果难以与噪声统计特性对齐。

Method: 基于贝叶斯框架，构造含噪数据中的辅助信号来评估去噪结果的一致性，在仅知道噪声为像素级独立（以干净信号为条件）的前提下，利用噪声数据的统计信息进行优化。

Result: 方法能够增强去噪结果的统计噪声一致性，在无需标定数据的情况下有效提升去噪质量。

Conclusion: 通过利用噪声数据的统计信息，无需精确的噪声分布或干净图像即可提升去噪质量，实现去噪结果与噪声统计的一致性增强。

Abstract: This work proposes a learning-based statistical refinement method for improving the denoising results of a given denoiser without knowing the precise noise distribution or accessing clean images or calibration data. While there are many existing successful denoising approaches for handling different kinds of noise, they typically require accurate modelling of the images and the noise (implicitly or explicitly), and hence the denoising results can be suboptimal due to different practical factors such as imperfect models, unreliable noise assumptions, or low quality data. In particular, when clean image samples are not available and there is a lack of knowledge of the underlying noise distribution, which is the case in various practical situations, the results may not well align with the noise statistics. The unawareness of the useful statistical information leads to suboptimal results. This work aims to make the best use of the statistical information to improve the consistency between the given denoising results and the noise statistics, under the assumption that the noise is conditionally pixel-wise independent given the clean signal. A method, based on a Bayesian formulation of an auxiliary signal in the noisy data, is proposed for evaluating the consistency of the denoising results, without precise information on noise distribution. By leveraging the statistical information from noisy data, the method enhances the statistical noise consistency and improves denoising quality.

</details>


### [167] [Budgeted LoRA: Distillation as Structured Compute Allocation for Efficient Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04341)
*Mohammed Sabry,Anya Belz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Budgeted LoRA是一种用于大语言模型蒸馏的框架，通过结构化计算分配来同时压缩训练成本和推理成本。该方法在模块级别分配密集和低秩计算预算，在中等预算下达到1.74x加速（与标准LoRA困惑度相当），在激进预算下达到4.05x加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数高效蒸馏方法（如LoRA）虽然降低了微调成本，但密集 backbone 保持不变，因此无法提供推理阶段的实际加速。需要在训练成本和推理效率之间取得真正的平衡。

Method: 提出Budgeted LoRA，将模型压缩建模为结构化计算分配问题。核心机制包括：(i) 模块级密集保留系数，(ii) 自适应低秩分配，(iii) 后训练压缩（选择性移除、近似或保留密集组件）。通过单一预算调节器控制学生模型家族。

Result: 中等预算下：困惑度与标准LoRA相当，模块级加速1.74倍；激进预算下：加速4.05倍，困惑度略有下降；在函数风格上下文学习探测上保持更高准确率。

Conclusion: 在计算受限蒸馏场景下，关键不在于匹配困惑度或移除更多参数，而在于控制密集计算如何转移到低秩路径。Budgeted LoRA证明了通过结构化计算分配可实现训练与推理成本的双重压缩。

Abstract: We study distillation for large language models under explicit compute constraints, with the goal of producing student models that are not only cheaper to train, but structurally efficient at inference time. While prior approaches to parameter-efficient distillation, such as LoRA, reduce adaptation cost, they leave the dense backbone unchanged and therefore fail to deliver meaningful inference savings. We propose Budgeted LoRA, a distillation framework that treats model compression as a structured compute allocation problem. Instead of using a fixed student architecture, we introduce a global compute budget that sets the final target fraction of dense computation retained. Under this constraint, the model redistributes capacity across dense and low-rank pathways via (i) module-level dense retention coefficients, (ii) adaptive low-rank allocation, and (iii) post-training compression that selectively removes, approximates, or preserves dense components. This formulation yields a family of students controlled by a single budget dial. Empirically, Budgeted LoRA matches standard LoRA perplexity at a moderate budget with a 1.74x compressed-module speedup; at an aggressive budget it achieves a 4.05x speedup with moderate perplexity degradation, and it preserves higher accuracy on function-style in-context learning probes. These results suggest that, under compute-constrained distillation, retaining behavior is less about matching perplexity or removing more parameters than it is about controlling how dense computation is transferred to low-rank pathways.

</details>


### [168] [Structural Equivalence and Learning Dynamics in Delayed MARL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04345)
*Jules Sintes,Ana Bušić,Jiamin Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文形式化证明了合作部分可观测多智能体系统中观测延迟(OD)与动作延迟(AD)的等价性，指出两者生成相同的联合策略集和状态-动作-观测轨迹分布，在Dec-POMDPs中具有相同的最优解。然而，通过数值实验发现，尽管解空间结构同构，实际学习动态存在本质差异：非转移独立条件下等价性失效，TD算法的因果信用分配误差导致不同学习行为，并成功实现了从OD到AD的零样本策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究仅在无限时域单智能体场景下讨论OD与AD的等价性，缺乏对任意时域部分可观测合作多智能体问题的形式化证明，同时未探索该等价性在学习动态层面的实际影响。

Method: 1) 利用observation-action历史形式化建立OD与AD的等价性证明框架；2) 在TI-MDPs中推导可处理的最小局部增强状态；3) 设计数值实验对比两种延迟机制下的学习动态；4) 验证零样本策略迁移的可行性。

Result: 1) 形式化证明OD与AD在合作多智能体系统中生成相同 admissible joint-policy 集，状态-动作-观测轨迹分布相同；2) TI-MDPs中observation-action历史可压缩为最小局部增强状态；3) 数值实验揭示：(a) 非转移独立条件下等价性失效，(b) TD算法的操作约束和因果信用分配误差导致不同学习行为，(c) 成功实现OD到AD的零样本策略迁移。

Conclusion: 本文将单智能体无限时域结果推广至任意时域分散式合作多智能体问题，证明了混合延迟配置可简化为纯OD系统。尽管最优解空间结构同构，实际学习动态存在本质差异，零样本策略迁移的可行性为复杂延迟系统提供了统一高效解决方案的路径。

Abstract: We formally establish the equivalence between Observation Delay (OD) and Action Delay (AD) in cooperative partially observable multi-agent systems using observation-action histories. We show that both systems generate identical admissible joint-policy sets, and their induced state-action-observation trajectories are identical in distribution, leading to identical optimal solutions in Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (Dec-POMDPs). This formally generalizes existing infinite-horizon single-agent results to any-horizon partially observable cooperative multi-agent problems with decentralized policy execution, and allows any mixed-delay configuration to be reduced to a pure OD system. We further prove that in Transition-Independent MDPs (TI-MDPs), the observation-action history reduces to a tractable minimal local augmented state.
  However, we show through numerical experiments that although the optimal solution spaces are structurally isomorphic, the practical learning dynamics are fundamentally different. First, using the minimal local augmented state, the equivalence no longer holds when transitions are not independent. Second, operational constraints and causal credit-assignment errors in Temporal Difference (TD) algorithms induce different learning behaviors across regimes. Finally, leveraging this structural equivalence to bypass these learning challenges, we demonstrate successful multi-agent zero-shot policy transfer from OD to AD, paving the way for unified, efficient solution methods in complex delayed systems.

</details>


### [169] [Probing Structural Mathematical Reasoning in Language Models with Algebraic Trapdoors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04352)
*Igor Rivin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 一个用于评估语言模型结构化数学推理能力的基准测试套件，基于SL(3, Z)中的子群构造问题，通过密码学风格的验证者-证明者不对称性设计，可区分具备内化代数先验知识的模型与依赖通用计算的模型，并揭示模型行为的四种分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前评估方法无法区分具备深层代数先验知识的语言模型与依赖通用计算的模型，特别是在处理开放可判定性边界问题时。

Method: 构建基于SL(3, Z)子群构造问题的基准测试，每个实例提供整数矩阵生成子群，要求推理者（缺乏构造时间信息）通过Aschbacher分类分析或Membership查询推导出算术不变量（指数、素数满射性或成员资格）。

Result: 在指数变体任务中，某一模型花费152分钟推理，识别出核成员资格问题为瓶颈，尝试构造性验证，最终以"DON'T KNOW"作答而非提交计算出的余核候选值——展示了在基准测试所探测的开放可判定性边界上的校准元认知能力。

Conclusion: 基准测试暴露了模型行为的四向分类（正确提交、错误提交、正确弃权、错误弃权），而传统答案评分会将这些不同行为混为一谈。

Abstract: We introduce a benchmark suite for evaluating structural mathematical reasoning in language models, built on subgroup-construction problems in SL(3, Z) with cryptographic-style verifier-prover asymmetry. Each instance presents a finitely generated subgroup as a list of integer matrices and asks for an arithmetic invariant -- index, surjection-at-prime, or membership -- that the construction-time information (N, K) pins down in O(1) closed form, but that the solver, lacking that information, must derive by either Aschbacher-classification analysis or by a membership query in SL(3, Z) of unknown decidability. The benchmark therefore distinguishes models with internalized algebraic priors (Aschbacher classes, McLaughlin's theorem, Property (T), the congruence subgroup property) from models that rely on general-purpose computation. We report empirical results across five representative reasoning traces from two state-of-the-art models. The headline result: on the index variant, one model spent 152 minutes of reasoning, explicitly identified the kernel-side membership question as the bottleneck, attempted constructive verification, and abstained with "DON'T KNOW" rather than commit to its computed cokernel candidate -- demonstrating calibrated meta-cognition on the open-decidability boundary that the benchmark was designed to probe. We argue that the benchmark exposes a four-way classification of model behavior (commit-correct, commit-wrong, abstain-correct, abstain-wrong) that standard answer-key scoring conflates.

</details>


### [170] [Mitigating Label Shift in Tabular In-Context Learning via Test-Time Posterior Adjustment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04363)
*Seunghan Lee,Jaehoon Lee,Jun Seo,Sungdong Yoo,Minjae Kim,Tae Yoon Lim,Dongwan Kang,Hwanil Choi,SoonYoung Lee,Wonbin Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN是一种用于表格数据的Foundation Model，但在标签偏移场景下容易过拟合多数类。本文提出DistPFN，通过测试时调整预测概率（降低训练先验影响、增强后验贡献）来解决这一问题，并进一步提出DistPFN-T（引入温度缩放）。在250+数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN是一种新兴的表格数据基础模型，通过在合成数据上的上下文学习实现强性能。然而，研究发现TabPFN存在标签偏移脆弱性问题——在训练数据中往往过度拟合多数类，导致分布偏移场景下性能严重下降。

Method: 本文提出DistPFN，这是首个专为表格基础模型设计的测试时后验调整方法。核心思路是：1）重新调整预测的类别概率；2）降低训练先验（即上下文类别分布）的影响权重；3）强调模型预测后验的贡献。方法无需架构修改或额外训练。同时提出DistPFN-T，引入温度缩放机制，根据先验与后验之间的差异自适应控制调整强度。

Result: 在超过250个OpenML数据集上评估，结果表明：DistPFN/DistPFN-T在多种TabPFN变体模型的标签偏移分类任务中均取得实质性改进，同时在无标签偏移的标准设置下保持原有性能。

Conclusion: 通过测试时的概率调整策略，DistPFN系列方法有效解决了TabPFN的标签偏移脆弱性问题，无需改变模型架构或进行额外训练，具有良好的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: TabPFN has recently gained attention as a foundation model for tabular datasets, achieving strong performance by leveraging in-context learning on synthetic data. However, we find that TabPFN is vulnerable to label shift, often overfitting to the majority class in the training dataset. To address this limitation, we propose DistPFN, the first test-time posterior adjustment method designed for tabular foundation models. DistPFN rescales predicted class probabilities by downweighting the influence of the training prior (i.e., the class distribution of the context) and emphasizing the contribution of the model's predicted posterior, without architectural modification or additional training. We further introduce DistPFN-T, which incorporates temperature scaling to adaptively control the adjustment strength based on the discrepancy between prior and posterior. We evaluate our methods on over 250 OpenML datasets, demonstrating substantial improvements for various TabPFN-based models in classification tasks under label shift, while maintaining strong performance in standard settings without label shift. Code is available at this repository: https://github.com/seunghan96/DistPFN.

</details>


### [171] [Online Nonstochastic Prediction: Logarithmic Regret via Predictive Online Least Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04364)
*Chih-Fan Pai,Yang Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了边际稳定、部分观测线性系统在非随机扰动下的在线预测问题。通过设计基于Luenberger预测器的定制提示来稳定无约束在线最小二乘学习过程，在有模型和无模型两种情况下均实现了对数遗憾，突破了传统方法依赖有界域/梯度的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线学习方法依赖有界域/梯度假设，而边际稳定系统在非随机扰动下可能产生无界轨迹，导致传统保证失效。需要突破有界性假设的限制，为边际稳定系统设计能处理无界轨迹的在线预测方法。

Method: 引入了无约束在线最小二乘法，通过从任意稳定化Luenberger预测器构造的定制预测提示来稳定学习过程。利用模型知识证明提示残差一致有界，并采用尾部界追踪技术处理系统轨道的潜在无界增长，实现对数遗憾保证。对于无模型预测，提出了对称系统的通用提示设计。

Result: 对于有模型情况，从任何稳定化Luenberger预测器构造的提示可确保提示残差一致有界，实现对数遗憾；对于无模型情况，对称系统存在简单通用提示可维持对数遗憾，无需模型知识即可实现实例级最优的自适应在线预测器。

Conclusion: 该方法通过稳定性分析和尾部界的追踪技术，证明了在边界增长的情况下能够达到对数遗憾，实现了与经典固定增益观测器相比的实例级最优自适应预测器，为非随机扰动下的部分观测线性系统提供了有效的在线预测理论框架。

Abstract: We study online prediction for marginally stable, partially observed linear dynamical systems under nonstochastic disturbances. Our objective is to minimize the cumulative squared prediction loss and compete with the best-in-hindsight Luenberger predictor. Standard online learning methods typically rely on bounded domains/gradients, and thus their guarantees may fail to deal with potentially unbounded trajectories in marginally stable systems. In this paper, we introduce an unconstrained online least squares method that stabilizes the learning process via tailored predictive hints. With model knowledge, we prove that hints constructed from any stabilizing Luenberger predictor render the hint residuals uniformly bounded, achieving logarithmic regret despite unbounded trajectory growth. We also discuss model-free prediction and introduce a simple universal hint for symmetric systems, under which logarithmic regret is maintained without model knowledge. Our results provide an adaptive, instance-wise optimal online predictor compared to classical fixed-gain observers under nonstochastic disturbances.

</details>


### [172] [Extending Differential Temporal Difference Methods for Episodic Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04368)
*Kris De Asis,Mohamed Elsayed,Jiamin He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇论文将差分时序差分（TD）方法扩展到情节性（episodic）强化学习问题。通过泛化差分TD并结合终止感知的奖励中心化，作者证明了该方法保持策略排序并与线性TD等价，从而继承其理论保证。实验表明奖励中心化可提升情节问题中的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Standard differential TD methods with reward centering can alter optimal policies in episodic problems, limiting applicability. Need to extend differential TD's benefits (bounded returns, offset removal) to episodic settings.

Method: Generalized differential TD via reward centering with termination-aware offset; established equivalence to linear TD for theoretical guarantees; extended multiple streaming RL algorithms to differential counterparts.

Result: The generalized differential TD maintains policy ordering in the presence of termination, inherits linear TD's theoretical guarantees, and empirically improves sample efficiency across various base algorithms and environments.

Conclusion: The proposed generalization of differential TD successfully extends value-based RL to episodic problems while preserving policy ordering guarantees, with empirical validation showing improved sample efficiency.

Abstract: Differential temporal difference (TD) methods are value-based reinforcement learning algorithms that have been proposed for infinite-horizon problems. They rely on reward centering, where each reward is centered by the average reward. This keeps the return bounded and removes a value function's state-independent offset. However, reward centering can alter the optimal policy in episodic problems, limiting its applicability. Motivated by recent works that emphasize the role of normalization in streaming deep reinforcement learning, we study reward centering in episodic problems and propose a generalization of differential TD. We prove that this generalization maintains the ordering of policies in the presence of termination, and thus extends differential TD to episodic problems. We show equivalence with a form of linear TD, thereby inheriting theoretical guarantees that have been shown for those algorithms. We then extend several streaming reinforcement learning algorithms to their differential counterparts. Across a range of base algorithms and environments, we empirically validate that reward centering can improve sample efficiency in episodic problems.

</details>


### [173] [$p$-adic Manifold Learning and Benchmark Tasks from Impartial Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04374)
*Tomoki Mihara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文引入p进值流形学习的概念，提出了一种算法来解决问题，并提出基于 impartial games 的基准测试任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To extend manifold learning techniques to p-adic number spaces and establish benchmark tasks from impartial games for evaluation.

Method: The paper proposes a specific algorithm to solve the p-adic manifold learning problem.

Result: Algorithm and benchmark tasks are proposed for p-adic manifold learning.

Conclusion: The paper introduces a new research direction combining p-adic manifold learning with impartial games, proposing both an algorithm and benchmark tasks for evaluation.

Abstract: We introduce $p$-adic manifold learning, propose an algorithm to solve it, and propose benchmark tasks from impartial games.

</details>


### [174] [Critical Windows of Complexity Control: When Transformers Decide to Reason or Memorize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04396)
*Sarwan Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Transformer模型在组合泛化任务中，其"记忆vs推理"的命运在训练过程中存在一个狭窄且可识别关键窗口期（critical window）被决定。仅在一个25%训练周期的窗口内应用权重衰减，就能达到与全训练周期使用权重衰减相当的OOD准确率（0.93 vs 0.91）。关键窗口的边界极其锐利：仅偏移100步就可能导致OOD从随机水平（0.15）跃升至推理水平（0.61）。该窗口位置随初始化规模系统性变化，但小初始化反而会收缩推理解的吸引域，与"小初始化更好"的普遍认知相悖。更重要的是，这种关键窗口现象具有任务特异性——在模算术grokking任务中未观察到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将复杂度控制视为单一的静态超参数选择，未能回答"在训练过程中的哪个阶段"复杂度控制实际上起着决定性作用。本文旨在揭示Transformer在组合泛化任务中，记忆与推理的命运是在何时（when）被确定的。

Method: 在受控的组合任务上设计系统化实验：(i) 全训练周期权重衰减对比单窗口（25%训练期）权重衰减；(ii) 固定总正则化预算，改变权重衰减的时间位置分布；(iii) 探索窗口边界的锐度（100步级别的偏移）；(iv) 研究初始化规模与关键窗口位置的交互关系；(v) 在模算术grokking任务上进行对比实验以验证任务特异性。

Result: 发现Transformer的"记忆-推理"命运在训练中存在一个尖锐的关键窗口期：(i) 单窗口权重衰减与全训练周期效果相当（0.93 vs 0.91 OOD准确率）；(ii) 总正则化预算固定时，中期放置比早期放置的OOD准确率高5-9倍；(iii) 窗口边界极其锐利：仅100步的偏移就导致OOD从0.15跃升至0.61；(iv) 关键窗口位置随初始化规模系统性变化，但小初始化反而缩小了推理解的吸引域，与"小初始化更好"的传统认知相悖；(v) 该现象具有任务特异性——模算术grokking任务中，调优得当的恒定权重衰减即可匹配调度权重衰减的效果。

Conclusion: Transformer的组合泛化能力由训练过程中的特定关键窗口决定，而非简单的静态超参数设置。该窗口的位置和宽度受初始化规模影响，且这种现象具有任务特异性。这些发现对理解深度学习中的泛化机制和设计更有效的训练策略具有重要意义。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that Transformers' compositional generalization is governed by \emph{complexity control}, initialization scale and weight decay, which steers training toward low-complexity reasoning solutions rather than high-complexity memorization. Existing analyses, however, treat complexity control as a single static hyperparameter choice, leaving open \emph{when} during training this control is actually decisive. We show that the memorization-versus-reasoning fate of a Transformer is determined within a sharp, identifiable window of training. On a controlled compositional task we find that (i)~weight decay applied for a single 25\%-of-training window matches full-training weight decay in out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy ($0.93$ vs $0.91$); (ii)~holding total regularization budget constant, placing it in the middle of training yields $5{-}9\times$ higher OOD accuracy than placing it early; (iii)~the boundary of the critical window is remarkably sharp, window onset shifted by as little as $100$ optimization steps causes mean OOD to jump from chance ($0.15$) to reasoning-regime ($0.61$); (iv)~the window's position depends systematically on initialization scale, but the basin of attraction for reasoning solutions \emph{shrinks} at small initialization, contradicting the prevailing recommendation that smaller initialization is uniformly better. We further show that the critical-window phenomenon is task-specific: it does not appear on grokking with modular arithmetic, where properly tuned constant weight decay matches scheduled weight decay.

</details>


### [175] [Beyond Rigid Geometries: The Spline-Pullback Metric for Universal Diffeomorphic SPD Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04406)
*Tushar Das,Subrata Dutta,Sarmistha Neogy,Koushlendra Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Spline-Pullback Metric (SPM)的新方法，通过参数化全局微分同胚（使用秩不变、单调约束的B样条）从静态度量选择转向通用几何逼近。SPM作为严格递增C¹微分同胚的通用近似器，理论上映射现有回拉度量，同时实现局部非线性谱建模，提供全局双射回拉几何避免秩交换不连续性和梯度不稳定性。在3个数据集上使用Linear Probes、SPDNet和深度黎曼ResNet达到最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional SPD matrix integration in deep learning relies on fixed Riemannian metrics (analogous to hand-crafted features), imposing rigid geometries that limit network expressivity and adaptability. Existing parameterized approaches often violate axioms of matrix functions through unconstrained operations, causing spatial folding, loss of global surjectivity, and gradient collapse.

Method: Parameterizes the global diffeomorphism via a rank-invariant, monotonically constrained B-spline, enabling SPM to act as a dense universal approximator for strictly increasing C¹ diffeomorphisms. Two instantiations are proposed: Spectral-SPM and Cholesky-SPM.

Result: Empirically validated with state-of-the-art performance across 3 datasets using Linear Probes, SPDNets, and deep Riemannian ResNets architectures.

Conclusion: SPM provides a globally bijective pullback geometry with theoretical guarantees of bijectivity, avoiding rank-swapping discontinuities and gradient instabilities inherent in prior approaches.

Abstract: The integration of Symmetric Positive Definite (SPD) matrices into deep learning has historically relied on fixed algebraic Riemannian metrics. Analogous to hand-crafted features in classical machine learning, these static formulations impose rigid geometries limiting network expressivity and adaptability. Recent attempts to parameterize these geometries often violate the axioms of primary matrix functions through unconstrained powers or rank-dependent scaling, inviting spatial folding, loss of global surjectivity, and gradient collapse at spectral singularities. In this paper, we introduce the Spline-Pullback Metric (SPM), instantiated as Spectral-SPM and Cholesky-SPM, marking a paradigm shift from static metric selection to universal geometric approximation. By parameterizing the global diffeomorphism via a rank-invariant, monotonically constrained B-spline, SPM acts as a dense universal approximator for strictly increasing $C^1$ diffeomorphisms and theoretically subsumes existing pullback metrics while enabling localized non-linear spectral modelling. Topologically, SPM provides a globally bijective pullback geometry precluding rank-swapping discontinuities and gradient instabilities. Empirically, SPM achieves a state-of-the-art performance across 3 datasets utilizing Linear Probes, SPDNets, and deep Riemannian ResNets.

</details>


### [176] [Counterfactual identifiability beyond global monotonicity: non-monotone triangular structural causal models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04413)
*Pengcheng Tan,Jiang Chen,Dehui Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在放松全局单调性假设后，结构因果模型仍能实现反事实识别的条件。提出非单调三角结构因果模型（NM-TM-SCM），通过机制级可逆性和上下文无关逆传输替代全局单调性，证明这与外生同构等价并蕴含完全反事实可识别性。实验验证表明该框架在非单调机制下具有系统性优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构因果模型的可识别性结果大多依赖全局单调性假设，但在具身交互中，相同的外生扰动在不同接触情境下可能引发相反响应，导致全局单调性假设失效。需要探索更宽松的识别条件。

Method: 提出NM-TM-SCM框架，保留三角递归结构，用机制级可逆性和上下文无关逆传输替换全局单调性假设。通过CausalInverter实现，包含三角可逆层、定向门和传输稳定性正则化。

Result: 理论证明机制级可逆性与上下文无关逆传输等价于外生同构，并蕴含完全反事实可识别性；局部可逆性单独不足。实验表明：合成非单调机制上非单调性增强时反事实收益系统增加；MuJoCo Door任务达到完美事件级反事实恢复，连续角度误差低于Transformer基线；MuJoCo Push任务上低数据预测器仍具竞争力，符合偏差-方差权衡。

Conclusion: 识别出介于全局单调三角模型与无约束黑盒世界模型之间的更广泛可识别机制，为具身交互中的反事实推理提供了理论基础和有效方法。

Abstract: Structural causal models provide a unified semantics for interventions and counterfactuals, but most identifiability results rely on restrictive assumptions like global monotonicity, which are often violated in embodied interaction, where the same exogenous perturbation can induce opposite responses under different contact contexts. We ask what structure still suffices once global monotonicity is dropped. We introduce non-monotone triangular structural causal models (NM-TM-SCM), which retain triangular recursion but replace global monotonicity with mechanism-wise invertibility and context-independent inverse transport. We prove that these conditions are equivalent to exogenous isomorphism and imply complete counterfactual identifiability, and we give a counterexample showing that local invertibility alone is insufficient. We instantiate the theory in CausalInverter, with triangular invertible layers, orientation gates, and transport-stability regularization. On synthetic non-monotonic mechanisms, the structural bias yields systematic counterfactual gains as non-monotonicity increases. On MuJoCo Door, our model achieves perfect event-level counterfactual recovery, lowers continuous angle error relative to a Transformer baseline, and delivers substantially more stable recovery than Transformer and conditional-flow predictors. On MuJoCo Push, where non-monotonicity is weaker, the same low-data predictors remain competitive or better, consistent with a bias-variance boundary. These results identify a broader identifiable regime between globally monotone triangular models and unconstrained black-box world models.

</details>


### [177] [FAAST: Forward-Only Associative Learning via Closed-Form Fast Weights for Test-Time Supervised Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04651)
*Guangsheng Bao,Hongbo Zhang,Han Cui,Yanbin Zhao,Yue Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FAAST是一种前向-only的联想适应方法，通过单次解析编译将标注样本转化为快速权重，在保持性能的同时将适应时间减少90%以上，内存占用降低95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练模型适应面临两难权衡：反向传播训练成本高，记忆/上下文学习推理开销大。需要一种高效且资源友好的适应方法。

Method: FAAST（Forward-only Associative Adaptation）采用解析方法将标注样本在单次前向传播中编译为快速权重，无需记忆或上下文依赖，实现恒定时间推理。

Result: 在图像分类和语言建模基准测试中，FAAST匹配或超越反向传播适应方法，适应性时间减少超过90%；相比记忆/上下文学习方法，内存节省达95%。

Conclusion: FAAST为监督任务适应提供了高效、可扩展的解决方案，尤其适用于资源受限场景。

Abstract: Adapting pretrained models typically involves a trade-off between the high training costs of backpropagation and the heavy inference overhead of memory-based or in-context learning. We propose FAAST, a forward-only associative adaptation method that analytically compiles labeled examples into fast weights in a single pass. By eliminating memory or context dependence, FAAST achieves constant-time inference and decouples task adaptation from pretrained representation. Across image classification and language modeling benchmarks, FAAST matches or exceeds backprop-based adaptation while reducing adaptation time by over 90\% and is competitive to memory/context-based adaptation while saving memory usage by up to 95\%. These results demonstrate FAAST as a highly efficient, scalable solution for supervised task adaptation, particularly for resource-constrained models. We release the code and models at https://github.com/baoguangsheng/faast.

</details>


### [178] [Demystifying Manifold Constraints in LLM Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04418)
*Kang An,Jiaxiang Li,Donald Goldfarb,Shiqian Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The paper demystifies the role of explicit manifold constraints in LLM pre-training by introducing MACRO, demonstrating that constraints on weights can replace heuristic stabilization techniques (normalization, weight decay) by independently controlling activation scales and rotational equilibrium, with provable convergence guarantees.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current LLM pre-training relies on heuristic stabilization techniques (explicit normalization layers, weight decay) whose underlying mechanisms are not well understood, despite the potential of constrained optimization approaches.

Method: Introduces MACRO (Msign-Aligned Constrained Riemannian Optimizer), a single-loop optimization framework that applies manifold constraints directly to weights, separating their effect from RMS normalization and decoupled weight decay mechanisms.

Result: Manifold constraints independently bound forward activation scales and enforce stable rotational equilibrium, effectively subsuming the roles of heuristic stabilization mechanisms while achieving competitive performance on large-scale LLM architectures.

Conclusion: MACRO provides a theoretically grounded alternative to heuristic stabilization techniques in LLM pre-training, with manifold constraints serving as a unified mechanism that subsumes normalization layers and weight decay, while maintaining convergence guarantees.

Abstract: The empirical success of large language model (LLM) pre-training relies heavily on heuristic stabilization techniques, such as explicit normalization layers and weight decay. While recent constrained optimization approaches that explicitly restrict weights may improve numerical stability and performance, the mechanism and motivation for adding constraints still remain elusive. This paper systematically demystifies the role of explicit manifold constraints in LLM pre-training. By introducing the Msign-Aligned Constrained Riemannian Optimizer (MACRO)-a provably convergent, single-loop optimization framework-our study disentangles weight regularization heuristics from interacting mechanisms like RMS normalization and decoupled weight decay. Theoretical analyses and comprehensive empirical evaluations reveal that manifold constraints independently bound forward activation scales and enforce stable rotational equilibrium, thereby subsuming the roles of these heuristic mechanisms. Evaluations on large-scale LLM architectures demonstrate that MACRO achieves highly competitive performance while rigorously preserving the theoretical guarantees of exact Riemannian optimization.

</details>


### [179] [Uncertainty-Aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization for Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04874)
*Huatian Zhang,Zhendong Mao,Lei Zhang,Yongdong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UE-DPO通过引入token级认知不确定性来解决多模态大语言模型中的幻觉问题，克服了现有DPO方法中自评敏感度信号带来的自我参照偏差，实现了更精细的视觉保真度对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DPO方法依赖模型自评的视觉敏感度信号来分配训练权重，但这种方法存在自我参照偏差：强化已学好的视觉线索，忽视难以感知但关键细节，限制了更深入的校准效果。

Method: 1) 从模型未能将token预测扎根于给定图像的失败中量化不确定性；2) 基于不确定性感知探索强度，对首选样本中视觉缺陷token施加更多学习压力；3) 减轻对非首选样本中有益知识的过度惩罚。

Result: 实验表明该方法在多个基准上相比现有方法有显著提升，具有良好的有效性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提供了方法的理论论证，证明通过不确定性引导的探索性学习可以有效识别认知缺陷并进行自我纠正，为多模态对齐提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) has proven to be an effective solution for mitigating hallucination in Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) by learning from preference pairs. One of its key challenges lies in how to transfer the sequence-level preference into fine-grained supervision on visual fidelity. To safeguard vision-related tokens that are prone to hallucination, existing methods typically allocate training emphasis according to the model's self-assessed visual sensitivity signals. However, such sensitivity, estimated by a model still under training, introduces self-referential bias: reinforcing already well-learned visual cues while neglecting hard-to-perceive but critical details, thereby limiting deeper alignment. In this work, we propose an Uncertainty-aware Exploratory Direct Preference Optimization (UE-DPO) method for MLLMs, which enables the model to uncover its cognitive deficiencies and actively explore for self-correction, guided by token-level epistemic uncertainty. Specifically, we first quantify the uncertainty from the model's failure to ground token predictions in the given image. Then, based on an uncertainty-aware exploration intensity, we encourage more learning pressure on visually deficient tokens in preferred samples, and alleviate the over-penalization of beneficial knowledge in dispreferred samples. Further, we provide a theoretical justification for our method, and extensive experiments demonstrate its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>


### [180] [Self-Attention as Transport: Limits of Symmetric Spectral Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04893)
*Dominik Dahlem,Diego Maniloff,Mac Misiura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明现有谱方法在分析注意力操作符时存在结构性方向盲点，并通过引入非对称系数G作为方向控制参数和容量参数φ，构建了可证伪的双轴诊断框架，为大语言模型幻觉的可预测失败模式提供了理论解释和实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large language models hallucinate in predictable ways through attention routing failures (over-concentration or excessive diffusion). Understanding whether spectral methods can diagnose such failures requires clarifying whether these methods can detect information-flow direction. Current spectral analysis of the symmetric component of attention operators may be fundamentally limited in this regard.

Method: Theoretical analysis: (1) Proved that every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic of the symmetric component of degree-normalized attention operator is orientation-blind; (2) Established asymmetry coefficient G as the unique directional control parameter via quantitative converse; (3) Derived closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape for canonical causal architectures; (4) Analyzed uniform causal attention (floor φ ≥ 1/5, worst cut at t*/n ≈ 0.32) and window attention (pierces floor as O(w/n)). Experimental validation: length-controlled evaluation on models up to 8B parameters.

Result: 证明转置不变的光谱诊断方法在本质上无法区分操作符与其转置，因此无法检测信息流方向；不对称系数G是方向的唯一控制参数。统一因果注意力的传输容量存在与n无关的下界φ≥1/5，最差切割点在t*/n≈0.32；窗口注意力作为O(w/n)突破该下界。不同失败模式在形状上有本质区别，而非仅是数值差异。两轴诊断（φ表征容量，G表征方向）能够预测瓶颈主导和弥散主导基准测试呈现相反极性。长度控制评估下，传输特征在8B参数模型上保持0.62-0.84的LC-AUROC可解释信号。
